### [1] [MergirafSemi: A Language-Agnostic Semistructured Merge Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11345)
*Pedro Lopes,Paulo Borba,Paola Accioly,Guilherme Cavalcanti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出MergirafSemi，一种语言无关的半结构化合并工具，利用轻量级具体语法树在特定程序区域内引导基于行的合并决策，在减少虚假冲突与保持合并精度之间取得更优平衡，且运行开销显著低于全结构化或语言特定的同类工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者经常面临合并冲突问题，现有工具存在两难：无结构（基于行）工具会产生大量虚假冲突或漏报真实冲突；全结构化工具计算开销高，语言特定工具开发维护成本大。需要一种能兼顾结构信息利用率和跨语言通用性及效率的折中方案。

Method: MergirafSemi采用语言无关的半结构化合并策略：利用轻量级具体语法树（Concrete Syntax Trees）捕获程序的结构特征以辅助合并决策，但不进行完整结构化建模；在特定的程序区域（如Java方法体）内应用基于行的合并。该方法在无需语言特定实现的前提下，将结构信息嵌入到合并流程中，平衡了精度与效率。

Result: 通过跨多种编程语言的真实合并场景实证研究，并与无结构、半结构化和全结构化工具对比，发现：更高结构粒度能提升自动冲突解决能力，但也会导致更激进的合并决策，增加漏报真实冲突的风险。MergirafSemi在减少虚假冲突的同时维持了竞争性的合并精度；在大多数常见场景中相比无结构工具大幅减少虚假冲突，相比半结构化语言特定工具保持相当效果，同时运行时间开销显著更低且执行行为更稳健。

Conclusion: MergirafSemi验证了半结构化合并方案的有效性，即在不过度增加结构建模复杂度的前提下，通过针对性利用语法结构信息能够在合并精度和效率之间取得良好平衡，为结构感知合并工具的设计提供了一种实用的折中路径。

Abstract: Developers frequently face merge conflicts when integrating concurrent changes. Most merge tools rely on unstructured, line-based comparisons, often producing spurious conflicts and missing actual conflicts. To address these limitations, structure-aware merge tools have been proposed, which leverage syntactic representations to improve merge accuracy. However, fully structured tools may incur higher computational cost, and language-specific tools require significant development and maintenance effort. To balance these trade-offs, we propose MergirafSemi, a language-agnostic semistructured merge tool that captures structural information without requiring full structural modeling or language-specific implementations, and applies line-based merging within specific program regions, such as method bodies in Java. Our tool leverages lightweight Concrete Syntax Trees to guide merging decisions while preserving flexibility and efficiency across languages. We evaluate our tool through an empirical study on real-world merge scenarios across multiple programming languages, comparing it with unstructured, semistructured, and structured tools. Our results show that increasing structural granularity improves automatic conflict resolution but can also lead to more aggressive merge decisions, increasing the number of missed actual conflicts. In contrast, MergirafSemi achieves a more balanced trade-off, reducing spurious conflicts while maintaining competitive accuracy and better runtime performance in most common scenarios. Compared to an unstructured tool, it substantially reduces spurious conflicts, and when compared to a semistructured language-specific tool, it achieves comparable effectiveness while exhibiting more robust execution behavior and significantly lower runtime overhead.

</details>


### [2] [The Devil Is in the Interface: Evaluating How Tool Architecture Shapes Coding Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11386)
*Xiangzhe Xu,Hamidreza Saghir,Qianhui Wu,Marc-Alexandre Côté,Tong Wang,Kiran Lakkaraju,Kexin Pei,Xiangyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究了编码智能体中工具架构（tool architecture）对智能体行为的影响，比较了六种工具架构在仓库级问题修复任务上的表现（涉及3个actor，共11,700条轨迹）。结果显示，即使工具能力相似，不同架构会显著改变智能体行为：结构化低级接口提升重复尝试一致性最多4.7倍；自然语言搜索扩大仓库探索并增加相关文件访问超11%；Python CodeAct风格接口以41.6%更少步骤和56.3%更低token消耗达到相似性能；而轻量级文本认知脚手架工具（如记录中间推理）对行为影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于智能体工具的研究主要聚焦于扩展智能体的能力（capability），但较少系统性地关注这些能力如何被组织、暴露给模型——即工具架构（tool architecture）这一设计维度。作者希望通过受控实验系统地理解工具架构对智能体行为的影响，弥补这一研究空白。

Method: 在仓库级问题修复任务上设计受控实验，固定底层信息和操作能力相似的前提下，比较六种工具架构（包括仅bash基础架构、结构化低级接口、自然语言搜索、Python CodeAct风格接口、轻量级文本认知脚手架工具等），跨三个actor和11,700条轨迹进行系统性对比分析，评估各架构对智能体行为（如一致性、探索范围、步骤数、token消耗）的影响。

Result: 主要发现包括：（1）相比仅有bash的基础架构，更结构化低级接口可提升重复尝试一致性最多4.7倍；（2）自然语言搜索能扩大仓库探索范围，增加相关文件访问超11%；（3）Python CodeAct风格接口以41.6%更少的步骤和56.3%更低的token消耗达到相似任务性能；（4）轻量级文本认知脚手架工具（如记录中间推理的工具）对智能体行为影响有限。

Conclusion: 工具架构是影响智能体行为的重要设计维度，即使工具能力相似，不同架构也会带来显著的行为差异。这为智能体系统设计提供了指导：选择适当的工具架构可以在保持任务性能的同时优化效率（步骤数、token消耗），并改善一致性和探索广度。同时，研究也指出某些工具架构（如轻量级认知脚手架）可能对行为影响有限，需要更谨慎的设计。

Abstract: As large language models continue to improve, agentic systems are becoming increasingly important, and tools are a key design dimension because they determine how agents access information and take action in their environments. Prior work on agent tooling has primarily focused on expanding what agents can do, but has paid less systematic attention to how those capabilities are organized and exposed to the model. We refer to this latter design dimension as tool architecture. We study tool architecture in coding agents through controlled experiments on repository-level issue fixing, comparing six tool architectures that hold the underlying information and actions similar while varying how they are organized and exposed to the model, across three actors and a total of 11,700 trajectories. Our experiments show that, even when tools provide similar capabilities, tool architecture changes agent behavior: Compared to a basic architecture where the agent has only the bash tool, more structured low-level interfaces improve consistency across repeated attempts by up to 4.7 $\times$; natural-language search broadens repository exploration and increases access to relevant files by more than 11%; and Python CodeAct-style interfaces achieve similar task performance with 41.6% fewer steps and 56.3% lower token usage. By contrast, lightweight text-based cognitive-scaffolding tools, such as tools that let the agent record intermediate reasoning, have limited effect on actor behavior.

</details>


### [3] [GraphAlignCoder: Aligning Program and Proof Graphs for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11394)
*Yueke Zhang,Zihan Fang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GraphAlignCoder是一个训练框架，通过构建实现图和形式化证明流图（基于Lean），将显式的正确性结构（程序区域为何正确的图描述）迁移到代码生成中，显著提升LLM在多个编程基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码LLM能生成语法正确但违反隐式语义约束的程序。已有的执行反馈训练方法只能判断程序是否失败，无法提供关于正确解决方案应如何组织的充分监督信号，缺乏对程序"为何正确"的结构化指导。

Method: GraphAlignCoder包含两条并行管线：(1) 构建实现图，捕获程序区域间的控制流和数据依赖关系；(2) 利用约束的Lean流水线产生证明轨迹，从中提取形式化证明流图。模型首先学习可执行代码及源自图结构的程序区域正确性描述，然后将这些知识巩固到代码生成阶段。

Result: GraphAlignCoder在所有基准上一致优于基线模型、纯代码SFT和CodeRL。相比CodeRL：LiveCodeBench v6解决数从38提升至50（相对增益31.6%）；BigCodeBench Hard从16提升至23（相对增益43.8%）；BigCodeBench Full从359提升至363。消融实验表明验证图注入带来初步推理增益，而验证到代码的巩固对跨基准鲁棒迁移至关重要。

Conclusion: 将显式正确性结构（实现图和形式化证明流图）通过两阶段训练注入代码LLM，比单纯执行反馈更有效，能够提升代码生成的语义正确性并在多种基准上实现稳健的跨基准迁移。

Abstract: Code large language models (LLMs) can generate syntactically plausible programs that nevertheless violate hidden semantic constraints. Existing execution-feedback training methods identify whether a completed program fails, but provide limited supervision about how a correct solution should be organized. We introduce GraphAlignCoder, a training framework that transfers explicit correctness structure into code generation.
  GraphAlignCoder constructs an implementation graph that captures control and dependence among program regions. In parallel, a constrained Lean pipeline produces proof traces, from which we extract a formal proof-flow graph. The model first learns executable code together with graph-derived descriptions of why individual program regions are correct, and then consolidates this knowledge into code generation. GraphAlignCoder consistently outperforms the base model, code-only SFT, and CodeRL across all benchmarks. Compared with CodeRL, it increases the solved count from 38 to 50 on LiveCodeBench v6 and from 16 to 23 on BigCodeBench Hard, corresponding to relative gains of 31.6% and 43.8%, while also improving BigCodeBench Full from 359 to 363 tasks. The ablation study further shows that verification-graph injection produces the initial reasoning gain, while verification to code consolidation is essential for robust cross-benchmark transfer.

</details>


### [4] [Simplifying Requirements Engineering in the Context of the LGPD: An LLM-Based Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11454)
*Cinara Gomes de Melo Carneiro,Renato de Freitas Bulcão Neto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）能否在巴西通用数据保护法（LGPD）框架下简化需求工程（RE），提出了一种利用现行立法自动生成用户故事和验收测试场景的方法，评估结果显示高性能，证实了LLMs从软件初始阶段确保法规合规的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私立法合规对需求工程构成复杂挑战，因为需要将法律规范转化为软件需求。这一法律到需求的翻译过程困难且容易出错，亟需自动化或简化的方法。

Method: 利用大型语言模型，以巴西通用数据保护法（LGPD）现行立法为输入，自动生成用户故事（User Stories）和验收测试场景（Acceptance Test Scenarios），从而将法律规范转化为可操作的软件需求。

Result: 评估结果显示该方法表现出高性能，证实了LLMs在确保法规合规方面从软件初始阶段即具有应用潜力。

Conclusion: LLMs能够有效简化隐私法规下的需求工程流程，为将法律文本自动转化为软件需求提供了可行路径，有助于实现从软件初始设计阶段起的监管合规。

Abstract: Compliance with privacy legislation poses a complex challenge to Requirements Engineering (RE): translating legal norms into software requirements. In this context, this study investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can simplify RE within the framework of the Brazilian General Data Protection Law (LGPD). The proposed approach utilizes current legislation to automatically generate User Stories and Acceptance Test Scenarios. The evaluation results demonstrated high performance, confirming the potential of LLMs to ensure regulatory compliance from the software's inception.

</details>


### [5] [Do Influence Tactics Matter? Investigating Prompt Framing Effects in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11513)
*Alex Deaconu,Anubhav Gupta,Manaal Basha,Nicholas Haydu,Gema Rodríguez-Pérez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次大规模实证探索了心理学启发的影响力策略（如理性说服、奉承、交换等八种影响战术）作为提示词框架对LLM在编码任务中表现的影响。在五个开源LLM和两个基准（LiveCodeBench和SWE-bench Verified）上评估发现，某些影响力诱导的提示词框架（尤其是强调紧迫性的）与代码正确性和安全性的下降相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已广泛集成到软件工程流程中，尽管已知提示词措辞和结构会影响模型性能，但基于心理学的提示词框架对LLM编码行为的影响尚未被探索。作者希望研究人类用于说服或激励他人的沟通策略是否能更有效地构建提示词，从而影响LLM在编码任务中的表现。

Method: 基于Yukl & Falbe的影响力战术分类法，将八种影响战术（如理性说服、奉承、交换等）操作化为可复现的提示词模板，在五个领先的开源权重LLM上使用LiveCodeBench和SWE-bench Verified两个基准进行系统评估，并从功能正确性、质量、可维护性和安全性四个维度衡量代码输出。

Result: 研究发现某些影响力诱导的提示词框架，特别是强调紧迫性的框架，与代码功能正确性和安全性的降低相关联。这是首个在大规模编码任务中实证检验影响力诱导提示词框架的研究，揭示了语言线索如何影响LLM输出。

Conclusion: 研究为设计透明和可解释的人机交互（特别是在代码生成场景中）提供了实用见解，表明提示词框架中的心理语言学线索对LLM编码行为有可测量的影响，值得在实际应用中加以关注和审慎设计。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into software engineering workflows, helping developers write, debug, test, and maintain code. While prompt wording and structure are known to influence model performance, the impact of psychologically inspired prompt framings remains unexplored. This study investigates whether different psychology-based communication strategies that humans use to persuade or motivate others can lead to more effective prompt framing, which may, in turn, affect LLM behaviour in coding tasks. Drawing on Yukl & Falbe's well-known taxonomy, we operationalized eight influence tactics (like rational persuasion, ingratiation, and exchange) into reproducible prompt templates. These prompt templates were evaluated across five leading open-weight LLMs using two widely adopted benchmarks: LiveCodeBench and SWE-bench Verified. We assessed the resulting code output on four key software quality dimensions: functional correctness, quality, maintainability, and security. Our results show that certain influence-induced prompt framings, particularly those emphasizing urgency, were associated with reduced correctness and security. This work presents the first large-scale empirical study of influence-induced prompt framing in software engineering tasks, offering insights into how linguistic cues may shape LLM outputs. We conclude with practical insights for designing transparent and interpretable human-AI interactions in code generation.

</details>


### [6] [Harnessing LLMs for Document-Guided Fuzzing of Python Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11744)
*Bin Duan,Tarek Mahmud,Meiru Che,Yan Yan,Naipeng Dong,Dan Dongseong Kim,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: VistaFuzz是一种文档引导的模糊测试技术，利用本地开源的LLM从API文档中提取参数规格，生成同时满足参数约束和参数间依赖关系的输入，以测试Python库API。在12个Python库的7,718个API上评估，发现40.1%的API存在参数间关系，成功报告74个问题，其中43个已被开发者确认，29个已修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python库在现代计算生态中至关重要，其API的可靠性直接影响下游应用。测试这些API需要满足单参数约束和参数间依赖关系的输入，而现有方法要么将约束隐式嵌入生成的程序中，要么依赖库特定的解析规则，缺乏通用且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: VistaFuzz采用文档引导的模糊测试方法：使用本地部署的开源大语言模型（LLM）从API文档中提取参数规格说明，并基于这些规格生成能够同时满足单参数约束和参数间依赖关系的测试输入。

Result: 在12个Python库的7,718个API上进行评估。结果显示40.1%的被测API存在参数间关系；禁用这种关系的解析后，这些API的有效生成率从95%以上骤降至31.6%–52.8%。VistaFuzz共报告74个问题，其中43个已获开发者确认，29个已被修复。

Conclusion: 参数间依赖关系在Python库API中普遍存在，而VistaFuzz通过文档引导+LLM的方式有效解决了这一测试难题，显著提升了测试输入的有效生成率，并成功发现了大量已被开发者认可的真实缺陷。

Abstract: Python libraries underpin deep learning, scientific computing, data analysis, and computer vision, making their reliability critical to downstream applications. Testing their APIs requires inputs that satisfy both per-parameter constraints and dependencies among parameters. Existing approaches either leave such constraints implicit in generated programs or rely on library-specific parsing rules. This paper introduces VistaFuzz, a document-guided fuzzing technique that uses a locally served open-sourced LLM to extract parameter specifications from API documents and generate inputs that satisfy both parameter constraints and inter-parameter dependencies. We evaluate VistaFuzz on 7,718 APIs across twelve Python libraries. Inter-parameter relationships occur in 40.1\% of tested APIs, and disabling their resolution reduces the valid generation rate on those APIs from above 95\% to 31.6\%--52.8\%. VistaFuzz reports 74 issues, of which 43 have been confirmed by developers and 29 have been fixed.

</details>


### [7] [Instruction Alignment for Binary Code Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11766)
*Huaijin Wang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出利用编译器调试信息中的指令级对齐知识来增强二进制代码表示学习，将指令对齐作为辅助训练目标，实验表明该方法能提升检索准确率并增强模型相似性判断的判别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制代码表示学习方法主要学习函数级嵌入，捕获的是粗粒度的语义关系，忽视了细粒度的指令级对应关系，从而丢失了编译器调试信息中蕴含的宝贵监督信号。

Method: 首先通过初步研究发现，经过函数级二进制代码相似性微调的模型比预训练模型具有更优的指令对齐能力，说明指令对齐与函数级嵌入质量存在强相关性。基于这一观察，设计了将指令对齐作为辅助训练目标显式纳入训练过程的方案。

Result: 实验表明，指令对齐训练提升了检索准确率，并为模型的相似性判断提供了更具判别力的信号。

Conclusion: 指令对齐作为一种辅助训练目标可以有效改善二进制代码表示学习的质量，在函数级相似性判断和检索任务上均带来积极收益。

Abstract: Binary code representation learning is a fundamental problem in software security and reverse engineering. Existing methods mainly learn function-level embeddings that capture coarse-grained semantic relationships between binary functions, but they largely ignore fine-grained instruction-level correspondences. This limitation misses valuable supervision signals available from compiler debug information, which can support the learning of more accurate and interpretable binary code representations.
  We propose to leverage instruction alignment knowledge to further improve binary code representation learning. Our preliminary study reveals that models finetuned for function-level binary code similarity exhibit substantially better instruction alignment than their pre-trained model, suggesting a strong correlation between instruction alignment and function-level embedding quality. Motivated by this observation, we design a training approach that explicitly incorporates instruction alignment as an auxiliary training objective. Our experiments show that instruction alignment training improves retrieval accuracy and provides more discriminative signal for the model's similarity judgments.

</details>


### [8] [Testing Deep Learning Library APIs via Cross-Framework Differential Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11886)
*Bin Duan,Ruican Dong,Naipeng Dong,Dan Dongseong Kim,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Xamt是首个跨框架的深度学习库API差分模糊测试方法，通过构建跨七个库的执行验证API等价组，结合方差引导的差分模糊测试，发现72个差异案例（4个崩溃+68个输出不一致），其中25个已被开发者确认，23个已修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习库API级测试技术多依赖于单一库内属性或CPU-GPU差分oracle，无法发现跨硬件后端行为一致的缺陷；需要跨框架差分测试来发现不同深度学习库之间API实现不一致的问题。

Method: Xamt方法包含：（1）通过显式API别名和参数角色归一化构建候选API对应关系；（2）使用成对执行和组级行为检查在普通输入上验证等价组；（3）对方差引导的差分模糊测试进行探索，涵盖普通、边界和非有限输入；（4）使用崩溃oracle和不一致性oracle标记异常执行和输出不一致。

Result: 在七个深度学习库中构建了676个执行验证的等价组，包含2,563个匹配API；识别出72个独立复现的差异案例，其中4个崩溃和68个输出不一致；72个开发者报告中25个已确认，其中23个已修复。

Conclusion: Xamt有效弥补了现有API级测试技术的不足，通过跨框架差分模糊测试成功发现了大量真实的深度学习库API差异缺陷，证明了该方法在发现跨库一致性问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Deep learning libraries underpin many safety- and reliability-critical applications, yet existing API-level testing techniques often rely on intra-library properties or CPU--GPU differential oracles and may miss defects that behave consistently across hardware backends. We present Xamt, a cross-framework differential fuzzing approach for deep learning library APIs. Xamt constructs and tests execution-validated groups of APIs intended to implement equivalent operations across seven libraries. It uses explicit API aliases and parameter-role normalization to construct candidate correspondences and validates them through pairwise execution and a group-level behavioral check on canonical ordinary inputs. The resulting groups are explored using variance-guided differential fuzzing with ordinary, boundary, and non-finite inputs. Crash and inconsistency oracles flag executions exhibiting abnormal termination or inconsistent outputs for subsequent reproduction and analysis. Across the seven libraries, Xamt constructs 676 execution-validated groups containing 2,563 matched APIs. Among these, Xamt identifies 72 independently reproduced discrepancy cases, including 4 crash cases and 68 output inconsistencies. Among the 72 developer reports, 25 have been confirmed, including 23 that have been fixed.

</details>


### [9] [Developing LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems in Software Engineering: A Mixed-Method Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11965)
*Mariama Celi Serafim De Oliveira,Motunrayo Osatohanmen Ibiyo,Marco Gianrusso,Claudio Di Sipio,Davide Di Ruscio,Phuong T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对软件工程领域中用于实现多智能体系统（MAS）的现有工具和框架进行了全面综述，通过对开源MAS框架进行定量分析（评估文档、功能和能力）以及定性评估（实现README.MD摘要这一共同用例），发现所选框架覆盖了MAS的基本组件，但缺少如智能体遥测等高级功能；在摘要任务上各框架的ROUGE分数无显著差异。文章最后提供了经验教训和挑战，帮助研究者和从业者根据需求选择合适的MAS框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能（尤其是LLM）驱动的MAS在软件工程中日益普及，但开发者面临着技术选择、协调规则设计和智能体角色规划等多重挑战。目前缺乏对现有MAS框架的系统性评估，因此需要提供全面的工具和框架概述，帮助开发者做出明智决策。

Method: 采用混合评估方法：（1）对最相关的开源MAS框架进行定量分析，从开发者视角评估其文档、特性和能力；（2）对选定框架的子集进行定性评估，通过实现一个共同的用例（README.MD文件摘要）来实证检验各框架的实际表现。

Result: 所选框架对MAS的基本组件覆盖良好，但高级功能（如智能体遥测）仍然缺失。在README.MD摘要任务中，各框架在ROUGE分数上无显著差异。此外，论文还提炼了一系列经验教训和挑战，以指导框架选择。

Conclusion: 当前MAS框架已具备支持软件工程任务的基础能力，但在高级功能方面仍有提升空间。由于各框架在核心任务上表现相当，选择框架时应更多关注特定需求和高级特性。文章提供的经验教训和挑战有助于研究者和从业者更有效地进行框架选型和系统设计。

Abstract: The proliferation of Generative Artificial Intelligence (Gen AI) powered by large language models (LLMs) has transformed the software development process, introducing new paradigms for code generation, debugging, testing, and maintenance. While early applications focused on leveraging single, independent LLMs to assist developers with isolated tasks, recent advances have shifted toward multi-agent systems (MAS) that orchestrate multiple LLM-based agents working collaboratively toward common objectives. Despite their promising potential, using MAS encompasses a set of challenges for developers who have to carefully select the right technology, devise proper coordination rules, and design specific roles for the involved agents. In this paper, we provide a comprehensive overview of the existing tools and frameworks for implementing MAS in software engineering. First, we conducted a quantitative analysis of the most relevant open source MAS frameworks by evaluating their documentation, features, and capabilities from the developers' perspective. Second, we performed a qualitative evaluation of a subset of the selected frameworks by implementing a common use case: the summarization of README.MD files. The findings show that the selected frameworks provide a good coverage of fundamental components of MAS, though advanced features such as telemetry of agents are still missing. In addition, the empirical evaluation shows that there is no significant difference in terms of ROUGE scores considering the summarization task. Finally, we provide a set of lessons learned and challenges that can help researchers and practitioners to select a suitable MAS framework according to their needs.

</details>


### [10] [RealisticTritonBench: A Benchmark for Triton-Kernel Generation in Real-World AI Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12004)
*Jinjun Huang,Zhongzhen Wen,Tongtong Xu,Meng Yan,Xin Xia,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RealisticTritonBench，第一个从真实AI框架的pull request中提取Triton内核生成任务的基准测试，解决了现有基准仅关注PyTorch-to-Triton翻译、只评估单个内核性能以及依赖可能有缺陷的手写评估脚本这三大局限。该基准将生成的内核集成回原始框架，通过端到端测试进行验证，实验表明现有LLM在真实世界Triton内核生成任务上仍表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估LLM生成Triton内核的基准存在三个关键局限性：(1) 任务仅限于PyTorch-to-Triton翻译，无法反映真实世界Triton任务的多样性和复杂性；(2) 仅评估单个内核性能而非端到端性能，而端到端性能才是AI框架实际部署的核心标准；(3) 依赖可能存在缺陷的手写评估脚本，模型可利用这些缺陷绕过正确性检查获得虚高的分数。

Method: RealisticTritonBench从流行的开源AI框架中系统地提取修改Triton内核的PR，将其转化为带具体工程上下文的生成任务。每个任务以自然语言需求为输入，要求生成对应的Triton内核实现，并配有完整可复现的评估环境。与以往基准不同，该基准将生成的内核集成回原始框架，使用端到端测试进行验证。

Result: 在RealisticTritonBench上评估了多个领先的LLM，发现它们在真实世界的Triton内核生成任务上仍然面临困难，表现不佳。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在自动生成真实世界生产级Triton内核方面仍有明显不足，RealisticTritonBench提供了一种更贴近实际部署场景的评估方式，为未来提升LLM生成Triton内核能力提供了基准和测试平台。

Abstract: In modern AI frameworks, GPU kernels are key to overall system performance. Combining usability, portability, and near-handwritten CUDA performance, Triton is widely adopted for implementing GPU kernels. Recent advances show the potential of large language models (LLMs) to automatically generate Triton kernels, reducing the manual effort required from expert kernel developers. Several benchmarks evaluate LLM-generated Triton kernels. However, they suffer from three key limitations: (1) they restrict tasks to PyTorch-to-Triton translation, failing to reflect the diversity and complexity of real-world Triton tasks; (2) they evaluate only individual-kernel performance rather than end-to-end performance, the core criterion for real-world deployment in AI frameworks; and (3) they rely on manually written evaluation scripts for individual kernels, which may contain flaws that models can exploit to bypass correctness checks and obtain inflated scores. To address these limitations, we introduce RealisticTritonBench, the first benchmark to derive Triton kernel generation tasks from real-world pull requests in popular AI frameworks, enabling realistic, production-like evaluation. RealisticTritonBench systematically extracts PRs that modify Triton kernels from popular open-source AI frameworks and transforms them into generation tasks with concrete engineering contexts. Each task takes a natural language requirement as input and requires a corresponding Triton kernel implementation, with a complete and reproducible evaluation environment. Unlike prior benchmarks focused on isolated kernel performance, RealisticTritonBench integrates generated kernels into their original frameworks and evaluates them using end-to-end tests, enabling a more faithful assessment. We evaluate leading LLMs on RealisticTritonBench and find that they still struggle with real-world Triton kernel generation tasks.

</details>


### [11] [From Safety Documentation to Safety Knowledge Support: An Evidence-Grounded LLM Framework for Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12025)
*Tuhinangshu Gangopadhyay,Rasmus Adler,Peter Liggesmeyer,Jan Reich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出医疗器械安全工程的核心研究问题并非安全文本生成，而是基于来源证据的安全知识支持。作者提出一个证据驱动框架，将设备工件、受控知识存储与检索、方法特定的候选安全项生成、批判与不确定性检查及专家审阅记录相结合，为专家决策提供准备、链接、检查和更新的候选安全工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗器械日益软件密集、互联且AI赋能，其开发需要符合ISO 14971和IEC 62304的风险管理证据，且这些证据需在需求、设计决策、软件变更、验证结果、投诉和售后数据间保持一致。现有工作成本高昂且依赖稀缺的安全与领域专家。虽然LLM可能降低部分工作量，但当前基于LLM的安全工程研究通常局限于孤立方法、通用提示或公开示例，对源链接、可追溯性、不确定性处理、生命周期更新和专家审阅记录支持有限，制约了其在受监管医疗器械开发中的应用。

Method: 提出一个证据驱动的框架，连接设备工件与受控知识存储和检索，执行方法特定的候选安全项生成，进行批判与不确定性检查，并记录专家审阅。框架为专家决策准备、链接、检查和更新候选安全工件，但不判定设备是否安全，也不提供监管批准。同时概述了一个评估策略，使用非公开或新建的医疗器械案例研究及专家参考分析，评估覆盖率、正确性、相关性、可追溯性、重复率、无依据声明和审阅工作量。

Result: 本文主要贡献在于问题的重新定义和研究框架的提出，属于概念性/立场性论文。其结果为：明确界定了核心研究问题（来源链接的安全知识支持而非文本生成），提出了完整的证据驱动框架，并设计了包含覆盖率、正确性、相关性、可追溯性、重复率、无依据声明和审阅工作量等多维度的评估策略。

Conclusion: 医疗器械安全工程的核心挑战在于建立证据支撑的安全知识体系，而非简单的文本生成。所述框架通过将LLM生成与专家审阅结合、强调可追溯性和不确定性管理，为在受监管环境中利用LLM辅助安全工程提供了可行路径。作者明确限定框架边界——不替代安全判定和监管批准——体现了对法规合规性的审慎态度。

Abstract: Medical devices are becoming more software-intensive, connected, and AI-enabled. Their development requires risk-management evidence aligned with ISO 14971 and, for software, IEC 62304. This evidence must be kept consistent across requirements, design decisions, software changes, verification results, complaints, and post-market data. These tasks are costly and depend on scarce safety and domain experts.
  Large language models (LLMs) may reduce parts of this effort because medical-device safety work is highly document-based. However, current LLM-based safety-engineering studies often address isolated methods, rely on generic prompting or public examples, and provide limited support for source links, traceability, uncertainty handling, lifecycle updates, and recorded expert review. This limits their use in regulated medical-device development.
  This paper argues that the central research problem is not safety-text generation, but source-linked safety-knowledge support. We propose an evidence-grounded framework that connects device artifacts, controlled knowledge storage and retrieval, method-specific generation of candidate safety items, critique and uncertainty checks, and recorded expert review. The framework prepares, links, checks, and updates candidate safety artifacts for expert decision-making. It does not decide whether a device is safe and does not provide regulatory approval. We also outline an evaluation strategy using non-public or newly built medical-device case studies and expert reference analyses to assess coverage, correctness, relevance, traceability, duplicate rate, unsupported claims, and review effort.

</details>


### [12] [ADEPT: A Unified Framework for Deep Learning Test Adequacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12144)
*Yidi Kao,Shawn Burnham,Tommi Rose Fahy,Ali Ghanbari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ADEPT是一个深度学习测试充分性度量框架，通过一致的工作流整合了神经元覆盖率、惊奇充分性、输入分布覆盖率、边界覆盖率和变异得分等多种代表性度量技术，并提供模板化接口、YAML配置管理和结构化结果报告，解决了现有度量工具难以复现、比较和采用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年中提出了许多深度学习测试充分性度量指标，但它们以独立研究原型的形式发布，具有不同的安装、预处理、执行流程和配置机制，导致极难复现、比较和在研究及实际部署中采用。

Method: ADEPT提供了一个一致执行工作流的框架，集成了多种代表性充分性技术（神经元覆盖率指标、惊奇充分性、输入分布覆盖率、边界覆盖率、源级和模型级变异得分），并提供基于模板的度量接口（带明确定义的扩展点整合新指标）、YAML配置管理、预处理缓存复用和结构化结果报告。

Result: ADEPT使研究人员和从业者能够无需花费数天或数周实现缺失工具或配置不同的研究原型，即可复现和应用充分性度量。论文提供了工程细节和演示视频（https://aub.ie/ADEPT_video）。

Conclusion: ADEPT通过标准化和一致化的框架设计，显著降低了深度学习测试充分性度量的使用门槛，使其易于集成到任何研究和开发工作流中，改善了该领域工具的可复现性和可采纳性。

Abstract: Over the past decade, many test adequacy metrics have been proposed for deep learning that characterize test dataset adequacy from different perspectives, e.g., neuron activation behavior, latent feature coverage, decision-boundary exploration, etc. However, these metrics are typically released as independent research prototypes with substantially different installation and preprocessing requirements, execution workflows, and configuration mechanisms. These complications make them quite difficult to reproduce, compare, and adopt in research work and practical deployment alike. In this paper, we present the engineering details of ADEPT, a framework that integrates representative adequacy techniques, including neuron-coverage-based metrics, surprise adequacy, input distribution coverage, boundary coverage, and source- and model-level mutation score, under a consistent execution workflow. ADEPT provides a template-based metric interface with well-defined extension points for integrating new adequacy metrics. Furthermore, it provides YAML-based configuration management, preprocessing-cache reuse, and structured result reporting, making it easy to use in any research and development workflows. ADEPT is designed for researchers and practitioners who wish to reproduce and apply adequacy metrics without spending days or weeks implementing missing tooling or configuring disparate research prototypes. A demo video is available at https://aub.ie/ADEPT_video.

</details>


### [13] [Towards Automated Domain Model Extraction from Source Code using Heuristics and Open-Source LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12228)
*Alessandra Mancas,Mounir Ammam,Hyacinth Ali,Kevin Delcourt,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种使用轻量级、可本地部署的大语言模型从源代码中自动化提取领域模型的方法，结合结构性和语义启发式与迭代推理，克服上下文窗口限制，在十个项目数据集上取得高F1分数，适用于隐私敏感的工业环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有LLM因隐私和保密约束无法在工业场景中使用，而紧凑型开源LLM受限于上下文窗口无法直接处理大型代码库，因此需要一种能在本地部署的轻量级LLM上运行的领域模型提取方法。

Method: 将结构性和语义启发式与基于LLM的迭代推理相结合，通过逐步分析代码元素的排序子集来克服上下文限制，渐进式地识别领域概念并细化领域边界，无需完整系统上下文。

Result: 在包含十个项目的数据集（每个项目包含精选的领域模型及其对应实现）上取得了高F1分数，同时方法完全可在本地部署的LLM上执行。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持高准确性的同时支持本地部署，特别适用于隐私敏感的工业环境中的逆向工程任务。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently shown strong capabilities for code understanding, making them promising for reverse engineering domain models from source code. However, state-ofthe- art proprietary LLMs cannot be used in many industrial contexts due to privacy and confidentiality constraints, while compact open-source LLMs that can run locally are limited by their context window and cannot process large code bases directly. In this paper, we propose an automated approach to extract domain models from source code using lightweight, locally deployable LLMs. Our method combines structural and semantic heuristics with iterative LLM-based reasoning to overcome context limitations. By progressively analyzing ranked subsets of code elements, the approach identifies domain concepts and refines domain boundaries without requiring full-system context. Our approach achieves high F1-scores on a dataset of ten projects, each comprising a curated domain model and its corresponding implementation, while remaining fully executable on locally deployable LLMs. This makes it particularly suitable for reverse engineering tasks in privacy-sensitive industrial environments.

</details>


### [14] [The Role Specialization Model (RSM): Coordinating LLM-Based Tools in Agentic Software Development - An Exploratory Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12311)
*Carlos Alberto Fernández-y-Fernández,Jorge R. Aguilar-Cisneros*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出角色专业化模型（RSM），通过案例研究将三种LLM工具（Antigravity、Gemini CLI、Qwen Code）按角色分工协调应用于Python桌面应用开发，并依据ISO/IEC 25010质量模型评估产出，验证了显式角色协调对开发流程组织与架构质量的促进作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）融入软件开发，出现了由自主智能体在人类监督下管理完整开发生命周期的Agentic Software Engineering（SE 3.0）范式。然而，如何协调具备不同能力的LLM工具、如何在真实开发流程中实现分工协作仍是未解问题，亟需一个系统化的角色分配框架来指导实践。

Method: 本研究采用探索性案例研究方法，提出角色专业化模型（RSM）作为协调框架，将Antigravity（基于Gemini 2.5后端的智能IDE）、Gemini CLI和Qwen Code（通过Ollama本地执行）三种LLM工具按角色分配进行协调。研究在增量开发一个Python桌面交互式气候数据可视化应用的过程中实施该框架，并围绕三个研究问题展开：工具协调方式（RQ1）、执行过程中的角色分配偏差及成因（RQ2）、以及产出对标ISO/IEC 25010质量模型的评估（RQ3）。同时记录工作流、观察到的偏差、提示词加固技术与定性质量评估。

Result: 结果表明，显式角色协调能够支持开发周期组织与架构质量的提升，但需要刻意设计的协调策略、上下文管理和对智能体产出的必要人工验证。此外，在执行RSM过程中观察到了与计划角色分配的偏离，这些偏差源于工具能力差异、上下文丢失等因素。最终产品通过ISO/IEC 25010模型进行了定性质量评估，显示架构质量得到改善。

Conclusion: 角色专业化模型（RSM）为多LLM工具协作开发提供了可行的协调框架，能有效支持开发流程组织与架构质量，但其成功依赖于精细的协调策略设计与人工监督验证，提示了Agentic SE尚需在工具集成和自动化验证方面进一步完善。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into software development workflows has given rise to a paradigm known as Agentic Software Engineering (SE 3.0), in which autonomous agents manage full development life cycles under human supervision. This paper presents an exploratory case study in which three LLM-based tools, Antigravity (an agentic IDE with a Gemini 2.5 backend), Gemini CLI, and Qwen Code (local execution via Ollama), are coordinated according to a role-distribution framework proposed in this work as the Role Specialization Model (RSM). Three research questions guide the study: (RQ1) how can LLM-based tools with distinct capabilities be coordinated through the RSM in a real development workflow; (RQ2) what deviations from the planned role distribution emerge during RSM execution and what factors explain them; and (RQ3) how does the resulting product compare against the ISO/IEC 25010 quality model. The objective was to propose the RSM with the incremental development of a Python desktop application for interactive climate-data visualization. The workflow, observed deviations, prompt-hardening techniques, and a qualitative quality assessment are documented. Results suggest that explicit role coordination can support development cycle organization and architectural quality, but requires deliberate coordination strategies, context management, and human verification of agent-generated outputs.

</details>

### [15] [TRACES: A Benchmark for Epistemic Reliability in Scientific Reasoning by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11415)
*Valentin Rodionov,Shamil Assylbekov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个包含42篇撤稿/欺诈/伪科学论文的探针语料库，用于直接测量大语言模型能否识别不可靠的科学文献。结果表明，尽管模型在初步拒绝率（IFR-a）上达到0.93，但在更严格的智能体式探针测试中，所有30个模型失效超过71%，22个模型失效超过90%，表明模型的拒绝行为主要源于话题键控的安全行为而非真正的认知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正被提议用于科学研究工作流，但在没有下游验证器的领域中，模型能否区分可靠与不可靠的科学文献这一关键能力尚未被直接测量。现有基准只评估有已知答案的事实性问题，无法捕获此类失败模式。

Method: 构建42篇撤稿/欺诈/伪科学论文的探针语料库。每个探针将从目标论文中近乎逐字提取的前言与科学上合理的研究设计请求配对，跨越五种声明类型（捏造观察、伪物理机制、魔法前提、合法化桥梁、空想实验）。使用两个互补评分（IFR-a评估直接拒绝，IFR-i评估识别不可靠但仍参与）以及Engagement Depth Index（EDI）量化对省略细节的复现程度。对30个模型进行10次重复运行评估。

Result: 聚合IFR-a为0.93±0.004，IFR-i为0.809±0.009。模型在95%的非空响应中参与了站不住脚的前提。所有评估模型在智能体式探针中失败超过71%，22/30模型失败超过90%。拒绝行为集中在少数高知名话题和特定探针上，在匹配结构对照下消失。

Conclusion: 模型的拒绝行为更符合话题键控的安全行为而非稳健的认识论能力，表明亟需为语言模型的科学部署建立护栏基础设施。

Abstract: Large language models are being proposed as agents in scientific workflows, in domains where no downstream verifier exists. Such deployment assumes the model can distinguish reliable scientific literature from unreliable literature, a capability that has not yet been directly measured. Existing benchmarks evaluate factuality on questions with known answers; the failure mode we target here is different. We introduce a probe corpus of 42 retracted, fraudulent, and pseudoscientific papers, paired with a methodology for eliciting and scoring single-shot model engagement with each paper's framing. Each probe pairs a preamble extracted near-verbatim from the target paper with a scientifically plausible study-design request. The probes span five claim types: fabricated observation, pseudophysical mechanism, magical premise, legitimization bridge, and cargo-cult experiment. Two complementary scores measure whether a model rejects the flawed premise outright (IFR-a) and whether it recognizes the unreliability while still engaging (IFR-i). A depth score, the Engagement Depth Index (EDI), quantifies reproduction of paper- or field-specific withheld details. Across 30 models and 10 repeated runs, aggregate IFR-a is 0.93 $\pm$ 0.004 and aggregate IFR-i is 0.809 $\pm$ 0.009. Models engaged with untenable premises in 95% of all non-empty responses. Every evaluated model fails more than 71% of agentic probes, and 22 of 30 models fail more than 90% of the time. Rejections are concentrated on a small number of high-notoriety topics and specific probes, and disappear under matched-structure controls. These results are consistent with topic-keyed safety behavior rather than robust epistemic competence, and indicate an urgent need for guardrail infrastructure for scientific deployment of language models.

</details>


### [16] [From Overlooked to Explored: Recovering Item Relations via Mixture of Perspectives for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11846)
*Junyoung Kim,Wonbin Kweon,Woojoo Kim,Jaehyung Lim,Dongha Kim,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PRISM模块，通过多视角透镜（Affinity View和Contrast View）重新校准自注意力机制，缓解序列推荐中相似性偏差问题，在7个真实数据集上超越SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐的核心挑战是捕捉用户偏好，而用户偏好隐含在物品间关系（包括同质和异质关系）中。虽然自注意力机制能计算物品对交互，但实证分析发现基于Transformer的序列推荐模型普遍存在相似性偏差：点积注意力分数过度偏向相似物品，系统性忽略了携带重要偏好信号的异质关系，限制了推荐性能。

Method: 提出PRISM（Perspective-based Relational Insight Synthesis Module），该模块使用K个视角透镜从不同观点校准注意力。具体包含两个视图：Affinity View（细化同质关系）和 Contrast View（暴露被相似性偏差抑制的异质关系），从而让模型能够捕捉完整的用户偏好谱系。

Result: 在7个真实世界基准数据集上的大量实验表明，PRISM一致性地优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: PRISM通过多视角关系合成有效地解决了序列推荐中自注意力机制的相似性偏差问题，能够同时捕捉同质和异质物品关系，从而提升推荐性能。

Abstract: Capturing user preference from a user's interaction sequence is the central challenge of Sequential Recommendation (SR). This preference intuitively emerges from inter-item relations: each item transition reflects a preference embedded in the relations between items, making the faithful capture of these relations essential for accurate recommendation. For this reason, self-attention is dominant in sequential recommendation for its ability to compute pairwise item interactions, yet our empirical analysis reveals that it consistently suffers from similarity bias across various types of transformer-based SR models: dot-product attention scores disproportionately favor similar items, systematically overlooking heterogeneous relations with meaningful preference signals and directly limiting recommendation performance. To address this, we propose PRISM (Perspective-based Relational Insight Synthesis Module), a module that re-examines item relations from multiple perspectives. PRISM employs K Perspective Lenses to calibrate attention from distinct viewpoints, combining an Affinity View that refines homogeneous relations and a Contrast View that exposes heterogeneous ones suppressed by similarity bias, enabling the model to capture the full spectrum of user preferences. Extensive experiments on seven real-world benchmarks demonstrate that PRISM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/327aem/PRISM/.

</details>


### [17] [Sci-Surf: Navigating Scientific Literature Discovery through Human Feedback and Intelligent Summarizatio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11973)
*Fang Guo,Qi Zhu,Rongcan Pei,Shuqi He,Hui Chen,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Sci-Surf是一个以意图为中心的知识发现系统，通过LLM驱动的用户画像实现反馈驱动的个性化推荐，并生成融合全文文本和视觉信息的多模态博客式论文解读。系统在为期一个月的在线评估中，通过语言化用户画像使预测对齐度平均提升10.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研出版物数量激增，研究者难以识别和深入理解相关新研究。现有学术发现平台依赖静态主题订阅或基于嵌入的相似度检索，仅提供摘要或简短总结，无法支持细粒度意图建模和深度论文解读。

Method: Sci-Surf结合两大部分：一是基于反馈驱动的个性化推荐系统，通过LLM进行用户画像构建来细化用户意图表示；二是多模态博客式论文消化系统，生成融合全文文本和视觉信息的结构化摘要。系统提供端到端的学术发现流水线。

Result: 通过真实用户评估，系统在推荐质量和消化质量上均有可量化的改进。具体而言，在为期一个月的在线评估中，整合语言化用户画像使预测对齐度平均提升10.4%。

Conclusion: Sci-Surf验证了意图中心设计在学术发现中的应用价值，证明了LLM驱动的用户画像和多模态论文消化能显著提升推荐准确性和论文理解深度，为下一代学术知识发现平台提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of scientific publications makes it increasingly difficult for researchers to identify relevant new studies and effectively comprehend them. Existing academic discovery platforms typically rely on static topic subscriptions or embedding-based similarity and provide only abstracts or short summaries, offering limited support for nuanced intent modeling and in-depth paper summarization. We present Sci-Surf, an intent-centric knowledge discovery system that integrates feedback-driven personalized recommendation with multi-modal blog-style paper digestion. Our approach refines user intent representations through LLM-based user profiling, while generating structured summaries that synthesize textual and visual information from full papers. The demo presents an end-to-end academic discovery pipeline and demonstrates measurable improvements in both recommendation quality and digestion quality through real-user evaluations. Specifically, the integration of verbalized profiles led to a 10.4% average improvement in predictive alignment with real-world user preferences throughout a month-long online evaluation.

</details>


### [18] [HCGRec: Hint-Conditioned Generative Recommendation with Semantic IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11980)
*Kangning Zhang,Haotian Fang,Xukun Luo,Hao Yin,Yang Gao,Peng Yan,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HCGRec是一种提示条件化的语义ID生成式推荐框架，通过为目标语义ID提供最小前缀提示（target-prefix hint）来恢复强化学习后训练阶段中零奖励样本的学习信号，并结合提示感知的信用分解（hint-aware credit decomposition）实现监督学习与GRPO的联合优化，显著降低零优势训练样本比例并提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义ID生成式推荐器在后训练阶段面临结构化优化瓶颈：当早期语义token进入错误分支时，有限的rollout难以到达真实物品，导致组相对优化得到完全相同的零奖励，无法产生有效优势信号（advantage）。超过70%的训练样本因此失效，严重制约了奖励后训练的效果。

Method: HCGRec包含三个关键设计：(1) 通过检查点rollout诊断每个训练实例，仅在当前生成器无法到达正确物品时提供最小目标前缀提示；(2) 被提示的前缀token与未提示的后缀token具有不同身份——前者是oracle提供的物品上下文，后者是采样的生成动作，因此引入提示感知的信用分解（hint-aware credit decomposition），对提示token使用监督学习保持物品语义和前缀结构对齐，对未提示后缀使用GRPO进行优化；(3) 将零奖励组转化为提示分支下物品token补全之间的信息性比较。

Result: 在多个序列推荐基准上，HCGRec相比监督微调和普通奖励后训练均有显著提升，同时将零优势训练样本从超过70%降低到20%以下。

Conclusion: HCGRec通过条件化提示策略有效解决了语义ID生成式推荐中奖励后训练的信号稀疏问题，为生成式推荐系统的后训练优化提供了一种实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic-ID generative recommenders represent each item as a short sequence of discrete semantic tokens and predict the next item by autoregressively generating this token sequence. This paradigm enables a unified generation interface for item IDs, histories, and item text, but it also creates a structured optimization bottleneck during reward-based post-training: when an early semantic token enters the wrong branch of the item-token space, finite rollout groups rarely reach the ground-truth item, so group-relative optimization receives identical zero rewards and produces no useful advantage. We propose Hint-Conditioned Generative Recommendation (HCGRec), a semantic-ID generative recommendation framework that recovers learning signal for such hard training instances. HCGRec diagnoses each instance with checkpoint rollouts and supplies a minimal target-prefix hint only when the current generator cannot reach the correct item. The model then generates the unhinted suffix under the hinted semantic branch, turning zero-reward groups into informative comparisons over item-token completions. Hinting also changes token identity: hinted prefix tokens are oracle-provided item context, while unhinted suffix tokens are sampled generation actions. We therefore introduce hint-aware credit decomposition, using supervised learning to preserve item-semantic and prefix-structure alignment for hinted tokens and GRPO to optimize the sampled suffix. Experiments on sequential recommendation benchmarks show that HCGRec substantially improves over supervised fine-tuning and vanilla reward-based post-training, while reducing zero-advantage training samples from over 70% to below 20%. The code is accessible at https://github.com/WncFht/GRec.

</details>


### [19] [Token-Level Credit Assignment Optimization for Generative Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12049)
*Xinpeng Zhao,Yang Liu,Ran Chen,Xinyu Ma,Daiting Shi,Pengjie Ren,Zhumin Chen,Zhaochun Ren,Xin Xin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向生成式检索的细粒度强化学习框架，通过在每个解码步骤估计token级别的相关性奖励来实现更精确的信用分配，从而对齐自回归DocID生成与检索目标，实验表明该方法优于序列级奖励基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索通过自回归方式生成DocID，本质上是一个序列决策问题，但检索效果通常在完整DocID生成后才被评估，导致token级生成与文档级相关性监督之间存在不匹配。现有的强化学习方法依赖序列级奖励，将相同的文档级反馈传播到所有解码步骤，这种粗粒度反馈难以识别哪些token决策导致了检索成功或失败。

Method: 提出细粒度强化学习框架，为每个token决策估计步骤级奖励，通过测量每个token决策如何改变对应生成轨迹的期望检索质量来实现。开发了适配DocID生成过程的具体奖励估计策略，并将其整合到策略优化框架中。

Result: 在检索基准上的实验表明，该方法一致地优于序列级奖励基线，验证了细粒度监督在对齐自回归DocID生成与检索目标方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 细粒度的token级奖励能够提供更精确的信用分配，帮助策略倾向于那些对文档级相关性贡献更直接的token决策，从而提升生成式检索的整体效果。

Abstract: Generative retrieval models perform document retrieval by autoregressively generating document identifiers (DocIDs). This process naturally forms a sequential decision problem, where each decoding step selects a DocID token and the complete token sequence determines the retrieved document. However, retrieval effectiveness is typically evaluated only after the full DocID is generated, creating a mismatch between token-level generation and document-level relevance supervision. As a result, existing reinforcement learning methods for generative retrieval mostly rely on sequence-level rewards, where the same document-level feedback is propagated to all decoding steps. Such coarse-grained feedback makes it difficult to identify which token decisions are responsible for successful or failed retrieval.
  In this work, we propose a fine-grained reinforcement learning framework for generative retrieval with token-level relevance rewards. Instead of assigning a single reward to the entire generated DocID, we estimate step-wise rewards by measuring how each token decision changes the expected retrieval quality of the corresponding generation trajectory. This enables more precise credit assignment and encourages the policy to favor token decisions that contribute more directly to document-level relevance. We further develop practical reward estimation strategies tailored to the DocID generation process and incorporate them into a policy optimization framework. Experiments on retrieval benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms sequence-level reward baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of fine-grained supervision for aligning autoregressive DocID generation with retrieval objectives.

</details>


### [20] [Making Collaborative Signals Count: Graph-Aware Large Language Models for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12184)
*Fenglin Yan,Bohao Wang,Jian Zhang,Yu Cui,Tongya Zheng,Ye Feng,Can Wang,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GALLM是一个图感知的大语言模型框架，通过构建覆盖文本令牌与物品令牌的协作图，将Text-Text、Item-Text和Item-Item三种关系转化为可学习的注意力偏置注入LLM注意力机制，无需额外图编码器即可捕捉全局协作信号，在四个基准数据集上平均HR@5提升9.76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为推荐系统骨干时，其语言中心化的预训练难以捕获用户-物品交互中的协作信号，而现有方法要么依赖外部推荐器注入协作表示，要么仅建模序列内依赖，无法充分利用全局协作模式。

Method: GALLM在文本令牌和物品令牌上构建协作图，建模三类关系：Text-Text关系保持语义依赖、Item-Text关系对齐物品令牌与其文本描述、Item-Item关系从全局物品共现模式中提取。这些关系被转化为轻量级可学习注意力偏置，集成到LLM注意力机制中，实现协作感知的令牌交互且无需额外图编码器。

Result: 在四个真实世界基准数据集上的实验表明，GALLM在所有对比基线中取得最佳性能，在HR@5上比最强基线平均提升9.76%。

Conclusion: 通过在LLM注意力机制中直接注入图关系偏置，GALLM有效弥补了LLM在顺序推荐中缺乏全局协作信号的问题，验证了图感知注意力增强对LLM推荐性能的显著促进作用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been widely adopted as backbones for recommender systems. However, their language-centric pretraining makes it difficult to capture collaborative signals implicit in user-item interactions, which are crucial for personalized recommendation. Existing methods either inject collaborative representations produced by external recommenders or model only intra-sequence dependencies, limiting their ability to exploit global collaborative patterns. To address this limitation, we propose GALLM, a graph-aware LLM framework for sequential recommendation. GALLM constructs a collaborative graph over text tokens and item tokens, and models three types of relations: Text--Text relations for preserving semantic dependencies, Item--Text relations for aligning item tokens with their textual descriptions, and Item--Item relations derived from global item co-occurrence patterns. These relations are transformed into lightweight learnable attention biases and incorporated into the LLM attention mechanism, enabling collaborative-aware token interactions without introducing an additional graph encoder. Experiments on four real-world benchmarks show that GALLM achieves the best performance among the compared baselines, improving over the strongest baseline by 9.76\% on average in HR@5.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [21] [FarSky: Task-Aware Latent-Space Coupling for Generative Intra-Hour Solar Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11254)
*Yann Fabel,Bijan Nouri,Milon Miah,Niklas Blum,Luis F. Zarzalejo,Julia Kowalski,Robert Pitz-Paal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FarSky，一个基于潜空间耦合和潜在扩散模型的生成式光伏辐照度预测框架，利用全天空成像仪图像实现短时（intra-hour）确定性及概率性太阳辐照度预测，在独立测试集上优于现有方法，最高提升11个百分点技能分，并显著改善爬坡事件检测（F1>60%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的太阳辐照度预测对光伏并网至关重要。虽然深度学习提升了预测精度，但现有方法多为确定性预测，难以表达不确定性，且对爬坡（ramp）事件的预测能力不足。这些局限促使作者探索生成式方法以获得概率性预测并改善对极端事件的捕获。

Method: FarSky采用两阶段框架：(1) 多任务自编码器学习共享潜空间表示，同时优化图像重建和辐照度估计两个任务，实现任务感知的特征学习；(2) 潜扩散模型以近期观测为条件，生成未来潜状态，再直接解码为辐照度预测。通过随机采样天然获得概率性预测。模型基于西班牙Almería太阳能平台的多年ASI数据集开发，并在两个独立测试集上评估。

Result: FarSky在确定性预测和概率性预测方面均取得最佳综合性能，预测技能分较基线最高提升11个百分点；在爬坡事件检测方面显著优于现有方法，F1分数超过60%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，将生成模型与任务感知的潜空间耦合相结合，在太阳辐照度预测领域具有巨大潜力，能够同时提供高精度的确定性预测和可靠的概率预测，并有效提升对爬坡事件的感知能力。

Abstract: Accurate solar irradiance forecasting is essential for the reliable integration of photovoltaic power into modern electricity grids. All-sky imagers (ASI) provide high-resolution observations of clouds, making them well suited for intra-hour forecasting. Recent deep learning approaches have substantially improved forecast accuracy but are often limited by deterministic predictions and a reduced capability to anticipate ramp events. This work proposes FarSky, a generative forecasting framework that leverages latent-space coupling to learn task-aware representations of sky images. A multi-task autoencoder first learns a shared latent representation for image reconstruction and irradiance estimation. A latent diffusion model then generates future latent states conditioned on recent observations, from which irradiance forecasts are directly decoded. Probabilistic forecasts are inherently obtained through stochastic sampling. The framework is developed using a multi-year ASI dataset acquired at the Plataforma Solar de Almería, Spain, and evaluated on two independent test datasets against persistence, state-of-the-art end-to-end, and generative forecasting approaches. FarSky achieves the best overall deterministic and probabilistic forecasting performance, improving forecast skill by up to 11 percentage points. Furthermore, it substantially improves ramp event detection over existing methods, achieving F1-scores above 60%. These results demonstrate the potential of combining generative models with task-aware latent-space coupling for solar forecasting.

</details>


### [22] [Why AI Detection Fails for Academic Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11256)
*Jonathan A. Karr,Grigorii Khvatskii,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对照实验量化了AI检测器在学术诚信中的政策失效问题：合规的"仅润色摘要"式AI辅助编辑被误判为不当行为的比率高达64-80%，而未经修改的原文亦有9-15%被误报；经"Undetectable AI"人类化改写后规避检测近乎完全（<4%被标记）。诚实使用AI编辑的受罚风险反而高于借助人类化工具规避检测，因此检测器分数不应作为不当行为的独立证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业AI检测器被机构用于学术诚信审查，但检测器无法区分AI编辑与完整LLM草稿，可能将两者均视为学术不端。作者旨在量化这种政策失效的具体程度，并揭示诚实AI辅助使用者在检测体系下面临的不公正风险。

Method: 对已发表的英文摘要开展受控研究，覆盖四个学科领域，时间跨度2013-2015（AI前）与2023-2025（AI后）两个时期；以tau=0.50的代理人类/AI标签，使用Pangram和GPTZero等检测器，设置"仅润色摘要"（符合指南的AI辅助代理）与经Undetectable AI人化后的AI改写等条件进行对比检测。

Result: "仅润色摘要"的编辑在Pangram/GPTZero下被标记率为64-80%；未修改的2023-2025原文被标记率为9-15%，非STEM领域显著高于STEM（p<0.001）；高检测分数与长token和学术词汇表（AWL）密度相关而非单纯作者意图。经Undetectable AI人类化后，被标记率低于4%（FNR>96%），规避近乎完全。

Conclusion: 检测器分数不能作为学术不端行为的独立证据。当前AI检测工具既无法区分合规AI编辑与不当使用，又极易被人化工具规避，导致诚实使用者反而承担更高制裁风险，政策需要重新校准。

Abstract: Institutions use commercial AI detectors for academic integrity, yet detectors cannot distinguish AI editing from full LLM drafts and may treat both as misconduct. In a controlled study of published English abstracts (four domains; 2013 to 2015 vs. 2023 to 2025), we quantify this policy failure under proxy human/AI labels at tau=0.50. Light "refine abstract only" edits, a proxy for guideline-compliant AI assistance, are flagged at 64 to 80% (Pangram/GPTZero). Unmodified 2023 to 2025 originals are flagged at 9 to 15%, with non-STEM rates far above STEM (p<0.001); elevated scores track long-token and Academic Word List density, not authorship intent alone. After Undetectable AI humanization, evasion is near-total: fewer than 4% of AI-labeled rewrites remain flagged (post-humanization detection rate <4%; FNR >96%). Honest AI-editing results in a higher sanction risk than humanizer-assisted evasion. Therefore, detector scores should not serve as standalone misconduct evidence.

</details>

*Johan Larsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Basin是一个Rust编程语言的数值优化库，提供统一的接口来表述和求解最小化函数的问题，包含丰富的求解器目录并对约束提供一流支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值优化是科学计算中的基础任务，广泛应用于模型拟合、仿真校准、机器学习训练和工程参数选择等领域，需要一个统一、一致的库来表述和求解此类问题。

Method: Basin为Rust语言设计，提供（1）单一的、一致的接口来表述优化问题；（2）广泛的求解器目录；（3）对约束问题的一流支持。

Result: 论文仅提供了Basin库的设计概述，未在摘要中给出具体的性能评测或实验结果。

Conclusion: Basin旨在为Rust生态提供一个全面且一致的数值优化解决方案，覆盖从无约束到有约束的多种优化场景。

Abstract: Basin is a numerical optimization library for the Rust programming language. Numerical optimization is the task of finding the inputs that minimize a function, and it is a fundamental element across the sciences: fitting a model to data, calibrating a simulation, training a machine learning model, or choosing engineering parameters that minimize cost. Basin gives users a single, consistent way to both state and solve such problems, with a broad catalog of solvers and first-class support for constraints.

</details>


### [24] [Federated Learning for Distributed CNC Tool Wear Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11281)
*Afsana Khan,Morris Stallmann,Marcin Pietrasik,Charis Kouzinopoulos,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在CNC刀具磨损预测中的应用，通过将刀具轨迹数据分布在模拟客户端上，验证了联邦学习模型性能接近集中式学习且显著优于本地客户端模型，支持分布式制造环境中的协作式刀具磨损预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刀具磨损预测对CNC加工中的产品质量和工艺可靠性至关重要。机器学习方法虽有潜力，但工业环境中加工数据呈分布式分布，且各机器、站点或组织之间禁止数据共享，限制了集中式机器学习方法的应用。联邦学习为此提供了无需传输原始运营数据即可进行协作训练的框架。

Method: 将刀具轨迹数据分布在模拟客户端上以模拟联邦学习场景；训练联邦学习模型，并与集中式参考模型及本地客户端基线模型进行性能对比。

Result: 联邦学习模型的性能接近集中式学习，且相比本地客户端模型有显著提升。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够支持在分布式CNC制造环境中实现协作式刀具磨损预测，为工业场景中的数据隐私保护和模型协作训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Tool wear prediction is an important task in CNC machining, where accurate monitoring of tool condition supports product quality and process reliability. Machine learning methods have shown potential for this task, but their use in industrial environments is limited by the distributed nature of machining data and by restrictions on data sharing between machines, sites, or organizations. Federated learning offers a suitable framework for this setting by enabling collaborative model training without transferring raw operational data. This paper investigates federated learning for CNC tool wear prediction. Tool trajectories are distributed across simulated clients to represent a federated learning scenario. The federated models are compared against centralized references and local client baselines. Results show that federated learning achieves performance close to centralized learning and improves significantly over local client models. These findings indicate that federated learning can support collaborative tool wear prediction in distributed CNC manufacturing environments.

</details>


### [25] [Terminal Symmetry as a Decision Resource: Statewise Refinement for Anytime Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11318)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"终态对称性"（terminal symmetry）这一决策资源视角，将其分解为"传输-精炼-验证"（transport-refine-certify）三阶段原则，并实例化为方法\method{}：通过 episode 固定的传输过程结构、状态受限的过程秩、状态依赖的残差秩以及序数秩 meet 机制，为顺序构造任务提供完成保证和最优验证器查询界。在 CAD 装配、Mini-Programs 和精确填充等任务上，任意时刻 AUC 分别提升最高 6.77、21.75 和 8.68 点；在官方 GRN 场景的 1,135 个目标移除 episode 上取得最低平均封顶验证成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多顺序构造任务在完成时呈现精确对称性，但执行过程是有方向的、依赖历史的。已有方法未系统利用终态对称性作为决策资源，本文旨在建立一种统一的"决策-资源"视角，将终态对称性转化为可复用的构造决策依据。

Method: 提出决策-资源分解：过程证据提供方向性（transport），终态对应关系将结构传输到等价结果（transport），实现状态证据在状态转移后精炼其当前决策相关性（refine），固定验证器证明执行合法性（certify）。实例化为\method{}：episode 固定的传输过程结构 + 状态受限过程秩 + 状态依赖残差秩（在接受转移后刷新）+ 序数秩 meet（top-k 集恰为两个提议前缀的并集）。meet 在前缀覆盖下提供完成保证，并在对应前缀信息模型下达到最紧验证器查询界。两状态构造预测了转移后动态-静态的严格分离。

Result: 在 CAD 装配、Mini-Programs 和精确填充任务上，状态级刷新使任意时刻 AUC 分别提升最高 6.77、21.75 和 8.68 点。在官方 GRN 场景 1,135 个目标移除 episode 上，\method{} 在所有三种尺度下均取得最低平均封顶验证器成本（对比 GRN 与 CDGS 风格规划器）。状态级信号在聚合与调度器组织间均可迁移。

Conclusion: 终态对称性可以成为一种可复用的顺序构造决策资源。通过"传输-精炼-验证"分解，该方法在多个任务域上一致提升性能，并具备最优查询效率和理论保证。

Abstract: Many sequential construction tasks exhibit exact symmetry at completion while their execution remains directed and history-dependent. We develop a decision-resource view of terminal symmetry: process evidence supplies directionality, terminal correspondence transports that structure across equivalent outcomes, realized-state evidence refines its current decision relevance after transitions, and a fixed verifier certifies execution. This decomposition yields transport--refine--certify. \method{} instantiates the principle with an episode-fixed transported process structure, its state-restricted process rank, a state-dependent residual rank refreshed after accepted transitions, and an ordinal rank meet whose top-$k$ set is exactly the union of the two proposal prefixes. The meet provides a completion guarantee under prefix coverage and attains the tight worst-case verifier-query bound under the corresponding prefix information model; a two-state construction predicts a strict post-transition dynamic--static separation. Across CAD assembly, Mini-Programs, and exact-fill packing, statewise refresh improves anytime AUC by up to $6.77$, $21.75$, and $8.68$ points, respectively. On 1,135 target-removal episodes from the official GRN OOD scenes, \method{} attains the lowest mean capped verifier cost at all three scales among the compared GRN and CDGS-style planners. The statewise signal also transfers across aggregation and scheduler organizations. Terminal symmetry thereby becomes a reusable decision resource for directed construction.

</details>


### [26] [Defending against Model Extraction for GNNs with Model Reprogramming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11495)
*Yan Wen,Zhenyi Wang,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphRP，一种基于模型重编程的主动防御框架，用于保护图神经网络在MLaaS场景下免受模型提取攻击。GraphRP通过结构感知门控机制和图拓扑原型，构建动态"结构防火墙"，在保持良性查询性能的同时最大化攻击者的估计误差。实验证明其在hard-label和soft-label攻击下均能显著降低攻击有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN模型提取攻击防御存在"欧几里得偏见"：直接迁移图像领域的防御策略（如随机噪声）到图数据上，忽略了节点间复杂的拓扑依赖，导致严重的模型效用下降。被动方法（如水印）无法实时阻止模型窃取，亟需一种主动且图感知的防御方案。

Method: GraphRP采用模型重编程（Model Reprogramming）范式实现主动防御。核心组件包括：(1) 基于可学习拓扑原型驱动的结构感知门控机制，动态选择性地调节模型决策边界；(2) "结构防火墙"设计：对位于训练流形上的良性查询保持保真度，对对抗性查询沿扰动方向最大化Fisher信息；同时给出理论保证，证明攻击者估计误差的下界随重编程噪声的结构敏感性增加而增大。

Result: 在hard-label和soft-label两种模型提取攻击设置下的广泛实验表明，GraphRP能够显著降低攻击有效性，同时保持良性查询的模型效用。

Conclusion: GraphRP通过将模型重编程与结构感知门控相结合，提供了一种有效的主动GNN防御框架，弥补了现有方法在拓扑感知和实时防御方面的不足。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) serve as the backbone for high-stakes applications in Machine-Learning-as-a-Service (MLaaS). Still, their black-box deployment exposes them to Model Extraction (ME) attacks, in which adversaries steal intellectual property by querying APIs. Existing defenses suffer from a critical ''Euclidean bias'': they transfer image-based strategies (e.g., random noise) to graphs, ignoring the complex topological dependencies between nodes, which often results in severe utility degradation. Passive methods like watermarking also fail to prevent theft in real time. To bridge this gap, we propose GraphRP (Graph Reprogramming Protection), a proactive defense framework that repurposes Model Reprogramming for security. Unlike static perturbations, GraphRP introduces a Structure-Aware Gating Mechanism driven by learnable topological prototypes. This creates a dynamic ''structural firewall'' that selectively modulates the model's decision boundary: it preserves fidelity for benign queries residing on the training manifold, while maximizing the Fisher Information along the perturbation direction for adversarial queries. Under standard assumptions (bounded loss, optimal attacker, and local second-order approximation), we prove a lower bound on the attacker's estimation error that increases with the structural sensitivity of the reprogramming noise. Extensive experiments on both hard-label and soft-label ME attacks demonstrate that GraphRP significantly degrades attack effectiveness while preserving benign utility.

</details>


### [27] [Contextual Quality-Diversity Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning for HVAC Control in Tropical Commercial Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11324)
*Tran Le Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CQD-ERL的上下文质量-多样性演化强化学习控制器，用于热带水冷冷水机组及其空气侧的监督控制。该控制器维护一个由数据驱动的运行环境（每日天气和负荷工况聚类）与行为描述符联合索引的产品档案，通过无梯度演化算子和软演员-评论家策略梯度算子共同填充（共享一个回放缓冲区），每个动作在执行前经过确定性安全屏蔽过滤。控制器在新加坡商业建筑的两层降阶环境中训练，并在全年回溯测试中与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线进行对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对暖通空调系统（特别是热带水冷冷水机组）的监督控制问题，传统单一标量化策略难以应对复杂多变的气候和负荷工况，需要一种能够维持多样化专门策略的控制方法以提升环境适应性和鲁棒性。

Method: 采用上下文质量-多样性（Contextual Quality-Diversity）框架结合演化强化学习（ERL）。具体包括：(1) 维护一个产品档案，由数据驱动的运行环境（天气与负荷聚类）和情境不变行为描述符联合索引；(2) 使用无梯度演化算子与软演员-评论家（SAC）策略梯度算子共同优化，两者共享一个回放缓冲区；(3) 每个动作执行前通过确定性安全屏蔽（safety shield）进行过滤；(4) 在两层降阶环境（表示潜在负荷、冷却塔趋近温度和湿度约束）中进行训练。

Result: 控制器在新加坡商业建筑的全年回溯测试中进行了评估，与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线进行了对比，论文摘要未报告具体量化结果。

Conclusion: CQD-ERL通过维护情境索引的多样化策略档案并引入安全性屏蔽，为暖通空调系统提供了比单一策略更优的监督控制方案，在全年跨气候与负荷变化场景下具备优于ASHRAE Guideline 36基线的潜力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a contextual quality-diversity evolutionary reinforcement-learning controller, CQD-ERL, for the supervisory control of a tropical, water-cooled chiller plant and its associated air side. Rather than converging to a single scalarised policy, the controller maintains a product archive of specialised policies indexed jointly by a data- driven operating context, a cluster of daily weather and load regime, and a context-invariant behaviour descriptor, filled by a gradient-free evolutionary operator and a soft-actor-critic policy-gradient operator that share one replay buffer. Every action is filtered through a deterministic safety shield before execution. The controller is trained on a two-tier reduced-order environment representing the latent load, cooling-tower approach and humidity constraints of a Singapore commercial building, and is evaluated over a full annual backtest against an ASHRAE Guideline 36 baseline.

</details>


### [28] [FunnelCausalNet: Funnel-aware Joint Conversion-Revenue Uplift for Multi-tier Coupon Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11675)
*Yu Zhang,Zhihan Wang,Guanlin Chen,Min Jiang,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FunnelCausalNet，一种将二元转化头与非负条件价值头耦合的抬升估计器，通过可分解的GMV漏斗结构降低方差，并在半合成与工业数据集上展现一致的ROI前沿优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优惠券活动旨在同时提升转化率和收入，但GMV遵循从转化到条件订单价值的确定性漏斗，且具有零膨胀和重尾分布特征，直接建模GMV抬升效果困难且方差大。

Method: FunnelCausalNet将二元转化头与非负条件价值头通过μ_gmv=μ_conv×μ_val耦合；在RCT假设下通过均方误差分析识别漏斗分解可降低方差的区间；配套边际分裂保形CARTE摘要、Bonferroni联合审计带以及基于RCT锚定估计的拉格朗日预算分配器。

Result: 在半合成Criteo-MT7上，FunnelCausalNet的均值AUUC_GMV在11个基准中处于领先特征交互基准的一个种子标准差内；消融实验使GMV效应误差较直接GMV回归降低18%-48%；在约490万保留曝光记录的工业Hotel-Coupon RCT日志上，全部7个相关锚点（10%-60%）处种子平均DeltaROI均最优；在稀疏二元消费公开基准上明确划分了收入聚焦排序器优于抬升曲线代理的机制边界。

Conclusion: 漏斗分解式抬升估计在理论分析与实证中均展现出方差降低与ROI一致的优越性，但需注意其理论为启发式而非共享表示神经网络的严格保证，且工业结果仅视为描述性前沿一致性而非独立显著性。

Abstract: Coupon campaigns seek to lift both conversion and revenue, but gross merchandise value (GMV) follows a deterministic funnel from conversion to conditional order value and is zero-inflated and heavy-tailed. We propose FunnelCausalNet, an uplift estimator coupling a binary conversion head with a nonnegative conditional-value head through $μ_{\mathrm{gmv}}=μ_{\mathrm{conv}}μ_{\mathrm{val}}$. Under explicit RCT, support, rate-gap, and cross-head covariance-control assumptions, an idealized leading-order MSE comparison identifies a regime in which funnel composition can reduce pointwise variance; this is a heuristic, not a guarantee for the shared-representation neural model. The estimator is paired with marginal split-conformal CATE summaries, combined through a Bonferroni union as audit bands, and a Lagrangian budgeted allocator using RCT-anchored estimates for subsidy-aware ROI accounting. On semi-synthetic multi-tier Criteo-MT7, FunnelCausalNet's mean AUUC_GMV is within one seed standard deviation of the leading feature-interaction baseline among eleven baselines, while a controlled ablation reduces GMV effect error versus direct GMV regression by 18--48% across tested zero-inflation regimes. On de-identified industrial Hotel-Coupon RCT logs with about 4.9 million hold-out exposure records per seed, expected-outcome evaluation sweeps full LP frontiers; FunnelCausalNet has the best seed-averaged mean DeltaROI at all seven correlated anchors from 10% to 60%, which we treat as descriptive frontier consistency rather than independent significance. On sparse binary-spend public benchmarks, revenue-focused rankers can dominate uplift-curve proxies, defining an explicit regime boundary.

</details>


### [29] [Long-Horizon Forecasting of Complete Financial Statements with Forma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11327)
*Travis L. Johnson,Jiannan Jiang,Soumyabrata Chaudhuri,Yihao Chen,Lauren Falvey,Donal O'Cofaigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProForma-20Q基准和Forma模型，首次实现长达20个季度的完整财务报表联合预测（78个条目），超越通用大模型和经典时序模型，且预测区间校准良好、满足会计恒等式，并支持无需重训的情景分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究最多预测一年内的财务报表，但折现现金流估值中大部分企业价值位于一年之后的时间窗口。因此需要能够完整、长期地联合预测财务报表的能力，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 构建ProForma-20Q基准，包含78个报表项目、预测未来1-20个季度，评分采用变化空间R²；提出Forma模型，将报表表示为(账目、季度、数值)三元组集合，采用掩码元组高斯似然进行训练。

Result: Forma在基准上击败所有对比方法（经典机器学习、链式梯度提升、零样本时序基础模型和前沿大语言模型），且随着预测期限延长优势扩大；其高斯预测区间从不欠覆盖；预测几乎满足会计恒等式，且精确一致性可恢复而不损失统计显著性精度；固定未来营收路径能锐化其余报表条目的预测。

Conclusion: 面向财务报表预测这一专业任务，专门的模型训练优于通用大模型的规模优势；Forma展示了长期、完整、可解释且支持情景分析的财务报表预测能力，对估值实践具有重要价值。

Abstract: Specialist training beats generalist scale when forecasting financial statements. To our knowledge, no prior work jointly forecasts complete financial statements beyond one year, yet in a discounted-cash-flow valuation most firm value sits past that window. We release ProForma-20Q, a reproducible benchmark for forecasting 78 statement line items 1-20 quarters ahead, for anonymized firms, from past statements and an industry code, scored by change-space $R^2$. On it, Forma, a transformer that reads statements as sets of (account, quarter, value) tuples and maximizes a masked-tuple Gaussian likelihood, beats every competitor we field: classical machine learning, chained gradient boosting, a zero-shot time-series foundation model, and frontier large language models. Its lead widens with horizon, where valuation needs accuracy most, and its Gaussian predictive intervals never under-cover. Forma's forecasts nearly satisfy accounting identities; exact coherence is recoverable at no statistically significant accuracy cost. Its tuple interface supports scenario analysis without retraining, and we show that pinning future revenue paths sharpens the rest of the statement.

</details>


### [30] [Weightless Fine-Tuning: Personalizing LLMs via Logit-Space Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11342)
*Bohan Zhang,Anqi Ni,Yixin Wang,Paramveer S. Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT)，一种无需权重更新的训练自由解码时方法，通过估计dropout诱导的交叉协方差来构建跨前缀传输算子，将作者的监督残差传输到当前提示的logit空间，从而在个性化场景中近似SFT的效果，且计算开销不到SFT的7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）是适配LLM到目标分布的标准方法，但在个性化等场景中，每个作者都需要独立的权重访问、优化、存储和重训练，导致成本过高，亟需一种轻量级、无需权重更新的替代方案。

Method: WFT在解码时计算作者训练序列上的监督残差，并通过由dropout诱导的交叉协方差估计的跨前缀传输算子，将这些残差传输到当前提示。该算子刻画了某一上下文的扰动如何传播到另一上下文的预测，用logit空间修正替代基于梯度的参数更新。

Result: 在三个LaMP个性化基准上，WFT取得了跨数据集的最佳平均性能，在单个任务上匹配或超过SFT，并在平均性能上优于其他轻量级基线。在预算受控的比较中，WFT以不到7%的有效计算量逼近SFT性能。Logit分析显示WFT与SFT诱导的logit位移余弦相似度达0.875，覆盖95%以上的下一个token概率质量。

Conclusion: WFT能够在不修改模型权重的情况下捕捉监督适配的分布效果，为个性化等需要高效多次适配的场景提供了一种训练自由的可行替代方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is a standard approach for adapting LLMs to a target distribution, but in settings such as personalization, where each author requires separate weight access, optimization, storage, and retraining, its costs become prohibitive. We propose Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT), a training-free decoding-time method that approximates the distributional effect of SFT without weight updates. WFT computes supervised residuals on an author's training sequence and transports them to the current prompt through a cross-prefix transport operator estimated from dropout-induced cross-covariance. The operator captures how a perturbation at one context propagates to predictions at another, replacing gradient-based parameter updates with logit-space corrections. On three LaMP personalization benchmarks, WFT achieves the best average performance across datasets, matches or exceeds SFT on individual tasks, and outperforms other lightweight baselines on average. In a budget-controlled comparison, WFT approaches SFT performance using less than 7% of the effective computation. Logit-level analysis shows a cosine similarity of 0.875 between the logit shifts induced by WFT and SFT over 95% of the next-token probability mass, suggesting that WFT captures the distributional effect of supervised adaptation without modifying model weights.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在真实系统部署中的关键障碍是超参数选择，尤其是在缺乏模拟器且在线实验成本高昂的场景下。先前提出的基于离线数据训练校准模型的方法仅在简单模拟环境中被评估过，缺乏真实工业场景下的验证。

Method: 在市政水处理厂这一真实工业环境中，评估多种校准模型方法（包括使用拉普拉斯距离度量的k近邻模型），基于高维、非平稳的传感器数据执行nexting预测任务，用于离线超参数选择。

Conclusion: 该工作为离线动力学模型支持真实世界RL部署提供了概念验证，同时指出了实践中面临的挑战和未来研究方向。

Abstract: A key obstacle to deploying reinforcement learning in real-world systems is hyperparameter selection, particularly when simulators are unavailable and online experimentation is costly. Prior work has proposed calibration models trained on offline data to approximate environment dynamics and enable offline hyperparameter selection, but these methods have so far been evaluated only in simple simulated settings. In this paper, we present the first application of calibration models in a real-world industrial setting: a municipal water treatment plant. We evaluate several calibration model approaches, including a k-nearest neighbors model with a Laplacian distance metric, on high-dimensional, non-stationary sensor data for nexting prediction tasks. Our results show that these models can generate realistic long-horizon rollouts and recover meaningful hyperparameter sensitivity trends. We further examine how calibration models scale to year-long datasets, how they support the selection of fine-tuning learning rates for pre-trained agents, and how robust they are under distribution shift. Overall, our findings provide a proof of concept for using offline dynamics models to support RL deployment in real-world environments, while highlighting important practical challenges for future work.

*Hang Fan,Wei Wei,Shengwei Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向日前电价预测的市场信息感知自适应框架，将Chronos-2时序基础模型通过门控低秩适配器（LoRA）迁移到数据稀缺的电力市场。在四个中国省级日前现货市场上，相比零样本Chronos-2平均MAE/RMSE降低6.24%/7.99%，相比原始Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电价预测对市场参与者至关重要，但电价具有波动性、市场特异性和对系统条件的高度依赖。现有监督方法高度依赖市场特定历史数据，在新建立或数据稀缺的市场中应用受限。因此需要一种不依赖目标市场标签的跨市场迁移方案。

Method: 提出market-information-aware adaptation框架：(1)构建多源市场信息（MSMI）接口，将7天价格上下文与预清算供需、备用、检修、发电容量及联络线变量对齐；(2)训练源域门控低秩适配器（gated LoRA），仅更新约1%的参数且无需目标市场标签；(3)门控机制根据备用紧张度和运行状态信号缩放冻结的源适配器；(4)采用leave-one-market-out协议评估跨市场迁移能力。

Result: 在四个中国省级日前现货市场实验中，所提框架相比市场信息感知的零样本Chronos-2平均MAE/RMSE降低6.24%/7.99%，相比原始Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%。消融实验表明，学习全局标量或随机门初始化无法复现增益，且相比Source-LoRA的额外改进有限。

Conclusion: 市场结构化输入和状态依赖的门控LoRA为数据稀缺的电力市场提供了一条实用的迁移路径。但门控机制的额外增益相对有限，提示该方向仍存在进一步优化的空间。

Abstract: Electricity price forecasting is crucial for market participants but remains difficult because prices are volatile, market-specific, and closely tied to anticipated system conditions. Existing supervised methods depend largely on market-specific historical data, limiting their use in newly established or data-scarce markets. This paper proposes a market-information-aware adaptation framework that transfers the Chronos-2 time-series foundation model to day-ahead electricity price forecasting. It first constructs a multi-source market information (MSMI) interface aligning 7-day price context with pre-clearing supply--demand, reserve, maintenance, generator-capacity, and intertie variables, and then trains a source-domain gated low-rank adapter (LoRA), updating about $1\%$ of model parameters without target-market labels. The gate scales the frozen source adapter according to reserve-tightness and operating-state signals. A leave-one-market-out protocol is adopted for evaluating cross-market transferability. Experiments on four Chinese provincial day-ahead spot markets show that the proposed framework reduces the average MAE/RMSE by $6.24\%/7.99\%$ relative to market-information-aware zero-shot Chronos-2 and by $3.05\%/3.52\%$ relative to vanilla Source-LoRA. Experiments show that the gain is not reproduced by a learned global scalar or by random gate initialization, while the additional improvement over Source-LoRA is limited. These results suggest that market-structured inputs and state-dependent gated LoRA can provide a practical transfer path for data-scarce electricity markets.

</details>


### [33] [Lifecycle-Optimal Tokenization: Vocabulary Size as a Deployment-Regime-Dependent Infrastructure Parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11361)
*Rima Mittal,Ankit Gubrani,Satyanarayana Kakollu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM分词器词表大小并非固定常数，而是部署场景的函数。通过引入生命周期总成本模型C_lifecycle(V)=C_train(V)+λ·C_infer(V,B)，在两种GPU（A10G和A100）上实验证明：推理最优词表随批量大小从32k（B=1）漂移到524k（B≥64），而质量在最优区间内几乎不变（<2% BPB差异）。提供实际容量规划指导：端侧部署用V≈32k，数据中心部署用V≈131-262k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词表大小是LLM基础设施的基础设计选择，但通常基于惯例而非部署分析在训练时固定。作者希望证明成本最优词表不是常数而是服务场景的函数，为容量规划提供可操作的指导。

Method: 形式化总部署成本为训练成本和推理成本之和（含推理量和批量大小两个参数）；在两种不同计算/内存边界比（ridge）的GPU家族（A10G和A100）上，针对1.3-2.3B规模模型进行受控实验，覆盖从内存受限到计算受限的不同服务区间，测量BPB质量指标和总成本。

Result: (1) 推理最优词表随批量变化16倍：B=1时为32k，B≥64时达524k；(2) 1.3-2.3B规模下质量最优词表为65k，证实词表偏好依赖模型规模；(3) 生命周期最优词表与训练最优相差可达16倍；(4) 在最优区间内质量近似不变（BPB差异<2%），词表可作为纯系统优化变量。

Conclusion: 词表大小应被视为部署感知的系统优化参数而非固定的训练超参数。给出明确建议：B=1的端侧部署用V≈32k；批量≥64且推理量≥10的数据中心部署用V≈131-262k。在实测范围内词表选择不影响质量，因此可以纯粹从系统成本角度进行优化。

Abstract: Tokenizer vocabulary size is a foundational design choice in large language model (LLM) infrastructure, yet it is typically fixed at training time based on convention rather than deployment analysis. We show that the cost-optimal vocabulary is not a constant but a function of the serving regime. We formalize total deployment cost as $C_{lifecycle}(V) = C_{train}(V) + λ\cdot C_{infer}(V, B)$, where $λ$ is inference volume and $B$ is the serving batch size. Through controlled experiments on two GPU families spanning the memory-bound to compute-bound regimes (A10G, ridge $\approx$ 117 FLOP/byte; A100, ridge $\approx$ 183 FLOP/byte), we demonstrate: (1) the inference-optimal vocabulary shifts 16x with serving batch, from 32k at $B=1$ to 524k at $B=64+$, driven by amortization of the $V \times d$ unembedding matrix read; (2) at 1.3-2.3B model scale, quality (bits per byte, BPB) is optimized at $V=65$k, confirming scale-dependent vocabulary preference; (3) the lifecycle-optimal vocabulary diverges from training-optimal by up to 16x for production deployments. Quality is approximately invariant across the optimal range ($<$2% BPB spread), making vocabulary a pure systems optimization with no quality penalty in the measured range. Our results provide actionable capacity planning guidance: on-device deployments ($B=1$) should use $V \approx 32$k; datacenter serving ($B \geq 64$, $λ\geq 10$) should use $V \approx 131$-262k.

</details>


### [34] [PAIR: Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting for Adaptive Rollout Allocation in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11368)
*Pixel Nomand,Elena Voss,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAIR（Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting）方法，解决RLVR中可验证奖励强化学习预算分配中的统计偏差问题。研究发现未裁剪的留一法组相对分数梯度不是独立点贡献之和，而是关于轨迹对的二阶U统计量。PAIR将短轨迹前缀视为顶点、对梯度项视为边构建对比图，通过前缀预测器估计正确性和剩余token成本，用凸优化选择续写概率，并对每条边按联合包含概率逆加权，实现对完整候选对梯度的设计无偏估计。在Qwen3-1.7B/4B上相比最强逐点分配器平均准确率提升+1.2/+1.4，同时比完整组GRPO节省51%/52%的生成token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR的大部分计算开销用于生成大量长推理轨迹。现有的预算分配器根据逐点难度或效用概念为提示、轨迹或token分配预算，但研究者发现这些方法存在统计错配：未裁剪的留一法组相对分数梯度本质上是关于轨迹对的二阶U统计量，而非独立点贡献之和。完成一条轨迹会揭示与所有其他已完成任务之间的对比信息，且自适应端点选择会改变哪些对项可被观测，这需要新的统计框架来处理。

Method: PAIR框架：(1) 将短轨迹前缀视为顶点、配对梯度项视为对比图的边；(2) 使用前缀预测器估计正确性和剩余token成本；(3) 通过凸优化在期望后缀token预算下选择正的续写概率；(4) 对每个由已完成顶点诱导的边按其对数的联合包含概率进行逆加权。在条件独立on-policy轨迹和未裁剪、未标准化的目标下，该估计器对完整候选对梯度是设计无偏的。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B/4B上的计算匹配RLVR实验中，PAIR相比最强逐点分配器平均准确率提升+1.2和+1.4，同时比完整组GRPO节省51%和52%的生成token。冻结样本总体估计审计确认：未加权的自适应选择存在偏差，而配对包含校正能在匹配后缀成本下恢复完整对目标。

Conclusion: PAIR通过揭示RLVR梯度估计的配对结构并引入设计无偏的包含重加权机制，在显著减少计算开销的同时提升了训练效果，为可验证奖励强化学习中的预算分配提供了一种统计意义上更严谨的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) spends most of its compute generating groups of long reasoning trajectories. Recent allocators reduce this cost by assigning budgets to prompts, rollouts, or tokens according to a pointwise notion of difficulty or utility. We identify a statistical mismatch: the unclipped leave-one-out group-relative score gradient is not a sum of independent point contributions, but a second-order U-statistic over pairs of rollouts. Completing one rollout therefore reveals contrast with every other completed rollout, and adaptive endpoint selection changes which pair terms are observable. We introduce PAIR (Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting), which treats short rollout prefixes as vertices and pair-gradient terms as edges of a contrast graph. A prefix-only predictor estimates correctness and remaining token cost; a convex design chooses positive continuation probabilities under an expected suffix-token budget; and each edge induced by completed vertices is inverse-weighted by its logged joint inclusion probability. Under conditionally independent on-policy rollouts and an unclipped, unstandardized objective, the resulting estimator is design-unbiased for the complete candidate-pair gradient. Across compute-matched RLVR runs on Qwen3-1.7B/4B, PAIR improves average accuracy by +1.2 and +1.4 over the strongest pointwise allocator while using 51% and 52% fewer generated tokens than full-group GRPO. A frozen-population estimator audit confirms that unweighted adaptive selection is biased, whereas pair-inclusion correction recovers the complete-pair target at matched suffix cost.

</details>

*Rajko Turudija,Dušan Stojiljković,Milan Zdravković,Marko Ignjatović*
