A Wasserstein relaxation of the drift term, valid under support mismatch, yields a depth-dependent drift--sensitivity trade-off whose minimizer identifies which interior layer to stabilize. An SGLD instantiation of the optimization term reduces it to a trajectory-level log-determinant budget, exposing a curvature-aware gradient-alignment statistic that serves as an online diagnostic of task-wise forgetting. Controlled and benchmark experiments confirm the predicted memory scaling, the interior funnel, and the alignment signal's link to forgetting.

</details>


### [70] [LEMUR: Latent Entropy-aware Multimodal Unlearning via Visual-anchored Reasoning Redirection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11691)
*Xinhao Zhong,Yuxia Qiao,Junhao Li,Hao Fang,Yi Sun,Bin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习后训练的多模态大推理模型（MLRMs）在推理链中会泄露已被遗忘的敏感信息，并提出LEMUR——一种基于熵动力学信号的、完全免训练的推理时遗忘框架，通过熵调控的视觉锚点潜变量注入来抑制推理轨迹中的敏感内容泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有遗忘方法能成功从最终答案中移除敏感事实，但RL训练的推理模型在探索式思维链中仍可能复现这些敏感信息，构成一种现有方法无法解决的隐私漏洞。这一漏洞在原生RL训练的MLRMs中尤为显著。

Method: LEMUR框架基于两个关键观察：(1) RL诱导的探索性推理使敏感内容呈现独特的token级熵特征，而基础模型中该特征基本不存在；(2) 利用熵动力学作为控制信号，识别敏感推理开始和净化应该停止的时间区间。在此区间内，通过熵调控的视觉锚点潜变量注入重定向推理轨迹，用基于输入图像重新扎根的、经过净化的概率加权嵌入替换已提交的token。

Result: 在多种MLRMs上的实验表明，LEMUR在抑制推理轨迹和答案层面的泄露方面持续优于现有遗忘方法，同时更好地保留了非敏感内容的实用性和输出流畅度。

Conclusion: RL诱导的熵动力学为隐私泄露提供了独特的信号，利用该信号可以实现对推理能力多模态模型的有效免训练遗忘，为RL训练模型的隐私保护开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training equips multimodal large reasoning models (MLRMs) with exploratory chains of thought (CoT), substantially improving visual reasoning. However, we find that this capability introduces a distinct privacy vulnerability: even when a sensitive fact is successfully unlearned from the final answer, the model may still reproduce it in its reasoning trace. This leakage is substantially more pronounced in natively RL-trained MLRMs than in their non -reasoning base models, revealing a privacy risk that existing unlearning methods are not designed to address. We show that RL-induced exploration leaves sensitive content with a distinctive token-level entropy signature that is largely absent from base models. Based on this observation, we propose LEMUR, a fully training-free, inference-time unlearning framework for natively RL-trained multimodal models. LEMUR uses entropy dynamics as a control signal to identify when sensitive reasoning begins and when sanitization should stop. During this interval, it redirects the reasoning trajectory through entropy-modulated visual-anchor latent injection, replacing committed tokens with sanitized, probability-weighted embeddings re-grounded in the input image. Across diverse MLRMs, LEMUR consistently outperforms existing unlearning met hods in suppressing both reasoning-trace and answer leakage, while better preserving non-sensitive utility and output fluency. These results demonstrate that RL-induced entropy dynamics provide a distinctive signal for privacy leakage and that exploiting this signal enables effective training-free unlearning for reasoning-capable multimodal models.

</details>


### [71] [REOPD: Reliability-Adaptive Reward Extrapolation for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11698)
*Yang Sun,Lichao Ma,Houyuan Qin,Yuxin Liu,Hanyang Lu,Yao Zhu,Pinlong Cai,Guohang Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REOPD提出了一种可靠性自适应的奖励外推框架，通过token级兼容性权重和batch级自适应预算的组合，为每个token分配动态系数λ，在保持教师对齐的同时沿可靠方向选择性外推，无需额外的验证器或奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励外推方法（如ExOPD）对所有token使用单一全局系数λ，可能驱动学生拟合隐式奖励中的极端峰值导致奖励破解和不稳定训练，且最优λ在不同领域间差异大，需要昂贵的超参数搜索。

Method: REOPD将token级兼容性权重与batch级自适应预算相结合，得到token级系数λ_{b,t}=1+γ_b q_t，其中γ_b为batch级自适应预算，q_t为token级可靠性权重，从而在保持教师对齐的同时沿可靠的教师参考方向进行选择性外推。该方法不需要验证器、奖励模型、价值模型或额外rollout。

Result: REOPD在单教师数学任务和多教师设置的两个领域上均优于G-OPD，在单教师代码任务上与G-OPD持平，展示了跨领域和教师配置的有效细粒度可靠性适应能力。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度的token级可靠性自适应，REOPD能够避免单一全局系数带来的奖励破解问题，同时在不同领域和教师配置下实现更优或相当的性能，是一种更稳健的OPD奖励外推框架。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own trajectories under dense token-level supervision from a teacher. Reward-extrapolation methods such as ExOPD amplify the teacher-reference log-likelihood ratio to move beyond direct imitation, but apply a single global coefficient $λ$ to every token. This can drive the student to fit extreme peaks in the implicit reward, causing reward hacking and unstable training, and the optimal $λ$ varies across domains, requiring costly sweeps. We propose REOPD, a reliability-adaptive reward extrapolation framework for OPD. REOPD combines a token-level compatibility weight with a batch-level adaptive budget, yielding a token-wise coefficient $λ_{b,t}=1+γ_b q_t$ that preserves teacher alignment while selectively extrapolating along reliable teacher-reference directions. It requires no verifier, reward model, value model, or extra rollout beyond standard OPD. REOPD outperforms G-OPD on single-teacher mathematics and on both domains in the multi-teacher setting, while matching G-OPD on single-teacher code, demonstrating effective fine-grained reliability adaptation across domains and teacher configurations.

</details>


### [72] [Consolidator: Learning Persistent Routed Memory Across Context Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11701)
*Sungwoo Goo,Hwi-yeol Yun,Sangkeun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Phasor Memory Network（PMNet）中的记忆巩固机制。通过引入仅12.35K参数的Consolidator算子，将短期记忆（STM）转化为长期记忆（LTM），并在清除KV缓存和STM后，验证持久化与可访问性（routing）之间的区别。在双段modulo-10映射任务中，直接LTM路由将更新映射的召回率从44.38%提升至87.02%（+42.64个百分点），证明巩固后的LTM不仅作为可检索内容，还作为访问状态塑造后续槽位选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观念认为将短期记忆复制到慢速存储（长期记忆）即可保留跨上下文边界的状态，但持久化本身并不能保证保留的状态会影响后续记忆访问。作者希望区分"记忆被保留"与"记忆影响访问"这两个概念，并验证学习式巩固操作能否同时实现内容的可检索性和对后续路由的引导作用。

Method: 在PMNet中使用Consolidator——一个共享的槽位局部算子，将路由后的STM变换后累积到LTM中，不重放源token。每次巩固后清除KV缓存和STM，但保留LTM供读取，并将LTM反馈到分层路由器中，从而调控后续输入访问哪些显式记忆槽位。在双段modulo-10映射任务中评估：第二段在同一记忆地址更新映射，第二次巩固和重置后，通过held-out查询从LTM恢复更新后的映射。骨干网络和记忆接口冻结，仅训练12.35K个Consolidator参数（占总模型29.95M的0.041%）。

Result: 在五次配对运行中（来自相同STM预训练检查点），直接LTM路由将更新映射召回率从44.38±1.94%提升至87.02±1.76%（+42.64±1.10个百分点）；两种条件下即时STM召回率均保持89.90%。学习式巩固比强制恒等累积在无路由时高21.40±1.91个百分点，有路由时高68.70±1.76个百分点。

Conclusion: 在该任务上，巩固后的LTM既作为可检索的内容，又作为访问状态（access state）塑造后续槽位选择。学习式巩固（而非简单恒等复制）对于实现这一双重功能至关重要，尤其是通过路由机制时效果最为显著。

Abstract: Copying short-term memory (STM) into a slower store can preserve state across a context boundary, but persistence alone does not ensure that the retained state influences subsequent memory access. We test this distinction in a Phasor Memory Network (PMNet) using Consolidator, a shared slot-local operator that transforms routed STM before accumulating it into long-term memory (LTM), without replaying the source tokens. After each consolidation, the KV cache and STM are cleared. The retained LTM can still be read and is also fed into the hierarchical router, thereby conditioning which explicit-memory slots subsequent inputs access. We evaluate this mechanism on a two-segment modulo-10 mapping task in which the second segment updates the mapping at the same memory address. Following a second consolidation and reset, a held-out query must recover the updated mapping from LTM. The backbone and memory interface are frozen, leaving only 12.35K Consolidator parameters trainable (0.041\% of a 29.95M model). Across five paired runs from the same STM-pretraining checkpoint, direct LTM routing raises updated-mapping recall from $44.38\pm1.94\%$ to $87.02\pm1.76\%$ ($+42.64\pm1.10$ percentage points), while immediate STM recall remains 89.90\% in both conditions; both train separate Consolidators and retain the same LTM read paths. Learned consolidation outperforms forced identity accumulation by $21.40\pm1.91$ percentage points without routing and $68.70\pm1.76$ with routing. Thus, on this task, consolidated LTM serves as both retrievable content and an access state that shapes subsequent slot selection.

</details>


### [73] [Robust and Efficient Noisy-Label Time-Series Classification via Dynamic Time Warping Based Granular Ball Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11704)
*Ziqiang Li,Yun Liu,Gouhei Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DTW-GBC（基于动态时间规整的粒度球计算）方法，通过将时间上相似的训练样本组织成粒度球并在粒度层进行分类，同时缓解标签噪声导致的性能下降并大幅减少推理时的DTW计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于DTW的最近邻分类器在时间序列分类中表现优异，但存在两个关键缺陷：对错误标注的训练样本（标签噪声）非常敏感，且在推理阶段需要大量DTW距离计算，计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出DTW-GBC框架，将时间上相似的训练样本聚合为粒度球，在粒度级别执行分类而非逐样本分类；并开发了两种粒度球构建策略来生成DTW-GBC的两个变体。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上引入对称标签噪声的实验表明，DTW-GBC的两个变体普遍缓解了标签噪声引起的性能退化，同时推理阶段所需的比较次数显著少于DTW-based 1-NN。

Conclusion: DTW-GBC在分类鲁棒性和推理效率之间取得了有利的平衡，为时间序列分类提供了一种既抗标签噪声又高效的新方法。

Abstract: Dynamic Time Warping (DTW)-based Nearest-Neighbor (NN) classifiers are effective for time-series classification but are vulnerable to mislabeled training samples and require numerous DTW computations during inference. We propose DTW-based Granular Ball Computing (DTW-GBC), which organizes temporally similar training samples into granular balls and performs classification at the granule level. We further develop two granular-ball construction strategies for DTW-GBC. Experiments on four benchmark datasets with symmetric label noise show that the two DTW-GBC variants generally mitigate the performance degradation caused by label noise while requiring substantially fewer comparisons than DTW-based 1-NN during inference. These findings suggest that DTW-GBC provides a favorable balance between classification robustness and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [74] [High-dimensional Multi-objective Bayesian Optimization with Learned Variable Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11713)
*Hongyan Wang,Jiayu Huang,Haotian Zheng,Xin Gao,Chi Ding,Ying Liu,Xia Wang,Qing Xu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ViaMOBO，一个基于决策变量交互分析的高维多目标贝叶斯优化通用框架。该框架通过变量交互分析模型判断决策空间是否可以完全或部分分解，然后在子空间中进行局部贝叶斯优化，从而应对高维昂贵黑盒多目标优化问题中传统MOBO方法的指数级采样复杂度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOBO方法在低维决策空间中表现良好，但在高维空间中因指数级采样复杂度而性能受限。缺乏能够有效处理高维昂贵多目标问题的通用框架。

Method: 利用变量交互分析模型判断目标函数的可分离性（可分离、部分可分离或不可分离），据此将决策空间完全或部分划分为多个子空间，并在每个子空间中独立执行局部贝叶斯优化。该分析不需要强假设，基于决策变量间的潜在独立或相互依赖关系进行推导。

Result: 在合成基准和实际基准上与最先进的MOBO方法进行了对比实验，结果表明ViaMOBO在高维昂贵多目标问题的Pareto前沿逼近方面优于其他MOBO基线方法。

Conclusion: ViaMOBO通过变量交互分析实现决策空间分解，有效解决了高维MOBO的采样复杂度瓶颈，为高维昂贵黑盒多目标优化提供了一种通用的高效方案。

Abstract: Multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) is effective in identifying the Pareto fronts for expensive black-box problems. However, most current MOBO approaches are limited to low-dimensional decision space due to its exponential sampling complexity. This paper presents decision variable interaction analysis-based MOBO, ViaMOBO, a generic framework for expensive multi-objective problems with high-dimensional decision space. The key idea of ViaMOBO is that it utilizes a variable interaction analysis model to determine whether the decision space can be completely or partially divided, and then performs local Bayesian optimization in the divided decision subspaces. Through the variable analysis model, it can be derived whether the objectives in black-box problems are separable, partially separable, or non-separable based on the potential independent or interdependent relationships among decision variables without any strong assumptions. We compare ViaMOBO with the state-of-the-art MOBO methods on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks. The experimental results demonstrate that ViaMOBO outperforms other related MOBO baselines in approximating the Pareto front of high-dimensional expensive multi-objective problems.

</details>


### [75] [Chain-of-Thought Shows the Path to a Tree: Realizing Branching Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11716)
*Debanjan Dutta,Anish Chakrabarty,Swagatam Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为有界深度Transformer给出了链式思维（CoT）的具体实现：用至多两层的硬注意力解码器实现深度优先搜索（DFS）和Dijkstra算法（涵盖广度优先搜索），并复用这些解码器在O(n)步内计算树的Strahler数和宽度，为非平凡线性步CoT层级提供了构造性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究将CoT步数与电路复杂度类联系起来刻画了有界深度Transformer的表达上限，但缺乏具体的、显式的深度有界构造实例，以及这些刻画所预设的遍历过程。本文旨在填补这一空白，为分支复杂度提供具体的CoT实现。

Method: 文章给出了DFS和Dijkstra算法的CoT实现，二者均使用至多两层硬注意力解码器。DFS解码器被复用以在2n-1步内用四层计算n顶点树的Strahler数；Dijkstra解码器被复用以在n-1步内用三层计算树的宽度。此外利用有序树与Dyck路径的经典双射（由DFS构造自然实现），在路径表示上给出了两种度量的独立构造。

Result: 给出了显式的深度有界CoT构造：DFS和Dijkstra算法分别由两层硬注意力解码器实现；Strahler数计算可在2n-1步内以四层完成，树宽度计算可在n-1步内以三层完成。构造无需层归一化或位置编码，且支持任意n叉树。

Conclusion: 由于以项形式给定的二叉树的Strahler数计算是NC^1完全的，本文的构造是对CoT分层中线性步数机制的一个非平凡见证，说明线性步CoT足以解决某些NC^1难的问题。

Abstract: Chain of Thought (CoT) lifts the expressive ceiling of bounded-depth Transformers, with characterizations tying the number of CoT steps to circuit complexity classes. What remains largely missing are concrete instantiations with explicit, depth-bounded constructions, and the traversal procedures such characterizations presuppose. We close this gap for branching complexity. We give CoT realizations of depth-first search (DFS) and of Dijkstra algorithm, the latter subsuming breadth-first search, by unique hard-attention decoders of at most two layers, and use them as a shared computational substrate: reusing the DFS decoder yields the Strahler number of an $n$-vertex tree in $2n-1$ steps with four layers, and reusing the Dijkstra decoder yields its width in $n-1$ steps with three. Since computing the Strahler number of a binary tree given as a term is \textsf{NC\textsuperscript{1}}-complete, and our constructions handle arbitrary $n$-ary trees without layer normalization or positional encodings, this is a non-trivial witness for the linear-step regime of the CoT hierarchy. Exploiting the classical bijection between ordered trees and Dyck paths, itself realized by our DFS construction, which emits the path as it traverses, we give independent constructions for both measures on the path representation.

</details>


### [76] [Epiplexity Guided Data Selection and Generation for Out-of-Distribution Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11746)
*Ellen Su,Andres Potapczynski,Shikai Qiu,Edward Hughes,Andrew Gordon Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将Epiplexity（描述计算受限学习器可从数据中提取的结构信息量）用作在线训练信号，分别通过拟合缩放律预测各域epiplexity增益以自适应调整采样权重（数据选择），以及通过REINFORCE策略梯度引导生成器最大化epiplexity（合成数据生成）。实验表明更高的epiplexity能提升零样本和微调任务的下游性能，支持了结构信息丰富的数据有助于跨域迁移的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代系统需要在训练时未指定的新任务上迁移，但什么样的数据有助于这些非预期的泛化尚不清楚。作者假设包含更多结构信息的数据可能包含可复用的共享电路和子程序，从而在更广泛的下游场景中发挥作用。Epiplexity作为衡量结构信息的指标，为推理数据与迁移能力之间的关系提供了机制。

Method: 1) 数据选择：对自然数据域的损失曲线拟合缩放定律，预测epiplexity增益与训练token的函数关系，并据此自适应确定训练中各域的采样权重。2) 合成数据生成：将生成器的奖励定义为学习器在先前生成数据缓冲区上的epiplexity变化，使用REINFORCE策略梯度引导生成器朝向最大化epiplexity的分布。

Result: 在两种方案下，更高的epiplexity均预测了更好的零样本和微调任务下游性能，验证了结构信息丰富的数据能产生跨域可迁移表征的假设。

Conclusion: epiplexity可作为有效的在线训练信号用于数据选择与合成数据生成，结构信息丰富的数据有助于提升跨域迁移性能，支持了"结构信息可复用"的核心假设。

Abstract: Modern systems are increasingly expected to transfer across tasks not specified during training. What data facilitates generalization in these new, unanticipated settings? One hypothesis is that data with more structural information could contain shared circuits and subprograms that could be recycled in a wider array of downstream settings. Epiplexity, a recently proposed measure of the structural information a compute-bounded learner can extract from data, provides a mechanism to reason about this relationship. In this paper, we show how to operationalize epiplexity as an online training signal for data selection and synthetic data generation. For selection, we fit scaling laws to the training loss curves of natural data domains to predict the expected epiplexity gain as a function of training tokens, and use this signal to adaptively determine the sampling weights over domains during training. For synthetic data generation, we define a generator's reward as the change in learner epiplexity over a buffer of previously generated data and use REINFORCE policy gradients to guide the generator toward an epiplexity-maximizing distribution. In both cases, higher epiplexity predicts improved downstream performance on zero-shot and fine-tuning based tasks, supporting the hypothesis that data rich in structural information yield representations that transfer across domains.

</details>


### [77] [MOON: Multi-Objective OrthoNormalized Updates for Multitask Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11749)
*Shiji Zhou,Kunlin Lyu,Lei Zhang,Ruodong Wang,Yifan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOON方法，在谱-核范数几何下进行多目标优化中的梯度操控，利用正交归一化后的梯度进行参数更新，以克服传统欧几里得空间中梯度操控忽略参数矩阵结构的问题。理论上证明了在平滑非凸目标下Pareto平稳性测度的收敛率（确定性O(T^{-1/2})，随机梯度O(T^{-1/4})），实验表明MOON在多个基准上提升优化效率和多任务最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOO方法在多任务学习中通常将模型参数展平为向量，在欧几里得几何下进行梯度操控，忽略了Transformer等现代架构中参数的矩阵结构。作者指出欧几里得空间中的梯度操控在矩阵几何下并非最陡下降方向，可能限制优化效率。

Method: MOON基于矩阵值参数的陡峭下降理论，在谱-核范数几何下执行梯度操控，并使用正交归一化后的操控梯度进行参数更新。具体而言，该方法将梯度操控从传统的向量空间拓展到矩阵流形，利用谱范数和核范数的几何性质获得更符合矩阵结构的下降方向。

Result: 理论上，对于平滑非凸目标，MOON在确定性设置下达到O(T^{-1/2})的Pareto平稳性收敛率，在随机梯度设置下达到O(T^{-1/4})。实验上，在多个基准任务中MOON一致提升了优化效率和最终多任务性能，代码已开源。

Conclusion: MOON通过将多目标梯度操控从欧几里得几何迁移至谱-核范数矩阵几何，更好地利用参数矩阵结构，实现了更优的优化效率和多任务性能，为MOO在多任务学习中的应用提供了新的理论视角和实用方法。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has demonstrated significant success in multi-task learning by mitigating task conflicts through gradient manipulation. However, most existing methods flatten model parameters into vectors and perform gradient manipulation under Euclidean geometry, thereby overlooking the matrix structure prevalent in modern architectures such as Transformers. In this paper, we show that gradient manipulation in Euclidean space does not generally yield the steepest descent direction under matrix geometry, potentially limiting optimization efficiency. Drawing from the theory of steepest descent for matrix-valued parameters, we propose MOON (Multi-Objective OrthoNormalized Updates), which performs gradient manipulation under spectral--nuclear norm geometry and uses the orthonormalized manipulated gradient for parameter updates. Theoretically, for smooth non-convex objectives, we establish convergence of the averaged Pareto-stationarity measure at rates of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ in the deterministic setting and $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ under stochastic gradients. Empirical results across various benchmarks show that MOON consistently improves both optimization efficiency and final multi-task performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/KunlinLyu/MOON.

</details>


### [78] [TradingMoE: Routing the Right Experts in Evolving Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11785)
*Chang Zhou,Xingtong Yu,Minbin Huang,Zhennan Wu,Yuan Fang,Hong Cheng,Xinming Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TradingMoE，一种面向交易的稀疏混合专家模型，通过Query-Key路由和稀疏专家选择更新机制，使冻结的稠密大语言模型能适应不同资产和市场条件下专家效用的动态变化，在股票和加密货币市场的22个基线对比中，累计收益分别提升30.89%和30.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的交易系统存在两个关键缺陷：一是协调人类定义的外部专家或采用传统内部MoE路由器，无法直接评估单个专家对交易决策的实际贡献；二是路由器的训练信号缺乏对"非活跃专家"随市场条件变化可能变得更具适用性的直接反馈。研究发现原生路由器评分无法准确反映专家对交易决策的改善程度，且token级别的专家实用性存在紧凑的低维结构，这为设计更有效的路由机制提供了契机。

Method: TradingMoE由三部分组成：1) 在冻结的稠密LLM上添加轻量级残差专家；2) Query-Key路由器，将每个token在当前市场背景下所需专业知识表示为一个低维查询向量，并与可学习的专家密钥进行匹配；3) 稀疏专家选择更新机制，训练时对少量非活跃专家进行采样，评估其是否应替换当前Top-k路由中最弱的专家，从而使路由器能随市场条件变化更新专家选择，同时保持稀疏计算效率。

Result: 在股票和加密货币市场上与22个基线对比，TradingMoE将累计收益分别提升了30.89%和30.7%；滚动纸面交易实验表明该优势在前向-only部署场景下依然保持。

Conclusion: TradingMoE通过捕捉专家实用性的低维结构并引入动态专家选择更新机制，有效解决了LLM直接交易中专家效用随市场和资产变化的问题，在真实交易场景中验证了其鲁棒性和有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for financial analysis and trading, but direct trading remains challenging because the predictive capabilities required can vary across assets, decision fields, and market conditions. Existing LLM-based trading systems either coordinate human-defined external experts or adopt conventional internal Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routers that do not directly evaluate how individual experts contribute to trading decisions. Moreover, these routers receive no direct signal indicating when an inactive expert has become more suitable as market conditions change. We find that native router scores poorly reflect how much individual experts improve trading decisions, frequently leaving better alternatives unselected. We further reveal that token-specific expert usefulness exhibits a compact low-dimensional structure. Based on these findings, we propose TradingMoE, a trading-oriented sparse MoE that augments a frozen dense LLM with lightweight residual experts. We introduce a Query-Key router that represents the expertise required by each token under the current market context as a low-dimensional query and matches it with learnable expert keys. We further propose a sparse expert selection update mechanism that samples a few inactive experts during training and estimates whether they should replace the weakest expert in the current Top-k route. This mechanism enables the router to update expert selection as market conditions change while preserving sparse computation. Experiments against 22 baselines on stock and cryptocurrency markets show that TradingMoE improves cumulative return over the best-performing baselines by 30.89% and 30.7%, respectively. Rolling paper-trading experiments further demonstrate that its advantage persists under forward-only deployment.

</details>


### [79] [High-Order Liquid Evidence Encoding for Gradual GNSS Spoofing Detection in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11790)
*Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Fatima Ashraf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于因果高阶液态证据框架的GNSS欺骗检测方法，通过构建物理引导的GNSS-运动不一致残差及其一阶、二阶离散变化的证据流，利用自适应液体编码器进行层次化耦合，在真实AV-GPS数据集上取得了最优的F1分数（0.9535和0.9777）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS欺骗攻击，特别是连续且细微的攻击，难以检测，因为单个观测可能看似合理，但GNSS隐含位移与车载运动之间的不一致会逐渐积累。现有方法依赖静态车辆行为特征或单一残差信号，未显式建模这种演化过程。

Method: 提出因果高阶液态证据框架：首先构建物理引导的GNSS-运动不一致残差（比较GNSS隐含位移与车载运动导出位移）；然后为残差水平及其一阶和二阶离散变化分别形成证据流，根据证据阶次选择相关上下文线索；每条流由独立的自适应液体编码器处理，所得时间状态被层次化耦合，在窗口端点仅利用当前和过去的观测预测欺骗。

Result: 在真实世界AV-GPS数据集的三个子集上进行实验，所提方法在数据集1和数据集3上取得了所评估时序模型中最高的F1分数，分别为0.9535和0.9777。在数据集3上，该方法在四个采样步内检测到两个标记的正常到攻击转换。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模GNSS-运动不一致的演化（包括高阶变化），所提出的因果液态证据框架能够更有效地检测连续且细微的GNSS欺骗攻击，优于现有时序模型，且代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: Accurate Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS)-based localization is essential for safe and reliable autonomous driving. However, spoofing attacks can manipulate vehicle position estimates. Continuous and subtle attacks are particularly difficult to detect because individual GNSS observations may remain plausible while the inconsistency between GNSS-implied displacement and onboard vehicle motion gradually increases. Existing methods often rely on static vehicle-behavior features or a single residual signal and do not explicitly model this evolution. To address this problem, we propose a causal high-order liquid evidence framework for GNSS spoofing detection. The method first constructs a physics-guided GNSS--motion inconsistency residual by comparing GNSS-implied displacement with onboard-motion-derived displacement. It then forms separate evidence streams for the residual level and its first- and second-order discrete variations, with relevant contextual cues selected according to the evidence order. Each stream is processed by a separate adaptive liquid encoder, and the resulting temporal states are hierarchically coupled to predict spoofing at the window endpoint using only current and past observations. Experiments on three subsets of the real-world AV-GPS dataset show that the proposed method achieves the highest F1-scores among the evaluated temporal models on Dataset~1 and Dataset~3, reaching 0.9535 and 0.9777, respectively. On Dataset~3, it detects both labeled normal-to-attack transitions within four sampling steps. Code and datasets are publicly available at: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/GNSS_Spoofing.git.

</details>


### [80] [Orientation, not magnitude: the causal structure of task-vector interference in merged language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11797)
*Chencheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Model merging by task arithmetic works until it doesn't, and the field diagnoses why with magnitudes: layerwise representation bias, deviations from cross-task linearity, parameter overlap. Tracking the exact layerwise cross-term of merged LLMs through a factorial ledger and intervening on it directly, we find magnitude insufficient - and inconsistent across model families - as a diagnostic axis. An exact decomposition of the layerwise flux shows it is dominated by amplifying transport of the existing cross-term (~65-70% in both families, gain >1 per late block), and erasing the term is undone by propagation - rebuilt to 99% of its norm at cosine 0.99 - unless applied near the output; a basin test with six starting displacements establishes the carried direction as an attractor of the forward pass. That direction is causally load-bearing: erasure along it removes expressed interference dose-dependently and saturates at exact erasure, while norm-matched wrong-direction controls fail or backfire. Instruction wrappers gate the effect: the same erasure finds 13x less relative interference to remove under a wrapper that internally amplifies the cross-term, because the wrapper drowns the interaction in a template-pinned main effect rather than shrinking it - a structure that replicates across further instruction templates but not under a length-matched control. Magnitude, by contrast, is at best a coarse correlate, and the striking +-15% "universality" of naive bfloat16 generation turns out to be quantization roughness. Task pairs whose local cross-term generation differs by at most 1.9x differ by 14x-337x in causally removable interference. All 46 predictions were preregistered and frozen before their data; falsifications, including of our own headline expectations and of behavioral recovery under a validated continuous endpoint, are reported as such.

</details>


### [81] [JAPE: Joint Anomaly Prediction and Intrinsic Explanation in Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11801)
*Yian Wei,Yuanyuan Yao,Lu Chen,Xiangmin Zhou,Tianyi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAPE是一个面向多变量时间序列异常预测的联合异常预测与解释框架。它首次将异常预测从数值偏差建模提升到依赖结构建模，通过解耦时空表示、双视角告警机制和原生预测性解释，在5个真实基准数据集上显著提升了异常预测的F1、AUC-PR和可解释性指标（MRR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要将异常表征为未来数值的偏差，存在两大局限：(1) 对微弱异常前兆引发的细微依赖关系变化敏感度不足；(2) 告警时无法提供原生的变量级解释。作者希望通过建模演化中的依赖结构来弥补这两个空白。

Method: JAPE提出了三个核心技术组件：(i) 解耦时空表示（DSTR）骨干网络，将时间与空间建模解耦，并通过可学习的滞后聚合捕获滞后感知的依赖关系，在数值偏差显现前感知结构前兆；(ii) 双视角告警机制，融合数值预测与演化依赖图进行逐点异常预测，即便数值偏差微弱也能捕获结构证据；(iii) 原生预测性解释（NPE），直接复用预测的依赖图，无需额外模型或训练即可按结构偏差对变量排序。

Result: 在5个真实世界基准数据集、3种预测时域上，JAPE相较于基线平均提升F1达19.7%，AUC-PR提升41.3%，可解释性MRR提升26.6%。

Conclusion: JAPE通过将异常预测问题从数值偏差建模重构为依赖结构建模，同时解决了微弱前兆感知和原生变量级解释两个难题，在预测性能和可解释性上均取得了显著提升，为多变量时间序列异常预测提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series anomaly prediction aims to identify whether and when anomalies will occur over a future horizon from historical observations. Existing methods primarily characterize anomalies as deviations in future numerical values, which may overlook subtle dependency changes induced by weak anomaly precursors and provide no native variable-level explanation together with the alert. To bridge these gaps, we propose JAPE, a Joint Anomaly Prediction and Explanation framework that lifts anomaly prediction from numerical-deviation modeling to dependency-structure modeling. JAPE is the first anomaly prediction framework to explicitly model evolving dependency structures for both point-wise alerting and native variable-level explanation. Specifically, JAPE (i) proposes a Decoupled Spatio-Temporal Representation (DSTR) backbone that decouples temporal and spatial modeling and captures lag-aware dependencies via learnable lag aggregation, thereby perceiving structural precursors before numerical deviations emerge; (ii) designs a dual-view alerting mechanism that fuses numerical forecasts with evolving dependency graphs for point-wise anomaly prediction, capturing structural evidence even under subtle numerical deviations; and (iii) presents Native Predictive Explanation (NPE), which directly reuses the predicted dependency graphs to rank variables by structural deviations without additional models or training. Extensive experiments on five real-world benchmarks across three prediction horizons demonstrate that JAPE improves average F1 and AUC-PR by 19.7% and 41.3%, respectively, while improving explainability with 26.6% gain in MRR.

</details>


### [82] [Learning with Bilevel-Minimax Optimization for Efficient and Reliable Transfer Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11815)
*Yaohua Liu,Yifan Guo,Jiaxin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BMAT（Bilevel-Minimax Adversarial Transfer）框架，从双层最小最大优化视角重新审视基于迁移的对抗攻击，通过显式耦合初始化、替代模型适应和梯度动力学三要素，提升对抗样本在黑盒受害者模型上的迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迁移攻击主要关注扰动生成，但迁移性本质上由初始化、替代模型适应和梯度动力学的耦合共同决定。现有方法未从统一优化框架层面建模这三者的协同关系，导致跨架构迁移性能受限。

Method: 提出双层最小最大（bilevel-minimax）优化框架：外层双层公式捕获初始化与扰动间的依赖关系；内层最小最大问题促进替代模型鲁棒性以实现跨架构泛化。算法上设计了自底向上的集成求解器，包含Soft权重调制器（Soft Weight Modulator）和隐式梯度逼近器（Implicit Gradient Approximator），实现初始化、替代适应与扰动优化的三元耦合。

Result: 在分类和分割基准上，BMAT在超过30个受害者模型上超越10余个强基线，在架构内与跨架构迁移性上均有提升，分割任务上mIoU最大降低2倍。

Conclusion: BMAT通过bilevel-minimax框架系统性地建模了迁移攻击中初始化-适应-梯度的三元耦合，并配套了有效的求解算法和理论分析，显著提升了对抗样本的黑盒迁移能力。

Abstract: Transfer-based adversarial attacks craft adversarial examples using surrogate models to mislead black-box victim models. Beyond perturbation generation, transferability is fundamentally governed by the coupling of initialization, surrogate adaptation, and gradient dynamics. We revisit this challenge from a bilevel-minimax perspective and propose BMAT (Bilevel-Minimax Adversarial Transfer). The bilevel formulation captures the dependency between initialization and perturbation, while the inner minimax problem promotes surrogate robustness for cross-architecture generalization. Algorithmically, we develop an integrated bottom-up solver that combines a Soft Weight Modulator and an Implicit Gradient Approximator to enable ternary coupling among initialization, surrogate adaptation, and perturbation optimization. We further provide theoretical insights into the optimization dynamics of the proposed bilevel-minimax framework. Extensive experiments on classification and segmentation benchmarks show that BMAT outperforms more than 10 strong baselines across more than 30 victim models, improving both intra- and cross-architecture transfer and yielding up to a 2x reduction in mIoU. Code is available at https://github.com/callous-youth/BMAT.

</details>


### [83] [Towards Understanding On-Policy Distillation through the Lens of Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11829)
*Xinmu Ge,Zizhuo Zhang,Yu Huang,Jianing Zhu,Lin Yuan,Wanli Gu,Weichang Wu,Weiran Huang,Xiaolu Zhang,Bo Han,Jun Zhou,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过测试时扩展（test-time scaling）的视角重新审视在线蒸馏（OPD）对LLM推理能力的影响，发现OPD主要提升采样效率而非真正扩展学生的推理能力边界，并将其定性为"幻觉式蒸馏"（illusory distillation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为在线蒸馏（OPD）能够让学生模型从更强的教师模型中蒸馏知识，从而扩展其超越自身基础能力的推理能力。作者希望检验这一观点是否成立，通过采样预算K的视角，使用pass@K和avg@K指标系统地评估OPD对模型推理能力边界的真实影响。

Method: 作者在多种OPD变体上进行实验，通过变化采样预算K并使用pass@K和avg@K进行评估；同时追踪OPD训练过程中pass@K的动态变化，并使用pass@1024作为判据进行问题级别的可解性分析，考察哪些问题在训练后变得可解/不可解。

Result: 实验发现：1) OPD训练的模型在所有采样预算下维持avg@K优势，但pass@K优势随着K增大逐渐转移到未经过OPD的基础模型；2) OPD训练过程中pass@K动态显示小K性能增强是以牺牲大K能力边界为代价的；3) 使用pass@1024作为判据的问题级可解性分析显示不对称性——OPD导致更多原本可解的问题变成不可解，而原本不可解的问题变成可解的数量较少。

Conclusion: 从能力扩展的角度来看，OPD更像是一种"幻觉式蒸馏"（illusory distillation）：其表面上的性能提升主要来源于采样效率的改善，而非真正从教师模型中获取了新的推理能力。这一发现对现有OPD的理论理解和实践应用具有重要启示。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as a promising post-training technique for enhancing LLM reasoning. It is commonly believed to enable the student model to distill knowledge from a stronger teacher model, thereby expanding capabilities beyond the pre-OPD base model. In this study, we examine this view through the lens of test-time scaling by varying the sampling budget K and evaluating performance with pass@K and avg@K. Specifically, across several OPD variants, we observe that OPD-trained models maintain superior avg@K performance across sampling budgets, while the advantage in pass@K gradually shifts to the pre-OPD base models as K increases. These results suggest that OPD primarily improves sampling efficiency rather than consistently expanding the student's reasoning capability boundary. The pass@K dynamics throughout OPD training further reveal a progressive shift toward stronger small-K performance at the expense of the large-K capability boundary. Furthermore, a problem-level solvability analysis using pass@1024 as the criterion reveals an asymmetry: OPD causes more previously solvable problems to become unsolvable than previously unsolvable problems to become solvable. Together, these findings suggest that, from the perspective of capability expansion, OPD behaves more like an "illusory distillation": its apparent gains arise primarily from improved sampling efficiency rather than from acquiring genuinely new reasoning capabilities from the teacher.

</details>


### [84] [Kernel Methods for Learning Operators with Multiple Inputs and Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11831)
*Adrien Weihs,Chunyang Liao,Jingmin Sun,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于核的通用编码器-解码器框架用于算子学习，支持多输入多输出场景，并引入KernelMO系列方法，在五类参数化偏微分方程上达到与神经算子相当甚至更优的预测精度，同时大幅降低训练和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习无限维对象之间的映射是科学机器学习中的核心挑战，现有神经算子方法虽然有效但计算成本高，需要一种兼具数学可处理性和计算效率的通用算子学习框架。

Method: 提出一个将观测、表示、学习和重构分离的通用核编码器-解码器框架，用于多输入多输出算子学习；通过逼近理论证明收敛率由最具挑战性的组成逼近问题决定，而非整体问题维度；并据此开发KernelMO系列，包含算子值核和乘积空间核两种互补的核方法，支持闭式训练与推理。

Result: 在五类参数化偏微分方程上，所提方法达到有竞争力或最先进的预测精度，同时相比神经算子和深度学习模型显著降低训练与推理成本。

Conclusion: 所提出的核方法框架为算子学习提供了一种数学上可处理、计算上高效且轻量的替代方案，适用于多输入多输出和多算子学习场景。

Abstract: Learning mappings between infinite-dimensional objects is a central challenge in scientific machine learning. We introduce a general kernel-based encoder-decoder framework for operator learning that separates observation, representation, learning, and reconstruction. We develop this framework for multi-input, multi-output operator learning, where operators map between products of potentially distinct function spaces. Our approximation theory shows that, although the number of inputs and outputs can increase, the convergence rate is governed by the most challenging constituent approximation problem rather than the overall problem dimension. The framework leads to practical kernel methods with closed-form training and inference, combining mathematical tractability with computational efficiency. We further specialize the approach to multiple operator learning by introducing KernelMO, a family of kernel methods with complementary operator-valued and product-space formulations. Across five families of parametric partial differential equations, the proposed methods achieve competitive or state-of-the-art predictive accuracy while reducing training and inference costs relative to neural operator architectures and deep learning based models, offering an efficient and lightweight alternative.

</details>


### [85] [Air Quality Station Simulation via LSTM and Attention-Based Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11839)
*Alexander Kostadinov,Petar O. Hristov,Dessislava Petrova-Antonova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SATADL，一种用于城市空气质量站故障期间数据模拟的深度学习模型，能够在测量站失效长达48小时的情况下，基于复杂关系推断多时段空气质量预报。在四个全球空气质量站数据集上，SATADL在决定系数和均方根误差方面均优于多种基线模型，适合作为虚拟代理站。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空气质量监测站网络的可靠运行对空气质量分析至关重要，但硬件故障和断电常导致数据不可用，影响分析质量。现有方法缺乏在多小时前瞻预测中模拟失效测量站的能力，因此需要一种能够推断数据中复杂关系并提供多时段预报的模型。

Method: SATADL是一种深度学习模型，其架构能够从数据的不同方面提取信息，通过多分支/多视角的结构学习污染物浓度与环境因素之间的复杂非线性关系，实现长达48小时的前瞻预测。研究使用四个全球范围的空气质量站数据，以PM10浓度为预测目标，设计48小时假设故障实验验证模型性能，并与多个基线和已发表的深度学习模型进行对比。

Result: SATADL在不同预测窗口（最长48小时）上，针对决定系数（R²）和均方根误差（RMSE）两个指标均优于所有对比的基线和已发表的深度学习基准模型，证明了其在模拟失效站点数据方面的出色表现。

Conclusion: SATADL能够有效地作为虚拟代理站，在城市空气质量监测站故障期间提供可靠的预测数据，确保气象与环境分析的连续性。其在多种预测窗口和多个地理区域数据上的稳定表现，表明该模型具有良好的泛化能力和实际部署价值。

Abstract: Poor air quality in urban areas is driven by a complex chain of processes and presents a significant public health concern. To better understand and control the mechanisms that determine air quality, cities deploy networks of measurement stations, and launch initiatives for collecting denser data about the concentration of pollutants in the atmosphere. Extracting insights from the stations relies on their reliable and uninterrupted operation. However, hardware is susceptible to faults and black- outs that may result in data unavailability, which affects the overall quality of analyses. In this paper, we present a deep-learning model, called SATADL, which can infer complex relations and output multiple-hour-ahead air-quality forecasts. The goal of the model is to simulate the mea- surements of an unresponsive station until its operation is restored. The architecture of the model, which allows it to extract information from different aspects of the data, is described in detail and a careful examination of all of its components is provided. We demonstrate the performance of SATADL on four sets of air quality stations from around the world, by using it to simulate the concentration of PM10 for periods of hypothetical failures of one of the measurement stations, lasting for as long as 48 hours. A selection of baseline and published deep learning models were trained and used as a benchmark. The results show that SATADL per- forms better across different prediction windows, for both coefficient of determination and root mean squared error, demonstrating its suitability as a virtual proxy station.

</details>


### [86] [Small-Scale Experiments: Are We There Yet?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11859)
*Nicholas Lourie,Kyunghyun Cho,Karen Ullrich,Sanae Lotfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示缩放定律在小规模模型上"失效"的根本原因是超参数敏感性而非模型规模本身。作者通过消融实验证明，经过充分调优的超参数比任何其他因素都重要；随着模型规模增大，超参数损失面维度降低，使调优变容易。基于此发现，作者提出了一种新的模型中心研究方法论，并成功从小规模实验复现了Transformer中预归一化优于后归一化的大规模结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放定律承诺了成本效益的实验，但六年过去仍未能完全兑现。研究者发现缩放定律在小规模（最小4M参数）下不可靠，普遍认为必须使用大规模模型。作者质疑这一结论，认为真正的混淆因素是超参数敏感性，而非模型规模本身。

Method: 通过消融基础缩放定律配方证明超参数调优的重要性；分析超参数损失面的维度随规模的变化；结合近期文献，提出新的模型中心研究方法论，并将其应用于Transformer归一化层位置这一经典问题。

Result: 发现小模型对超参数高度敏感，但敏感性随规模增大而消退；超参数调优比其他任何因素都更关键；超参数损失面随规模增大而变得低维；通过新方法论，从小规模实验成功恢复了大规模结论——预归一化在模型增大时效果更好。

Conclusion: 缩放定律并非不存在，而是在小规模上被超参数敏感性掩盖。只要具备正确的工具和对超参数动态的更深入理解，小规模实验可以兑现缩放定律长期承诺的价值。

Abstract: Scaling laws promised cost-effective experiments; six years later, they have yet to fully deliver. Instead, researchers have found them unreliable at small scales (starting at 4M parameters) and concluded that sizable models cannot be avoided. We show this is not the case: the confounding factor is hyperparameters. Small models are highly sensitive, but hyperparameter sensitivity fades with scale. This small-scale sensitivity makes scaling laws easy to miss because they only emerge on the fully tuned frontier, and reaching that frontier requires an extensive search far beyond what most ever run. By ablating the basic scaling law recipe, we show well-tuned hyperparameters matter more than any other ingredient. Further, we reveal why those hyperparameters become easier to find: as scale increases, the hyperparameter loss surface becomes lower dimensional. Nevertheless while scaling laws exist in small models, extrapolation hits statistical limitations. A holistic approach is required. Synthesizing our insights with the recent literature, we develop a new methodology for model-centric research and demonstrate it on a question that once took the field years to settle: where to place normalization layers in the transformer architecture. From small-scale experiments, we recover the large scale result: pre-normalization works better as models grow in size. With the right tools and a better understanding, small-scale experiments can deliver on scaling laws' long-awaited promise.

</details>


### [87] [Forward and Inverse Virtual Metrology for Phototransistor Gain: A Hierarchical, Uncertainty-Aware Approach for Small Production Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11868)
*Mahshid Amirabgir,Lorenza Ferrario,Paolo Conci,Mahdieh Amirabgir,Giancarlo Orengo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对硅双极光电晶体管制造中工艺参数与器件增益的预测问题，在仅13-14次工艺运行的小样本、多层次结构数据上展开研究。通过方差分解发现约一半的增益方差来自批次间而非批次内，从而给出了基于工艺配方的增益预测上限；并提出了前向增益预测器、逆向配方搜索以及嵌套物理实体（批次/晶圆/芯片）的多级数据质量评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅双极光电晶体管的工艺流定制、优化与稳定化需要耗费数月的洁净室时间才能测量最终器件，因此在运行前基于工艺参数预测器件增益的模型具有超出其本身精度的价值。然而传统虚拟计量的大语料范式不适用于仅有十几次工艺运行的小样本、层次化结构场景。

Method: 方法包括三部分：(1) 对器件增益进行方差分解，量化批次间与批次内方差占比；(2) 建立前向增益预测器，输出带相对不确定性信号的预测；(3) 构建逆向搜索以返回目标增益对应的工艺配方；(4) 设计面向嵌套物理实体（批次、晶圆、芯片）的多级数据质量评估框架，含显式跨层级关联评分。

Result: 研究发现约一半的器件增益方差位于工艺批次之间而非批次内部，这意味着仅依赖工艺配方的预测存在结构性上界。基于此构建的前向预测器提供了相对且含不确定性的信号，逆向搜索能够从目标增益反推工艺配方。

Conclusion: 该工作表明小样本、层次结构的制造场景可通过方差分解建立预测的理论边界，并提供了完整的前向预测、逆向设计和数据质量评估工具链；同时公开了标准化数据集与分析代码以保证完全可复现性。

Abstract: The customization, optimization and stabilization of the process flow of a silicon bipolar phototransistor commits months of cleanroom time before a finished device can be measured, so a model that predicts device gain from process parameters before a run has value out of proportion to its accuracy. We study this problem on a real fabrication history, thirteen to fourteen process runs of a single device: a small-sample, hierarchically structured setting unlike the large-corpus regime of conventional virtual metrology. Decomposing the variance of device gain, we find that roughly half of it lies between process runs rather than within them, so recipe-only prediction is bounded by construction. Building on these findings we provide a forward gain predictor with a relative, uncertainty-aware signal, an inverse search that returns recipes for a target gain, and, as the foundation for all of it, a multi-level data-quality assessment tailored to the nested physical entities of fabrication (batch, wafer, die) with an explicit cross-level linkage score. The normalized dataset and analysis code are released for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [88] [DCM Bandits: Multiplayer Information Asymmetric Cascading Bandits for Multiple Clicks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11873)
*Andy Wang,Charlton Shih,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将在单智能体依赖点击模型（DCM）Bandits 扩展到多人信息不对称场景，其中多个智能体与共享的有序列表交互并可观察多次点击。研究了两类不对称性（动作和奖励），为三种至少含一种不对称性的设置提供次线性遗憾保证，并证明小终止概率下无需知道终止排序，优于先前单智能体结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DCM Bandits研究主要针对单智能体设定，而现实场景中多个智能体（如推荐系统中的多个广告商）需要与同一排名列表交互，且各自拥有不同的信息视角（动作空间和奖励信号不同）。这种信息不对称性给探索和利用策略带来新的挑战，亟需理论上的分析与算法设计。

Method: 扩展DCM Bandits到多人信息不对称框架，系统建模了两类不对称性：(1) 动作不对称，即不同智能体可选择的动作集合不同；(2) 奖励不对称，即不同智能体观察到的奖励信号不同。针对三种至少存在一种不对称性的设置设计了相应的bandit算法，并给出了次线性遗憾界。此外，对于较小的终止概率，证明智能体无需预先知道终止排序即可取得良好性能。

Result: 为三种不对称设置提供了次线性遗憾保证，改进了先前的单智能体结果（小终止概率下无需知道终止排序）。实验验证了算法在不对称环境中的有效性，并揭示了反馈结构（全反馈vs首次点击反馈）对协调探索和最小化遗憾的关键作用。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于将DCM Bandits从单智能体推广到多人信息不对称场景，为这一更具现实意义的设定提供了理论保障。同时指出了匹配的信息论下界仍为开放问题，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the Dependent Click Model (DCM) Bandits to a multiplayer information-asymmetric setting, where multiple agents interact with a shared ranked list and may observe multiple clicks per session, introducing new challenges for selection strategies. We study asymmetry in (1) actions and (2) rewards, providing sublinear regret guarantees for three settings where at least one asymmetry is present. Establishing matching information-theoretic lower bounds for these settings is left as an open problem. We further show that for small termination probabilities, the termination ranking need not be known, improving on prior single-agent results. Experiments confirm that our algorithms perform well across asymmetric environments and highlight the critical role of feedback structure, specifically the distinction between full versus first-click feedback, in coordinating exploration and minimizing regret.

</details>


### [89] [Disentangling the Expressivity of RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11909)
*Selim Jerad,Anej Svete,Jiaoda Li,Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对旋转位置编码（RoPE）Transformer的表达能力进行了形式化分析。研究发现，若每个旋转分量都是周期性的，则RoPE Transformer恰好能识别带模谓词的过去时序逻辑（PTPL+MOD）可定义的语言；而传统RoPE由于旋转永不重复，只能提供依赖于精度的有界固定偏移回看模拟，而非全长度模特征化。对照实验证实了这一分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于RoPE成功的解释存在两种对立的观点：一类表达性研究将周期位置信息与模谓词联系起来，另一类机制性和长上下文研究强调位置锚点和局部偏移。作者希望统一这两种观点，并弄清传统RoPE（旋转不重复）与理想化周期RoPE在表达能力上的本质区别，从而拉近理论表达性刻画与实际使用模型之间的距离。

Method: 作者在全均匀、有限精度的soft-attention Transformer框架下进行形式化分析。他们首先刻画周期性RoPE（每个旋转分量均周期）的表达能力，证明其恰好对应带模谓词的过去时序逻辑语言类；随后分析传统RoPE，因其旋转角度（通常为2πi/n形式）永不重复，推导出精度相关的有界固定偏移回看操作模拟，而非全长度模特征化。最后通过构建的周期调度实验与传统RoPE进行对比验证。

Result: 周期性RoPE Transformer精确识别PTPL+MOD可定义的语言类；传统RoPE因旋转永不重复，只能实现有界、精度依赖的固定偏移回看模拟。对照实验表明，构造的周期调度能在模语言任务上实现长度泛化，而传统RoPE表现出有界局部性偏置，且可能损害需要位置不变地访问远距离上下文的任务。

Conclusion: 理论与实验共同表明，传统RoPE的无限旋转特性并不等同于理想的周期位置编码。传统RoPE本质上更像一个有界局部性机制，其表达能力的理论刻画需要考虑精度和序列长度的有限性。这一发现连接了现有的表达性与机制性观点，并为实际使用的RoPE模型提供了更贴近的理论基础。

Abstract: Two accounts recur in explanations of the success of rotary position embeddings (RoPE). Expressivity studies associate periodic position information with modular predicates, whereas mechanistic and long-context studies emphasize positional anchors and local offsets. We formalize both accounts for fully uniform, finite-precision soft-attention transformers. We find that, if every rotary component is periodic, RoPE transformers recognize exactly the languages definable in past temporal logic with modular predicates. Conventional RoPE is different: The rotations it computes never repeat. This yields a precision-dependent bounded simulation of fixed-offset look-back operators, rather than an all-length modular characterization. Controlled experiments match this separation: Constructed periodic schedules length-generalize on modular languages, while conventional RoPE behaves more like a bounded locality bias and can impair tasks requiring position-invariant access to distant context. Altogether, our findings shed light on RoPE transformers, bringing theoretical expressivity characterizations closer to models used in practice.

</details>


### [90] [A Factor Graph Approach to Scalable Multi-Output Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11917)
*Wouter W. L. Nuijten,Esther G. van Pelt,Albert Podusenko,İsmail Şenöz,Wouter M. Kouw*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多输出高斯过程回归表达为Forney风格因子图，通过最近邻链将候选输入排序为一维序列，沿链以线性高斯转移因子演化隐Matérn过程，并通过线性coregionalization模型混合L个隐过程到D个输出。后验计算通过精确高斯消息传递完成，复杂度为O(C(DL²+L³))，对缺失观测只需省略局部因子而无需重构协方差矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多输出高斯过程回归的计算成本随观测数乘以输出数呈立方级增长，且当不同输出在不同输入点被观测时，稠密核矩阵方法需要特殊处理。现有方法在扩展性上存在瓶颈，需要一种能够高效处理大规模数据、缺失观测且不依赖稠密核矩阵的方案。

Method: 将多输出高斯过程回归构建为Forney风格因子图：通过最近邻链将固定候选输入集排序为一维序列；沿链用线性高斯转移因子刻画隐Matérn过程的演化；线性模型coregionalization通过确定性混合因子将L个隐过程混合为D个输出，并配备每个输出独立的标量观测因子。后验计算简化为链上的精确高斯消息传递，复杂度为O(C(DL²+L³))，缺失观测通过省略局部因子自然处理。

Result: 在合成输入维度扫描实验中，低输入维度下因子图后验与精确核矩阵后验紧密吻合；维度越高差距逐渐增大但仍与稀疏变分、最近邻两种近似基线持平。在电力时间序列预测上，因子图公式在预测精度上与三个基线匹配，同时计算复杂度随数据点数量线性增长，而精确核矩阵方法已不可行，诱导点基线仍显著更慢。

Conclusion: 因子图公式化方法在低输入维度下能精确逼近核矩阵后验，随数据规模线性扩展，对缺失观测具有天然适应性，最适合输入维度较低的候选集场景，为大规模多输出高斯过程回归提供了高效且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Multi-output Gaussian process regression scales cubically in the number of observations times outputs, and dense kernel-matrix methods need bespoke handling whenever different outputs are observed at different inputs. We express multi-output Gaussian process regression as a Forney-style factor graph in which a nearest-neighbor chain orders a fixed candidate set of $C$ inputs into a one-dimensional sequence. Along this chain, latent Matérn processes evolve through linear-Gaussian transition factors, while the linear model of coregionalization mixes $L$ latent processes into $D$ outputs through a deterministic mixing factor and per-output scalar observation factors. Posterior computation reduces to exact Gaussian message passing on the chain at cost $\mathcal{O}(C(DL^2 + L^3))$ after chain construction, and missing observations omit their local factor without any covariance-matrix restructuring. The formulation therefore scales in the number of data samples and in the rate of missing observations, while remaining best suited to candidate sets in low input dimension.We compare the factor-graph formulation against an exact kernel-matrix baseline, a sparse-variational inducing-point baseline, and a nearest-neighbor baseline on a synthetic input-dimension sweep and on electricity time series forecasting. At low input dimension the factor-graph posterior tracks the exact kernel-matrix posterior closely, and the gap grows gradually as input dimension increases while staying competitive with both approximate baselines. On the electricity time series our factor-graph formulation matches all three baselines in forecast accuracy while scaling linearly in the number of data points, where the exact kernel-matrix method becomes infeasible and the inducing-point baseline remains substantially slower.

</details>


### [91] [Distillation of Foundation Models for Time-dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11937)
*Daniel Musekamp,Boshra Ariguib,Andrei Manolache,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TREX（Teacher Rollout Extension）知识蒸馏框架，将预训练基础模型（用于时间相关偏微分方程）的预测能力转移到紧凑高效的学生模型中。通过教师模型生成长期合成轨迹（可选噪声注入）来增强有限的下游数据，使学生在参数量减少数个数量级、推理速度提升一个数量级以上的同时，匹配甚至超越教师的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练基础模型虽能通过少量微调在下游偏微分方程任务中取得高精度，但模型规模大、计算开销高，难以作为数值求解器的快速替代品。需要一种方法将大模型能力压缩到小模型中，同时保持低数据场景下的强泛化能力。

Method: TREX框架从微调后的教师模型出发，通过教师自回归滚动生成长期合成轨迹来扩充有限的下游训练数据，可选地加入周期性噪声注入。该方法无需显式知道初始条件分布即可采样教师诱导的滚动分布，使学生模型暴露于长时程状态以及自回归预测中遇到的局部恢复行为。学生模型还可融入任务特定的归纳偏置（如等变性质）。

Result: 在多个PDE基准上评估TREX，所得学生模型在精度上匹配或超越教师模型，同时参数量减少数个数量级，推理速度提升一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: TREX有效地将大规模预训练PDE基础模型的知识蒸馏到紧凑高效的学生模型中，解决了基础模型作为快速数值求解器替代品时面临的计算瓶颈，表明知识蒸馏结合教师滚动数据增强是一种在大幅压缩模型规模的同时保持甚至提升精度的可行路径。

Abstract: Foundation models for time-dependent partial differential equations (PDEs) are trained on large and diverse collections of physical systems and can generalize effectively to new downstream tasks. After fine-tuning on only a few trajectories from a target domain, they can achieve strong accuracy in low-data regimes. However, these models are typically large and computationally intensive, limiting their usefulness as fast surrogates for numerical solvers. We propose Teacher Rollout Extension (TREX), a knowledge distillation framework that transfers the predictive capability of a pretrained foundation model into a compact and efficient student. Starting from a fine-tuned teacher, TREX augments limited downstream data by generating long synthetic trajectories through teacher rollouts, optionally with periodic noise injection. This procedure samples from the teacher-induced rollout distribution without requiring explicit knowledge of the initial-condition distribution, while exposing the student to long-horizon states and local recovery behavior around states encountered during autoregressive prediction. The student can further incorporate task-specific inductive biases, such as equivariance, that the teacher does not necessarily enforce. We evaluate TREX on multiple PDE benchmarks. The resulting students can match or surpass the teacher's accuracy while reducing the number of parameters by several orders of magnitude and achieving more than an order-of-magnitude speedup in inference.

</details>


### [92] [TailBooster: A Dual-Layer Generative Framework for Extreme Value Augmentation with Operational Validity Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11951)
*Karim Aly,Alexei Sharpanskykh,Jacco Hoekstra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TailBooster，一种双层生成框架，用于航空运输极端事件（如严重延误和异常飞行时间）的合成数据增强。它结合统计层（IQR提取尾部数据）和深度学习层（自编码器清洗不合规记录），在极端到达时间和延误预测上将MAE分别降低47-49%和29-57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空运输极端事件（严重延误、异常飞行时间）会引发级联网络中断，造成巨大的运营、经济和安全性成本。这类事件在历史数据中极为稀少，导致机器学习模型训练信号不足。现有合成数据增强方法存在两个缺陷：传统生成模型对分布尾部表达不足（欠表征），且无法保证生成样本在运营上可行（如短飞行时间配上长飞行距离）。尚无方法能同时解决混合型表格数据中这两个限制。

Method: TailBooster采用双层生成框架：1）统计层：利用四分位距（IQR）从历史数据中提取极端样本，为专用生成模型（Tabular Variational Autoencoder, TVAE）提供尾部聚焦的训练信号；2）深度学习层：应用基于自编码器的清洗机制，丢弃违反历史数据中学习到的运营包络线的合成记录。框架完全数据驱动且模型无关。

Result: 在美国航班记录上从五个维度评估：多样性、统计相似性、保真度、运营有效性、实用性。数据驱动的清洗显著改善了运营有效性，定向增强提升了极端事件预测的实用性。在六个回归算法上，相比传统合成数据，极端飞行时间预测MAE降低47-49%，极端到达延误预测MAE降低29-57%；将真实数据与合成极端数据混合时也获得了可比拟的增益。

Conclusion: TailBooster通过结合尾部聚焦的生成建模与自编码器清洗，有效解决了极端事件合成数据中分布尾部欠表征和运营不可行两个核心问题。作为完全数据驱动、模型无关的框架，它可扩展到极端事件预测至关重要且缺乏领域规则的领域。

Abstract: Extreme events in air transport, such as severe arrival delays and abnormal air times, cause cascading network disruptions with substantial operational, economic, and safety costs. Such events are rare in historical records, leaving insufficient training signal for machine learning models. Synthetic data augmentation offers a principled solution, but conventional generative models under-represent distributional tails and give no guarantee against operationally infeasible instances, such as a short air time paired with a long flight distance. No existing approach addresses both limitations for mixed-type tabular records. We propose TailBooster, a dual-layer generative framework combining generative modelling with two anomaly detection layers. A statistical layer extracts extremes via the interquartile range, supplying tail-concentrated training signal to dedicated generative models, here a Tabular Variational Autoencoder. A deep learning layer then applies autoencoder-based cleaning, discarding synthetic records that violate the operational envelope learned from historical data. The framework was evaluated on US flight records across five dimensions: diversity, statistical similarity, fidelity, operational validity, and utility, the latter two being the primary improvement targets. Data-driven cleaning markedly improved operational validity, while targeted augmentation enhanced utility for extreme-event prediction. Across six regression algorithms, training on the framework's records reduced Mean Absolute Error by 47-49% on extreme air time and 29-57% on extreme arrival delay prediction relative to conventional synthetic data, with comparable gains when real records were enriched with synthetic extremes. Being fully data-driven and model-agnostic, TailBooster extends to domains where extreme-event prediction is critical and domain-specific rules are unavailable.

</details>


### [93] [LoongReflect: Boosting Long-Horizon Reflection in Search Agents via Global Perspective Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11967)
*Zhixin Zhang,Xinke Jiang,Zhibang Yang,Weixuan Xu,Guohong Qiu,Xu Chu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LoongReflect框架，将推理智能体的反思形式化为记忆控制策略，通过可逆轨迹树上的显式反思和回溯动作，结合快速通道（教师蒸馏）与慢速通道（基于结果的GRPO）的协作机制来解决反思学习中局部与全局信号不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反思是长程推理智能体的关键能力，但有效学习反思非常困难：反思在局部分支内执行，而其效用只能由最终轨迹结果决定。这种局部-全局不匹配使得基于结果的强化学习只能为反思决策提供局部、稀疏且延迟的监督信号，难以有效学习反思策略。

Method: LoongReflect将反思形式化为记忆控制策略：1) 将智能体运行在可逆轨迹树上，支持显式的reflect和backtrack动作；2) 通过前视外梯度的协调机制结合两种互补信号——快速通道从特权教师中蒸馏全局信息丰富的反思行为，监督仅限反思和回溯token；慢速通道使用基于结果的GRPO优化完整轨迹，将局部控制决策与最终任务成功对齐。

Result: 在多跳检索增强生成和数学推理基准上的实验表明，LoongReflect一致优于仅基于结果的强化学习和自蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 通过将反思建模为记忆控制策略并协调局部蒸馏与全局结果优化的双通道学习，LoongReflect有效解决了反思学习中的局部-全局监督不匹配问题，显著提升了大语言模型智能体在长程推理任务中的反思能力和整体性能。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on long-horizon reasoning to solve complex tasks involving planning, tool use, and memory. A critical capability in such settings is reflection: assessing trajectory progress, identifying missing evidence and unreliable intermediate states, and deciding whether to continue, revise, or abandon the current branch. Learning effective reflection, however, is challenging because reflection is performed locally within the current branch, whereas its utility can only be determined by its contribution to the final trajectory outcome. This local-global mismatch makes outcome-based reinforcement learning provide only local, sparse and delayed supervision for reflective decisions. To solve these, we propose LoongReflect, a training framework that formulates reflection as a memory-control policy. The agent operates over a reversible trajectory tree using explicit reflect and backtrack actions. Reflection consolidates verified facts, missing evidence, and branch-specific risks into working memory, while backtracking removes an unreliable branch from the active context and preserves a concise corrective lesson. To learn this policy, LoongReflect combines two complementary signals through a look-ahead, extragradient-style coordination mechanism. A fast channel distills globally informed reflective behavior from a privileged teacher, with supervision restricted to reflection and backtracking tokens. A slow channel optimizes complete trajectories using outcome-based GRPO, aligning local control decisions with final task success. Experiments on multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation and mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over outcome-only reinforcement learning and self-distillation baselines.

</details>


### [94] [TESLA: Taylor Expansion of Sinusoidal Learnable Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11970)
*Daehwa Ko,Jaehyeon Kim,Seunghyun Ham,Jay Hoon Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TESLA激活函数，通过可学习的正弦与余弦项组合实现对多项式阶数的显式控制，从而解决神经网络的奇偶性难题。在n=32的奇偶问题上，仅用10万样本（约占输入空间0.002%）即实现强泛化，并在30%标签噪声下保持高精度；同时在ImageNet-100上取得与主流方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇偶性问题（判断二进制向量中1的个数为奇偶）对标准神经网络仍具挑战性，根本原因在于其线性不可分性以及需要全局交互才能解决。现有神经网络缺乏对高频分量进行选择性放大的机制，因此难以在样本稀疏的情况下泛化到这类依赖全局结构的任务。

Method: 提出TESLA激活函数，定义为正弦和余弦项的可学习组合。通过约束TESLA系数可以获得Lipschitz/Rademacher复杂度界，并能塑造训练动态以强调高频结构，实现多项式阶数的显式控制与高阶分量的选择性放大。

Result: 在n=32的奇偶任务上，TESLA以10万训练样本（约0.002%的2^32输入空间）实现强泛化；在高达30%标签噪声下仍保持高精度。与SIREN、SNAKE和傅里叶特征嵌入等周期性和频域基线相比，在奇偶性和Forrelation任务上表现更优。在ImageNet-100上取得与基准相当的性能，表明激活层面的阶数控制可迁移至通用视觉任务。

Conclusion: TESLA通过激活级别的可学习正弦/余弦组合与阶数控制，为奇偶性等依赖全局高频交互的任务提供了有效解决方案，且该设计具有向通用视觉任务迁移的潜力，展示了激活函数层面显式结构控制的价值。

Abstract: The parity problem--deciding whether the number of ones in a binary vector is odd or even--remains challenging for standard neural networks due to linear inseparability and the need for global interactions. We propose TESLA, an activation defined as a learnable combination of sine and cosine terms, enabling explicit control over polynomial degree and selective amplification of high-order components. Theoretically, we show that constraining TESLA's coefficients yields Lipschitz/Rademacher complexity bounds and shapes the training dynamics to emphasize higher-frequency structure. Empirically, on parity with input length n = 32, TESLA attains strong generalization with 100K training samples (approximately 0.002% of the 2^32 input space) and remains robust under heavy corruption, retaining high accuracy with up to 30% label noise. We also compare against periodic and frequency-based baselines (SIREN, SNAKE, and Fourier feature embeddings) on parity and Forrelation. Beyond synthetic structure, TESLA delivers comparable performance on ImageNet-100, indicating that activation-level degree control transfers to more general vision workloads. Code: https://github.com/KAU-QuantumAILab/TESLA

</details>


### [95] [Remote Sensing and Machine Learning-Based Analysis of Land Use and Vegetation Change in Dhaka District, Bangladesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12001)
*Muhammad Masud Tarek,Md. Alamgir Hossain,Md. Samiul Islam,Muntasir Hasan Kanchan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用Sentinel-2和Landsat 8遥感影像，结合Google Earth Engine平台上的决策树、KNN和随机森林三种机器学习分类器，分析了孟加拉国达卡区2019至2024年间土地利用与植被动态变化。结果显示建成区面积增加59.5%，植被减少8.46%，水体减少7.77%，随机森林分类器精度最高，研究揭示了城市无序扩张带来的环境压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 达卡区的快速城市化引发了土地利用和环境条件的重大变化，需要系统的监测手段为城市规划决策和生态可持续性提供科学依据。

Method: 采用Sentinel-2 MSI和Landsat 8高分辨率卫星影像进行土地覆盖分类；计算NDVI、NDBI、NDWI三种光谱指数；在Google Earth Engine平台上使用决策树、K近邻（KNN）和随机森林三种监督机器学习分类器进行地物识别；通过混淆矩阵和Kappa统计量评估分类精度。

Result: 2019至2024年间，城市建成区面积增长59.5%，植被覆盖下降8.46%，水体面积减少7.77%；土地转换以植被区和水域向城市基础设施转化为主导趋势；随机森林分类器取得最高分类精度。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了达卡区由无管制城市扩张引起的日益加剧的环境压力，证明了遥感与机器学习工具在提供及时、可操作数据以支持可持续城市发展、土地使用监管和生态系统保护政策方面的潜力。

Abstract: Rapid urbanization in Dhaka District, Bangladesh has triggered substantial alterations in land use and environmental conditions, necessitating systematic monitoring for informed urban planning and ecological sustainability. This study employs remote sensing data and machine learning techniques to analyze spatiotemporal changes in land cover and vegetation dynamics between 2019 and 2024. High-resolution satellite imagery from Sentinel-2 MSI and Landsat 8 was utilized to classify land cover types and compute spectral indices including the Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI), Normalized Difference Built-up Index (NDBI), and Normalized Difference Water Index (NDWI). A supervised machine learning approach incorporating Decision Tree, K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), and Random Forest classifiers was applied using labeled geospatial training points within Google Earth Engine. Accuracy assessments were conducted using confusion matrices and kappa statistics. Results indicate a 59.5% increase in urban built-up areas and a significant decline in vegetation (-8.46%) and water bodies (-7.77%) over the five-year period. Land conversion from vegetated and aquatic areas to urban infrastructure was identified as a dominant trend. Among the models, Random Forest demonstrated the highest classification accuracy. These findings underscore the growing environmental pressures driven by unregulated urban expansion in Dhaka. The study highlights the potential of remote sensing and machine learning tools in providing timely, actionable data to support sustainable urban development, land-use regulation, and ecosystem conservation policies.

</details>


### [96] [Dual-Model Sentiment Analysis of Consumer Reviews in the Retail Coffee Sector Using Machine Learning and Deep Learning Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12007)
*Muntasir Hasan Kanchan,Md. Alamgir Hossain,Md. Samiul Islam,Muhammad Masud Tarek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于ConsumerAffairs平台收集的700余条星巴克客户评论，构建了经典机器学习与深度学习的对比情感分析框架。SVM在机器学习模型中取得最高准确率（91.0%），双向LSTM在深度学习中表现最佳，同时发现类别不平衡显著影响正面情感召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费者评论对品牌认知与商业策略至关重要，尤其在零售咖啡等服务业中。然而现有研究缺乏对真实世界消费者评论数据进行机器学习与深度学习方法的系统性对比评估，特别是在类别不平衡场景下的表现差异。

Method: 从ConsumerAffairs收集700余条星巴克评论，进行预处理和探索性数据分析以识别时间与地理模式；将星级评分二值化为情感标签（4-5分为正面，1-3分为负面）；分别评估5种机器学习模型（逻辑回归、SVM、决策树、随机森林、朴素贝叶斯）和5种深度学习模型（LSTM、RNN、双向LSTM、GRU、CNN），使用准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数进行性能评估。

Result: SVM在机器学习模型中准确率最高（91.0%）；双向LSTM在深度学习模型中表现最强且在未见数据上具有良好的泛化能力；类别不平衡显著降低了多个模型的正面情感召回率。

Conclusion: 研究为零售咖啡行业的客户体验分析提供了机器学习与深度学习方法的对比评估，强调了在实际消费者情感分析中合理选择模型和进行预处理的重要性。

Abstract: Consumer reviews play an important role in shaping brand perception and business strategies, particularly in service-driven industries such as retail coffee. This study presents a comparative sentiment analysis framework for Starbucks customer reviews using classical machine learning and deep learning approaches. The dataset, collected from ConsumerAffairs, contains more than 700 reviews and was analyzed through preprocessing and exploratory data analysis to identify temporal and geographic patterns. Sentiment labels were generated by binarizing star ratings, with ratings of 4 and 5 classified as positive and ratings of 1 to 3 as negative. The resulting dataset was substantially imbalanced toward negative sentiment. Five machine learning classifiers, including Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine (SVM), Decision Tree, Random Forest, and Naive Bayes, were evaluated alongside five deep learning models: LSTM, RNN, Bidirectional LSTM, GRU, and CNN. Model performance was assessed using accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score. SVM achieved the highest accuracy among the machine learning models at 91.0 percent, while Bidirectional LSTM showed the strongest performance among the deep learning models and demonstrated good generalization on unseen data. The findings also show that class imbalance negatively affected positive sentiment recall across several models. Overall, this study provides a comparative evaluation of machine learning and deep learning approaches for real-world consumer sentiment analysis and highlights the importance of appropriate model selection and preprocessing for customer experience analytics in the retail coffee sector.

</details>


### [97] [Reducing Symmetry Increase in Equivariant Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12010)
*Ning Lin,Jiacheng Cen,Anyi Li,Wenbing Huang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统地刻画了等变神经网络（ENN）中对称性输入导致输出对称性增大的现象，证明了对称性增大的下确界存在性，提出了可计算的算法来计算该下确界，并为特征设计提供了实用指南以减少有害的对称性增大，最后通过合成数据和QM9数据集验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变神经网络（ENN）在科学领域有广泛应用，但处理对称输入时表达能力会下降，因为经过等变映射后对称性会增大。此前研究仅记录了特定案例中的对称性增大现象，缺乏对其成因的严谨理解和通用的减小策略，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: （i）对于任意给定的特征空间和输入对称群，证明增大的对称性存在一个由特征空间结构决定的下确界；（ii）在此基础上开发了一个可计算的算法来推导该下确界，并提出特征设计的实用指南以防止有害的对称性增大；（iii）在标准正则性假设下，证明对大多数等变映射，提出的指南能有效减少对称性增大。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界QM9数据集上的可视化和实验验证了理论预测，证实了所提出的对称性增大下确界理论以及特征设计指南的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了对称性增大现象的详细深入刻画以及一个原则性的约简框架，包括下确界的存在性证明、可计算的推导算法和实用的特征设计指南，为理解和缓解ENN在对称输入下的表达能力退化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Equivariant Neural Networks (ENNs) have empowered numerous applications in scientific fields. Despite their remarkable capacity for representing geometric structures, ENNs suffer from degraded expressivity when processing symmetric inputs: the output representations are invariant to transformations that extend beyond the input's symmetries. The mathematical essence of this phenomenon is that a symmetric input, after being processed by an equivariant map, experiences an increase in symmetry. While prior research has documented symmetry increase in specific cases, a rigorous understanding of its underlying causes and general reduction strategies remains lacking. In this paper, we provide a detailed and in-depth characterization of symmetry increase together with a principled framework for its reduction: (i) For any given feature space and input symmetry group, we prove that the increased symmetry admits an infimum determined by the structure of the feature space; (ii) Building on this foundation, we develop a computable algorithm to derive this infimum, and propose practical guidelines for feature design to prevent harmful symmetry increases. (iii) Under standard regularity assumptions, we demonstrate that for most equivariant maps, our guidelines effectively reduce symmetry increase. To complement our theoretical findings, we provide visualizations and experiments on both synthetic datasets and the real-world QM9 dataset. The results validate our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [98] [SoftWater: Class-Aware Rate Allocation for Softmax Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12026)
*Joao V. Cavalcanti,Ashia C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SoftWater方法，将softmax输出层（head）量化建模为KL散度下的率失真问题，利用二阶分析揭示类别感知几何结构，通过可分离性近似将Kn×Kn的Cholesky分解简化为n×n分解，使得可基于单次前向传播进行格编码。在5个模型（1B-32B）上的59/60个测试点上优于WaterSIC量化器，将2比特下head引起的KL降低6.5-8.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化pipeline通常将softmax输出层保留高精度，但在具有现代词表的小型LLM中，输出头占了15-30%的参数，名义上的"2-bit"模型因fp16的head实际每权重存储位数远高于2比特。因此需要专门的softmax层量化方法。

Method: 1) 将softmax层量化建模为原始与量化输出分布之间KL散度下的率失真问题；2) 通过二阶分析揭示类别感知几何：量化误差由特征协方差和类别特定softmax曲率联合加权；3) 采用可分离性近似，将Kn×Kn的Cholesky分解替换为按类别缩放的n×n分解，使格可通过逐次干扰消除编码，且统计量来自单次前向传播。

Result: 在5个规模1B到32B的模型上，SoftWater在head速率匹配的条件下，在59/60个测试点上优于WaterSIC量化器（该量化器在linear-layer WMSE下近似最优但非输出KL最优），且未使用该pipeline的任何改进技巧，将head引起的KL在2比特下降低6.5x-8.3x。在Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct上，2比特head移除45-60%存储字节，困惑度仅增加2.9-3.7%。在捆绑权重模型上，4比特head近乎无损，2比特head困惑度增加低于4%。

Conclusion: SoftWater使得softmax输出层的量化在率和失真上均达到实际可用的水平。由于类别侧统计来自校准数据，将校准与部署域匹配可在该域上获得最低KL。head量化在捆绑权重模型中同样实用。

Abstract: Post-training quantization pipelines routinely leave the softmax output layer in high precision. Yet in small LLMs with modern vocabularies, the head holds 15--30\% of all parameters, so a nominal ``2-bit'' model with an fp16 head can store several times as many bits per weight. We pose softmax-layer quantization as a rate-distortion problem under the KL divergence between the original and quantized output distributions. A second-order analysis reveals a class-aware geometry: quantization error is weighted jointly by feature covariance and class-specific softmax curvature. A separability approximation replaces the $Kn\times Kn$ Cholesky with one $n\times n$ factorization rescaled per class, making the lattice encodable by successive interference cancellation, with both statistics from a single forward pass. The resulting method, SoftWater, gives fine grids to frequent, low-variance classes and coarse grids to rare ones, a large gap under Zipfian token distributions. Across five models from 1B to 32B, SoftWater outperforms the released WaterSIC quantizer (near-optimal under linear-layer WMSE but not output KL) at matched head rates on 59 of 60 test points, using none of that pipeline's refinements and cutting head-induced KL by $6.5\times$--$8.3\times$ at 2 bits. On Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct with quantized bodies, a 2-bit head removes 45--60\% of stored bytes for a $2.9$--$3.7\%$ perplexity increase. Because the class-side statistic comes from calibration data, matching calibration to the deployment domain gives the lowest KL on that domain throughout. On a tied model, a 4-bit head is near-lossless and a 2-bit head costs under 4\% perplexity, making head quantization of such models practical.

</details>


### [99] [Uncertainty-Aware Probabilistic Constrained Clustering from Entangled Pairwise Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12027)
*Shaojie Zhang,Ke Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出不确定性感知的概率约束聚类（UPCC）问题形式化，并引入ProbPair角度成对目标函数及ECI-PP框架（估计器-校正器-积分器），通过信念估计、校正和可靠性感知集成来精炼不完美的软标签监督，在多种概率监督设置下优于现有深度约束聚类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数深度约束聚类（DCC）方法仅针对硬的、专家无关的约束标签，对具有实数值、蕴含内在模糊性、专家判断和随机噪声的软监督标签仅做数值处理而缺乏语义建模，无法应对现实中更真实的不确定性监督场景。

Method: 首先将问题形式化为不确定性感知概率约束聚类（UPCC），通过异质观测过程定义规范化的偶然不确定性目标，并分析其条件可辨识性。随后提出ProbPair——一种针对概率关系的角度成对目标函数，并构建ECI-PP框架，包含估计器（belief estimation）、校正器（correction）和可靠性感知积分器（reliability-aware integration）三个模块，以逐步精炼不完美的监督信息。

Result: 在多种具有挑战性的概率监督设置下，在多个基准数据集上的实验表明ECI-PP优于现有最先进的深度约束聚类方法，且在使用共享默认配置时仍能保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将软约束从数值层面提升到语义和不确定性层面，并提出端到端的估计-校正-积分框架，ECI-PP能够有效处理真实世界中带有噪声和模糊性的成对监督信息，为概率约束聚类提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Pairwise constrained clustering typically relies on hard must-link/cannot-link labels, whereas realistic pairwise supervision may be real-valued and entangle intrinsic ambiguity, expert judgment, and stochastic corruption. Existing deep constrained clustering (DCC) methods mainly target hard, expert-agnostic constraints, treating soft labels mostly numerically rather than semantically. We formalize this setting as uncertainty-aware probabilistic constrained clustering (UPCC), defining a canonical aleatoric target through a heterogeneous observation process and analyzing its conditional identifiability. We introduce ProbPair, an angular pairwise objective for probabilistic relations, and build ECI-PP, an estimator--corrector--integrator framework that refines imperfect supervision via belief estimation, correction, and reliability-aware integration. Across challenging probabilistic supervision settings, experiments on diverse benchmarks show that ECI-PP outperforms state-of-the-art DCC methods and remains robust with a shared default configuration.

</details>


### [100] [Clustered Randomized Smoothing for Stochastic Prediction Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12037)
*Eduardo Figueiredo,Frederik Mathiesen,Julian Schumann,Jens Kober,Arkady Zgonnikov,Luca Laurenti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出聚类α平滑（clustered α-smoothing）框架，通过先对噪声样本进行聚类，再在每簇内局部应用α平滑，最终将各簇预测组合成混合分布，解决了随机平滑在多模态随机回归场景下因模式坍缩（mode collapse）导致的预测失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代随机预测器能够建模丰富的多模态结果分布，但在安全关键领域中确保鲁棒性至关重要。随机平滑是提升鲁棒性的领先技术，然而在多模态随机回归场景中，随机平滑常因模式坍缩而失败，产生无法反映真实分布的均值预测。

Method: 提出聚类α平滑框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 使用任意聚类算法对噪声样本进行划分；(2) 在每个簇内局部应用α平滑；(3) 将各簇预测组合为混合分布。作者将平滑分布解释为α平滑器的混合，并推导出平滑预测落在对应不同模态的紧致区域并集内的概率下界。

Result: 在两个基准上进行实证评估：(1) 驾驶模拟器数据集上的随机轨迹预测中，与α平滑相比，Wasserstein距离平均降低27%；(2) 四旋翼控制任务中（模态对应到达目标的可行路径），与最先进随机平滑方法相比碰撞率降低81%。

Conclusion: 聚类α平滑通过保留多模态信息并同时提供鲁棒性保证，显著优于现有随机平滑方法，为安全关键领域中的随机多模态预测提供了一种有效的鲁棒性增强方案。

Abstract: Modern stochastic predictors can model rich, multi-modal outcome distributions. However, this expressive power comes with challenges in ensuring robust predictions $-$ a critical requirement in safety-critical domains. Randomized smoothing is a leading technique for improving robustness, particularly against adversarial perturbations. Yet, in stochastic multi-modal regression settings, randomized smoothing often fails due to mode collapse, yielding averaged predictions that do not reflect the underlying distribution. To address this limitation, we propose clustered $α$-smoothing, a framework that (1) partitions noisy samples using an arbitrary clustering algorithm, (2) applies $α$-smoothing locally within each cluster, and (3) combines the resulting predictions into a mixture distribution. By interpreting the smoothing distribution as a mixture of $α$-smoothers, we derive a lower bound on the probability that the smoothed prediction lies within a union of compact regions corresponding to distinct modes. We empirically evaluate our framework on two benchmarks, demonstrating substantial improvements over state-of-the-art methods. In stochastic trajectory prediction on a driving simulator dataset, our approach achieves, on average, a $27\%$ lower Wasserstein distance to the ground-truth distribution compared to $α$-smoothing. In quadrotor control, where modes correspond to distinct feasible paths to a target, our method reduces the collision rate by $81\%$ relative to the state-of-the-art randomized smoothing.

</details>


### [101] [Towards Truly Unsupervised Evaluation of Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12057)
*Hafiz Saud Arshad,Muhammad Rajabinasab,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判性分析了现有无监督特征选择评估方法的缺陷，指出它们本质上并非真正的无监督评估（至多是"无监督下游任务下的监督评估"），并提出了一种基于主成分分析和最优传输的全新真正无监督评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征选择是数据挖掘中最基本且重要的任务之一，但现有的无监督特征选择评估方法存在关键设计缺陷，其"无监督"性质值得质疑。这些方法可能在评估过程中隐性使用了标签信息，从而破坏了无监督评估的纯粹性。

Method: 首先对现有所谓的无监督评估技术进行批判性讨论，揭示其并非真正无监督的本质；然后提出一个全新的无监督评估框架，该框架利用无监督主成分分析（PCA）和最优传输（Optimal Transport）来衡量特征选择算法的质量，全程不依赖任何标签信息。

Result: 提出了一个真正无监督的特征选择评估框架，能够在没有任何标签信息的情况下衡量特征选择算法的质量，克服了现有方法的根本性设计缺陷。

Conclusion: 现有被广泛采纳的无监督特征选择评估方法存在根本性缺陷，至多只能算作"无监督下游任务下的监督评估"；本文提出的基于PCA和最优传输的新框架实现了真正的无监督评估，为特征选择算法的公平评价提供了更可靠的途径。

Abstract: Feature selection is one of the most important and fundamental tasks in data mining, tackled by a family of methods with an established set of evaluation techniques to measure the quality of a specific method. Most of the methods commonly used for the unsupervised evaluation of feature selection algorithms suffer from critical design flaws which question their unsupervised nature. In this paper, we provide a critical discussion on the established allegedly unsupervised evaluation techniques, and shed light on the reasons why they are not truly unsupervised but, at best, supervised evaluation under an unsupervised downstream task. We also propose a novel, truly unsupervised evaluation framework to measure the quality of the feature selection algorithms without any form of information about the labels. The proposed framework utilizes unsupervised Principal Component Analysis, and optimal transport to measure the quality of the feature selection methods in a truly unsupervised manner.

</details>


### [102] [Faithful, Sufficient and Understandable: Rethinking Graph Counterfactual Explanations via Discrete Diffusion Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12083)
*David Bechtoldt,Sidney Bender*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GDCE-I，一种基于离散去噪扩散模型和离散反转机制的图反事实解释方法，通过分布感知的编辑在整个域编辑空间内搜索，同时提出了统一的评估框架（解释期望）并在四个基准上大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在化学、生物、网络分析等领域表现优异，但缺乏内在可解释性，限制了其在高风险场景的应用。现有的图反事实解释方法面临两难：要么编辑偏离数据流形（产生无效图），要么搜索空间不完全覆盖编辑空间。此外，现有评估框架不一致且不完整。

Method: 提出GDCE-I方法：1) 使用离散去噪扩散模型生成分布感知的编辑，确保编辑结果在数据流形上；2) 设计新颖的离散反转方案，使搜索能够覆盖整个编辑空间（包括节点和边的类别修改）；3) 推导了一套解释期望（explanation desiderata）框架，用于统一评估所有反事实解释方法。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，GDCE-I在所定义的解释期望框架下大幅优于相关工作。在分子领域，定性分析表明GDCE-I能够产生可解释且分布内（in-distribution）的解决方案。

Conclusion: GDCE-I同时满足了数据流形保持和完整编辑空间搜索两个要求，并提供了统一的评估协议，为图反事实解释提供了更可靠和实用的方案。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) achieve strong predictive performance on graph-structured data across domains such as chemistry, biology, and network analysis, yet they provide no intrinsic explanation of their predictions. This limits their adoption in high-stakes and safety-critical settings. Counterfactual explanations address this by revealing the minimal structural modifications that would change a model's prediction. On graphs, however, such a modification is hard to produce. The search space is discrete and combinatorial, and a valid answer must respect categorical node and edge types together with domain rules such as chemical valency in the case of molecular graphs. Existing explainers give up one of two things. Either edits are not held on the data manifold, or the search does not span the full edit space. We propose Graph Diffusion Counterfactual Explanation via Inversion (GDCE-I), which gives up neither. A discrete denoising diffusion model with a novel discrete inversion scheme enables distribution-aware edits leveraging the whole domain edit space. We further address the incomplete and inconsistent evaluation of graph counterfactuals by deriving a framework of explanation desiderata and applying it to every method under one shared protocol. Across four benchmarks, GDCE-I outperforms related work by a large margin on the defined framework. For the molecular domain, we further qualitatively show that GDCE-I attains interpretable in-distribution solutions.

</details>


### [103] [NAE: Normalizing AutoEncoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12084)
*Muhammad Abdur Rafae,Niels Landwehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Normalizing Autoencoder (NAE)，通过条件损失对齐代理损失梯度与重建损失梯度，改进了流自编码器（flow autoencoders）中现有的次优训练范式，在分子生成、表格数据和图像基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流自编码器（包括全维度和瓶颈设置）使用的训练损失在理论上被证明是次优的——编码器和解码器的代理损失必须与重建损失对齐优化。作者希望纠正这一理论缺陷并提升生成性能。

Method: NAE采用一种新颖的条件损失，使得代理损失（surrogate loss）的梯度与重建损失（reconstruction loss）的梯度对齐。通过理论分析证明这种对齐是训练流自编码器的最优策略，并由此设计了新的训练目标。

Result: 在分子生成、表格数据和图像基准等广泛实验中，NAE均取得了最先进的性能，验证了损失对齐策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文阐明了损失对齐在流自编码器中的关键作用，并确立了NAE作为一种强大的生成框架。该工作为流自编码器领域提供了新的理论视角和实用的方法论改进。

Abstract: We consider the setting of Normalizing flows with approximate inverses, an established paradigm spanning both full-dimensional ($d=D$) and bottleneck ($d<D$) settings, and group these models under the term flow autoencoders. We present a theoretical investigation into their training dynamics and prove that the proposed loss used by existing approaches is suboptimal; specifically, both encoder and decoder surrogates must be optimized in alignment with reconstruction loss. Guided by these insights, we propose Normalizing Autoencoder (NAE), which employs a novel conditional loss that aligns the surrogate loss gradient with that of reconstruction loss, directly improving upon the current standard. Extensive experiments across molecule generation, tabular data, and image benchmarks demonstrate that NAE achieves state of the art performance. Our work highlights the importance of loss alignment in flow autoencoders and establishes NAE as a powerful generative framework.
