</details>


### [104] [Task- and dataset-specific information in protein language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12090)
*Roman Joeres,Ilya Senatorov,Olga V. Kalinina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了13个蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）在15个下游任务、11个数据集上的中间层嵌入信息量，发现PLMs的最后一层嵌入很少能产生最佳下游任务表现，并揭示了任务类型与嵌入层分布之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型已在计算生物学领域广泛应用，但社区普遍默认使用模型最后一层的嵌入，对其内部各层的行为和嵌入信息量缺乏深入理解，亟需系统性的分析来指导更优的嵌入选择策略。

Method: 对13个PLMs在15个下游任务上，从每个中间层提取嵌入并训练探针模型进行比较，同时计算各层潜在空间的统计特征来评估嵌入所含信息，以识别最优嵌入层与任务类型之间的关系。

Result: 1）最后一层嵌入很少是下游任务最优选择；2）预训练目标与残基级属性预测任务的相似性导致随层数增加理解能力稳定提升；3）对于全蛋白任务，数据集而非任务本身决定PLMs的表现；4）浅层嵌入更适合深突变扫描（DMS）数据，深层嵌入更适合多样化天然蛋白数据；5）在人工蛋白任务上PLMs性能显著下降。

Conclusion: PLMs的中间层嵌入往往比最后一层更具信息价值，嵌入层的选择应基于下游任务类型和数据特性，而非默认使用最后一层，这为PLM在蛋白质工程和生物学研究中的实际应用提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Protein language models (PLMs) have transferred the latest advances from natural language processing to computational biology. These models, trained on large corpora of protein sequence data, are widely used to translate amino acid sequences into latent-space embeddings, ready for use in diverse downstream tasks (DTs). By a common consensus, embeddings from the model's last layer are used, and the model's internal behavior remains poorly understood. We analyzed 13 PLMs across 15 DTs from 11 datasets to investigate the informativeness of embeddings created in intermediate PLM layers. We trained probe models on embeddings from each layer, compared their performance, and computed characteristics of the latent spaces they span to estimate the information they contain, and found that the last layers of PLMs rarely contained embeddings that led to the best results on downstream tasks. Furthermore, we identified a connection between DTs and the distribution across PLMs' layers of the relevant information to predict that task. For example, similarity between the pre-training objective and the objective of predicting properties of individual residues leads to a steady increase in understanding of such tasks across the layers of PLMs. On the other hand, for whole-protein tasks, we observe that the dataset, rather than the task itself, defines PLMs' ability to perform well on a DT. Embeddings from shallow layers of PLMs perform better for datasets that contain deep mutational scan (DMS) data, while datasets containing diverse natural proteins find most useful embeddings in the models' deeper layers. Additionally, we discover that the performance of PLMs drops significantly when tasks are introduced for artificial proteins.

</details>


### [105] [Confidence Calibration of Deep Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12100)
*Coby Penso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文针对高可靠性场景中深度模型的置信度校准问题，在标签噪声、无监督域适应和隐私保护三种现实条件下分别提出校准方法，包括基于噪声模型的置信度重构、噪声感知的保形预测、无需目标标签的域适应校准，以及局部差分隐私保形预测框架，实现可靠且隐私保护的深度模型预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法通常假设可访问干净验证数据，但现实场景中标签噪声和域偏移普遍存在，导致标准方法产生误导性的置信度估计。在高风险应用中，可靠的置信度估计与预测本身同等重要，因此需要开发在非理想条件下依然有效的校准方法。

Method: 本文提出了三类方法：(1) 标签噪声下的校准：利用估计的噪声模型建模有噪声与干净标签分布之间的关系，重构无噪声的置信度估计，并扩展至保形预测以在噪声下估计干净的符合性分数；(2) 无监督域适应下的校准：从源域性能和域差异估计目标域准确率，实现无需目标标签的校准；(3) 隐私保护下的保形预测：提出局部差分隐私保形预测框架，在保证隐私的同时维持有效的置信度量化，兼顾隐私、计算可行性和预测可靠性。

Result: 所提出的方法在标签噪声、域偏移和隐私约束条件下均能提供可靠的置信度估计，保形预测方法在噪声和隐私保护设置下保持了有效的覆盖率保证，并实现了隐私保护与预测可靠性之间的平衡。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了校准理论与安全关键应用实际部署之间的鸿沟，为可靠、隐私保护且对噪声具有鲁棒性的神经网络预测提供了系统性解决方案，推动了校准方法在复杂现实条件下的实用化。

Abstract: In high-stakes applications, reliable confidence estimates are as important as the predictions themselves. Confidence calibration ensures that predicted probabilities reflect the likelihood of correctness, making it essential for safe deployment of deep learning models. However, existing methods typically assume access to clean validation data, which is often unrealistic due to label noise and domain shifts. This thesis develops methods for improving calibration under these conditions.
  First, we address calibration under label noise. Standard methods can produce misleading confidence estimates when labels are unreliable. We propose a framework that uses an estimated noise model to reconstruct noise-free confidence estimates by modeling the relationship between noisy and clean label distributions. We extend this approach to Conformal Prediction (CP), which provides set-valued predictions with guaranteed coverage. Our noise-aware CP method estimates clean conformity scores despite label noise, enabling reliable uncertainty quantification. Next, we study calibration in unsupervised domain adaptation, where a model trained on a labeled source domain is adapted to an unlabeled target domain. Since labeled target data are unavailable, we estimate target-domain accuracy from source performance and domain discrepancies, enabling calibration without target labels. We also consider privacy-preserving settings in which user labels and model outputs must remain protected. We propose a locally differentially private conformal prediction framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification while maintaining privacy guarantees and balancing privacy, computational feasibility, and prediction reliability.
  Our results bridge calibration theory and practical deployment in safety-critical applications, contributing to reliable, privacy-preserving, and noise-resilient neural network predictions.

</details>


### [106] [Beyond Parameter Space: NTK-Guided Personalized Aggregation for Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12108)
*Mirko Konstantin,Stefan Zachow,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LIGHTYEAR是一个联邦学习框架，通过NTK（神经正切核）在函数空间而非参数空间中评估客户端更新的预测行为相似性，利用P2P拓扑让每个客户端基于私有验证数据选择对其目标域有益的更新并进行正则化聚合，在五个数据集和九个基线方法上持续优于集中式FL基线和现有P2P方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习中的更新选择方法多在参数空间比较模型参数或梯度，但在数据异质、非IID环境下，参数空间相似性无法准确反映预测行为，导致与客户端目标域不一致的更新（如异质数据或故障客户端产生的更新）会降低本地模型性能。

Method: LIGHTYEAR采用函数空间中的更新选择：基于NTK的协议分数（agreement score）刻画模型参数的预测行为，为每个客户端确定个性化聚合集；利用P2P拓扑让客户端直接交换更新并在私有验证数据上评估传入模型，仅选择对自身目标域有益的更新，并通过正则化聚合规则提高异质性下的稳定性。

Result: 在五个数据集和九个基线方法上，LIGHTYEAR在性能上一致优于集中式联邦学习基线和现有P2P方法。

Conclusion: LIGHTYEAR通过将更新选择从参数空间转移到函数空间，结合NTK协议分数和P2P拓扑，有效解决了异质环境下联邦学习更新选择不准确的问题，为个性化联邦聚合提供了更具表达力的准则。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed clients while keeping data local. A central challenge is determining which client updates are beneficial for aggregation with respect to each client's target domain. Existing methods typically address this problem in parameter space by comparing model parameters or gradients. However, parameter-space similarity can be a poor proxy for predictive behavior, especially under heterogeneous, non-IID data. Consequently, updates that are misaligned with a client's target domain, including those caused by heterogeneous data or malfunctioning clients, may degrade local model performance.
  We propose Local Inference Guided Aggregation for Heterogeneous Training Environments to Yield Enhancement Through Agreement and Regularization (LIGHTYEAR), a federated learning framework that performs update selection in function space. LIGHTYEAR uses an NTK-based agreement score to characterize predictive behavior and determine a personalized aggregation set for each client. By relating model parameters to local predictive responses, the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) provides a more expressive criterion for update selection than parameter-space similarity alone.
  Because function-space information is not available before aggregation in conventional centralized FL, LIGHTYEAR uses a peer-to-peer (P2P) topology in which clients exchange updates directly and evaluate incoming models on private validation data. Each client selects only updates that are beneficial for its own target domain and aggregates them using a regularized rule that improves stability under heterogeneity.
  Across five datasets and nine baseline methods, LIGHTYEAR consistently outperforms centralized FL baselines and existing P2P approaches.

</details>


### [107] [Attractor Image-Based Deep Learning of Arterial Pulse Waves for Age Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12117)
*Sara Vardanega,Patrick Segers,Philip Aston,Ernst Rietzschel,Jordi Alastruey,Manasi Nandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用SPAR方法将光电容积脉搏波（PPG）和动脉张力测量法获得的脉搏波时间序列数据转换为图像，并训练卷积神经网络将健康受试者分为两个年龄相近的组（35-40岁和50-55岁）。模型在内部和外部测试集上一致表现良好，F1分数均超过70%，表明SPAR衍生的脉搏波图像即使在年龄相近的健康成年人中也包含可区分的形态学特征，为利用智能可穿戴设备进行早期风险检测奠定了概念验证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动脉脉搏波形态随年龄演化，反映心血管系统的结构和功能变化，因此血管年龄是心血管健康的重要替代标志物。过早的血管老化可能提示疾病风险增加，脉搏波分析有望支持无症状成年人的风险分层，但需要一种有效的方法从脉搏波数据中提取可区分的特征。

Method: 采用Symmetric Projection Attractor Reconstruction (SPAR)方法将PPG和动脉张力测量的脉搏波时间序列数据转换为图像，使用这些SPAR图像训练卷积神经网络（CNN），将健康受试者分类为两个年龄相近的组（35-40岁和50-55岁）。

Result: 模型在内部和外部测试集上均展现一致的分类性能，PPG和动脉张力测量信号的F1分数均高于70%。

Conclusion: SPAR衍生的脉搏波图像即使在年龄相近的健康成年人中也包含可区分的形态学特征。该概念验证为未来利用SPAR结合智能可穿戴设备进行早期风险检测的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Arterial pulse waveform morphology evolves with age, reflecting structural and functional changes in the cardiovascular system. Thus, vascular age is a valuable surrogate marker of cardiovascular health, and premature vascular ageing can indicate increased disease risk. Pulse wave analysis could support risk stratification in otherwise asymptomatic adults. We transformed pulse wave time-series data from photoplethysmography (PPG) and arterial tonometry into images, using the Symmetric Projection Attractor Reconstruction (SPAR) method. These SPAR images were used to train a convolutional neural network to classify healthy subjects into two closely spaced age groups (35-40 and 50-55 years). The model demonstrated consistent classification performance across internal and external test sets, achieving F1 scores above 70% for both PPG and tonometry signals. These results suggest that SPAR-derived pulse wave images contain discriminative morphological features even among healthy adults close in age. This proof-of-concept lays the groundwork for future research into the use of SPAR for early risk detection using smart wearables.

</details>


### [108] [Adversarial Resilience of Poisson-Process Submodular Maximization over Matroids: From Robust Offline Optimization to Full-Bandit Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12134)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一般拟阵约束下的非负子模最大化问题，在离线算法面临任意受控取值预言机的假设下，证明了Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson过程(SGS-Poisson)的对抗鲁棒性定理，并据此推导出全赌博机CMAB算法的近似遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中，离线子模最大化算法常通过有噪声或受控的取值预言机获取函数值，而非精确预言机。然而现有研究缺乏对这类受控预言机下算法性能保持性的系统分析，尤其是针对非单调子模目标函数和一般拟阵约束。

Method: 提出SGS-Poisson算法，引入"Spiteful交换规则"和"Spiteful丢弃步骤"，在保持泊松强度不变的前提下，证明其在任意满足有界误差(ξ)的受控预言机下仍保持1/e(非单调)和1-1/e(单调)的极限近似因子；进一步通过离线到在线约简获得全赌博机CMAB算法。

Result: 在受控预言机下，算法分别实现期望值至少(1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)和(1-1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)，使用O~(nk²ε⁻²)次预言机调用；全赌博机CMAB算法的近似遗憾因子为1/e和1-1/e，遗憾为O~(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})。

Conclusion: SGS-Poisson算法对受控预言机具有对抗鲁棒性，无需修改其核心机制即可保持最优近似保证；离线到在线的约简为一般拟阵约束下的子模奖励全赌博机学习提供了首个具有精确最优近似-遗憾权衡的方案。

Abstract: We study nonnegative submodular maximization subject to a general matroid when the offline algorithm is given an arbitrary controlled value oracle. Our main result is an adversarial resilience theorem for the Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process (SGS-Poisson): without modifying its Poisson intensity, single-element exchange rule, or spiteful drop step, the algorithm retains limiting approximation factors $1/e$ for non-monotone objectives and $1-1/e$ for monotone objectives. More precisely, under every controlled oracle $\widehat f$ satisfying $|\widehat f(S)-f(S)|\le ξ$ for every set $S$, our implementation returns a feasible set with expected value at least $(1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$ and $(1-1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$, respectively, using $\widetilde O(nk^2\varepsilon^{-2})$ oracle calls. As a consequence, the offline-to-online reduction yields full-bandit CMAB algorithms for general matroid-constrained submodular rewards with exact limiting approximation-regret factors $1/e$ and $1-1/e$ and $\widetilde O(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})$ regret.

</details>


### [109] [HYDRA: Hyperbolic Dynamic Representation Architecture for Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12194)
*Zhao Su,Yuxin Xia,Haoran Li,Jun Shen,Qi Zhu,Qingguo Zhou,Binbin Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HYDRA，一种参数高效的KAN超双曲扩展架构，在庞加莱球中结合样条函数学习与低秩原型块，显著减少参数冗余并提升表示可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN通过用可学习的单变量函数替代标量权重来增强非线性函数逼近能力，但每个连接分配独立函数会导致严重的参数冗余，限制其可扩展性和效率。

Method: HYDRA将向量值输入映射到有界的双曲潜在空间（庞加莱球），在切空间内执行KAN风格的更新，并使用低秩原型块在隐藏维度间共享函数变换。双曲表示提供用于解释的径向坐标结构，半径控制通过防止边界饱和来提升训练稳定性。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，HYDRA在提升参数效率和表示可解释性的同时，持续取得具有竞争力或更优的预测性能。

Conclusion: HYDRA通过双曲几何与低秩共享机制有效解决了KAN的参数冗余问题，兼顾了性能、效率与可解释性。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) enhance nonlinear function approximation by replacing scalar weights with learnable univariate functions. However, assigning an independent function to every connection results in substantial parameter redundancy, limiting their scalability and efficiency. To reduce this redundancy, we introduce \textbf{HY}perbolic \textbf{D}ynamic \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{A}rchitecture (HYDRA), a parameter-efficient hyperbolic extension of KAN that combines spline-based functional learning with representations in the Poincaré ball. HYDRA maps vector-valued inputs into a bounded hyperbolic latent space, performs KAN-style updates in tangent space, and employs a low-rank prototype block to share functional transformations across hidden dimensions. The resulting hyperbolic representations provide a structured radial coordinate for interpretation, while radius control improves training stability by preventing boundary saturation. Extensive experiments across eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that HYDRA consistently achieves competitive or superior predictive performance while improving parameter efficiency and representation interpretability.

</details>


### [110] [ScreenShot: A Foundation Model for Few-Shot Combination Drug Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12219)
*Antoine de Mathelin,Christopher Tosh,Wesley Tansey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ScreenShot，一个基于层级Transformer的预训练模型，在40个药物筛选数据集（涵盖3,700种药物和6,000个生物样本）上预训练，通过上下文学习（in-context learning）直接基于功能测量数据预测新患者对联合用药的反应，无需微调和分子谱分析。在四个保留数据集上优于所有基线，并利用内部表征驱动的加权k-means++主动学习策略，以三分之一的预算实现与均匀筛选相同的命中检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物联合治疗可降低耐药风险，但组合筛选空间巨大，实验成本高、耗时且技术上常不可行。现有预测模型通常需要每个样本的分子谱分析及逐队列训练，在时间和组织样本稀缺时适用性受限。因此需要一种无需分子谱分析和微调、可直接从功能测量中预测联合用药反应的方法。

Method: ScreenShot采用层级Transformer架构，其结构与筛选数据的嵌套特性（药物组合嵌套于样本内）相匹配。模型在40个药物筛选数据集上预训练，覆盖3,700种药物和6,000个生物样本。通过少样本上下文学习，模型从新患者的少量观察中直接预测其对联合治疗的反应，无需微调或分子谱分析。此外，利用模型的内部表征驱动加权k-means++主动学习策略，指导实验选择。

Result: 在四个保留数据集上，ScreenShot在预测准确性和选择性有效治疗鉴定方面均优于所有基线。主动学习策略方面，以三分之一的实验预算即可达到与均匀筛选相同的命中检测效果。代码和交互式仪表盘已在GitHub公开。

Conclusion: ScreenShot展示了大规模预训练结合上下文学习范式在药物联合筛选中的潜力，能够在不依赖分子谱分析和微调的情况下对新样本进行预测，并且其内部表征可有效指导实验设计，大幅降低筛选成本。

Abstract: Treating patients with combinations of drugs reduces the risk of resistance to any individual drug. Finding effective combinations is difficult because the large search space makes combinatorial screens prohibitively expensive, time consuming, and often technically infeasible. Predictive models can fill this gap, yet existing methods typically require molecular profiling of each sample and per-cohort training, limiting their applicability when time and tissue are scarce. To address this challenge, we introduce ScreenShot, a hierarchical transformer pretrained on 40 drug screening datasets covering 3,700 drugs and 6,000 biological samples, whose architecture mirrors the nested structure of screening data. Given a few-shot context of observations from a new patient, ScreenShot predicts the response of the sample to combination therapies through in-context learning, operating directly on functional measurements with no fine-tuning and no molecular profiling. On four held-out datasets, ScreenShot outperforms all baselines in both prediction accuracy and identification of selectively effective treatments. ScreenShot's internal representations are directly useful for experimental design: we use them to drive a weighted k-means++ active learning strategy that selects which experiments to run, achieving the same hit detection as uniform screening with a third of the budget. Source code and interactive dashboard: https://github.com/tansey-lab/screenshot.

</details>


### [111] [An Efficient Near-Optimal Algorithm for Adversarial $m$-Set Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12231)
*Francesco Bacchiocchi,Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性组合赌博机问题（m-集动作），提出了一种计算高效且无需显式枚举动作集的算法，其高概率遗憾界与EXP3-KW算法匹配，解决了Maiti等人提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性组合赌博机中，动作集合大小为K=C(d,m)，可能呈指数级增长，直接应用有限动作集的算法（如EXP3-KW）需要指数级空间和时间。而每个动作的损失本质上由d维物品损失向量决定，这为设计更高效的算法提供了结构上的可能性。Maiti等人此前提出了该开放问题，即是否存在一个不用枚举动作集的多项式时间算法。

Method: 提出一种计算高效的算法，利用m-集动作的结构特性，用d个参数来表示每个采样分布，从而避免显式枚举包含K=C(d,m)个元素的动作集。该算法在每一轮中通过这d个参数进行采样和更新，在多项式时间内运行。算法针对自适应非预见性对手，保证了高概率遗憾界。

Result: 算法保证以至少1-δ的概率达到遗憾界R_T = O(√(dT log(K/δ)))，这与有限动作集EXP3-KW算法（Zimmert和Lattimore）的高概率遗憾界相匹配。但EXP3-KW的直接实现可能需要指数级空间，而本文算法仅需多项式时间和空间。

Conclusion: 该算法以多项式复杂度实现了与指数空间算法相同的最优高概率遗憾界，从而解决了Maiti等人提出的开放问题，证明了在对抗性组合赌博机中，利用线性动作结构可以实现计算效率与统计最优性的统一。

Abstract: We study adversarial combinatorial bandits with $m$-set actions, where at each round the learner selects $m$ out of $d$ items and observes only the aggregate loss of the selected items. The resulting action set contains $K=\binom{d}{m}$ elements and can therefore be exponentially large. Nevertheless, the loss of every action is determined by the same $d$-dimensional vector of item losses. We propose a computationally efficient algorithm that exploits this structure without explicitly enumerating the action set. Against adaptive non-anticipating adversaries, it guarantees, with probability at least $1-δ$, regret against the best fixed action of \[
  R_T =
  O\left(\sqrt{dT\log(K/δ)}\right). \] This matches the high-probability regret bound of the finite-action EXP3-KW algorithm of Zimmert and Lattimore, whose direct implementation may require exponential space. Our algorithm instead represents each sampling distribution with $d$ parameters and runs in polynomial time without enumerating the action set. Thus, it resolves the open problem posed by Maiti et al.

</details>


### [112] [Calibration Bets on the Past: Post-Training Quantization for Financial Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12259)
*Junyi Ye,Ivy Gateri Wanjiku*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了金融预测模型中激活校准对后训练量化（PTQ）的影响。在S&P 500横截面波动率预测场景下，覆盖7种神经网络架构、8个回测年份（2018-2025）和560个训练模型，发现激活校准在8位量化时影响甚微，但在4位量化时成为预测性能的主要决定因素。默认的绝对最大值校准在4位静态量化下会造成11-62%的信息系数损失，而百分位校准可恢复53-94%的退化。激活范围偏好随市场周期变化，激活校准是4位PTQ中第一级的部署决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融预测模型通常在完整精度下开发，但生产部署常需低精度推理以降低内存和计算成本。后训练量化（PTQ）无需重训即可实现低精度部署，但可靠的激活量化需要校准——即在部署前从历史数据估计激活范围并固定用于未来推理。这一部署选择对金融预测的重要性此前未被充分理解，本文旨在系统性地填补这一空白。

Method: 在S&P 500横截面波动率预测任务上，对7种代表性神经网络架构进行了系统的PTQ激活校准研究。量化覆盖权重和激活（W4A4和W8A8），对比绝对最大值（abs-max）校准与百分位校准方法，使用8个走前（walk-forward）测试年份（2018-2025），共训练和评估560个模型，以预测信息系数（IC）为主要评估指标。

Result: 研究发现：在8位量化下，激活校准对预测性能影响很小；但在4位量化下，激活校准成为预测性能的主要决定因素。默认abs-max校准下，4位静态权重和激活量化在受影响架构中导致11-62%的IC损失；替换为百分位校准可在四个受影响最严重的架构中恢复53-94%的退化。此外，最优激活范围随市场周期变化——窄范围在典型市场条件下有利于分辨率，但当测试期市场离散度超过校准历史窗口时优势减弱。

Conclusion: 激活校准是金融预测中可靠4位PTQ的第一级（first-class）部署决策，不能简单依赖默认配置。当4位量化仍存在显著性能退化时，应选择8位激活或仅权重量化（weight-only 4-bit）作为更稳健的部署方案。

Abstract: Financial forecasting models are typically developed in full precision, yet production deployment often requires low-precision inference to reduce memory and computational cost. Post-training quantization (PTQ) enables such deployment without retraining. However, reliable activation quantization requires calibration: activation ranges are estimated from historical data before deployment and then remain fixed during future inference. The importance of this deployment choice for financial forecasting remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of activation calibration for PTQ in cross-sectional volatility forecasting on the S&P 500. Our evaluation covers seven representative neural architectures, eight walk-forward test years (2018-2025), and 560 trained models. We find that activation calibration has little effect at 8 bits but becomes the primary determinant of predictive performance at 4 bits. Under default absolute-maximum (abs-max) calibration, static 4-bit quantization of both weights and activations removes 11-62% of the full-precision mean information coefficient in affected architectures. Replacing abs-max with percentile calibration recovers 53-94% of this degradation in the four most affected architectures. The preferred activation range also varies across market periods. Narrow ranges improve resolution under typical market conditions but lose part of their advantage when test-period market dispersion exceeds the calibration history. These findings show that activation calibration is a first-class deployment decision for reliable 4-bit PTQ in financial forecasting. When substantial degradation remains, 8-bit activations or weight-only 4-bit quantization provide more robust deployment choices.

</details>


### [113] [Earth observation embeddings are effective sub-grid descriptors for probabilistic weather downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12271)
*Pedro Sousa,Will Tebbutt,Sadiq Jaffer,Robin Young,Anil Madhavapeddy,Richard E. Turner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用地球观测基础模型（TESSERA嵌入）作为学习到的局部地表描述符，替代传统手工设计的地形特征，用于概率天气降尺度。该方法在五个气候多样区域中，显著提升了ERA5再分析产品和Aurora AI天气预报在2米温度和10米风速上的降尺度技能（CRPS分别改善11.5%和6.2%），并能在新部署站点上保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球天气再分析和预报在粗网格上解析大气状态，但站点尺度的应用需要任意位置的预测，而近地表条件依赖于未解析的地形和陆面属性。现有概率降尺度方法依赖手工设计的地形描述符，本文旨在探索地球观测基础模型能否提供可迁移的次网格地表表示来替代人工特征。

Method: 在卷积条件神经过程（convolutional conditional neural process）的降尺度框架中，引入通过学习得到的局部地表描述符：将10米分辨率的TESSERA嵌入补丁压缩为紧凑向量，与25 km分辨率的ERA5粗网格场共同输入模型。该方法在五个气候多样区域、空间和时间上均未见的站点上进行评估。

Result: 嵌入特征在2米温度和10米风速上均提升了点预测和概率预测技能（CRPS分别改善11.5%和6.2%）。分析表明：地形对温度次网格结构的解释力更强，而TESSERA为风速提供了额外的地表信息。当粗网格输入从ERA5切换到Aurora AI预报模型，以及预测无区域历史的新部署站点时，改进仍然保持。

Conclusion: 首次证明长期时间尺度的地球观测嵌入能够支持短时间尺度的天气降尺度，其中次网格偏差由持续性地表属性系统性地结构化。这一发现为天气降尺度提供了一种可迁移、无监督的地表表示新范式，可替代手工设计的地形描述符。

Abstract: Global weather reanalyses and forecasts resolve the evolving atmospheric state on coarse grids, but site-specific applications require predictions at arbitrary locations where near-surface conditions also depend on unresolved terrain and land-surface properties. Existing probabilistic downscalers address this gap using hand-crafted topographic descriptors. We ask instead whether Earth observation foundation models can provide transferable sub-grid surface representations for probabilistic weather downscaling.
  We augment a convolutional conditional neural process that downscales coarse ERA5 reanalysis fields at ~25 km resolution with a learned local surface descriptor, obtained by compressing a patch of TESSERA embeddings at 10 m resolution. Although these embeddings summarise surface conditions over annual timescales, they improve downscaling of instantaneous 2 m temperature and 10 m wind speed by encoding persistent surface properties that capture a location's departure from the coarse-grid atmospheric state. Across five climatically diverse regions, the embedding improves point and probabilistic skill at stations held out in both space and time, overall improving CRPS skill by 11.5% for 2 m temperature and 6.2% for 10 m wind speed. We further analyse how its contribution differs by variable, finding that topography explains more of temperature's sub-grid structure, while TESSERA provides additional surface information for wind speed.
  These improvements persist when the coarse input is changed from ERA5 to forecasts from the Aurora AI forecasting model, and when predicting at newly deployed stations with no regional history. To our knowledge, this is the first evidence that long-timescale Earth-observation embeddings can support short-timescale weather downscaling where sub-grid departures are systematically structured by persistent surface properties.

</details>


### [114] [A Framework for Designing Reward Functions: From Objectives to Features to Human-Aligned Reward Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12302)
*Di Yang Shi,W. Bradley Knox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套正式流程，使非专家用户能够基于自然语言任务描述，通过三个步骤实例化并迭代符合人类偏好的线性奖励函数：提炼目标并推导可测结果变量、通过最小费用部分覆盖选择因果代表性的奖励项、通过偏好询问拟合权重。该方法首次保证确定性无冲突的可行权重区域，并以O(n log κ)次偏好查询收敛到期望精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励函数设计是强化学习与机器人任务中的核心瓶颈，传统方法通常需要专家手动设计奖励或大量偏好数据，缺乏系统化、可迭代且对人友好的流程。本文旨在为非专家提供一种形式化的奖励设计方法，确保生成的奖励函数能够对齐给定轨迹偏好顺序。

Method: 方法由三步组成：(1) 从自然语言任务描述中提炼基本目标并推导可测结果变量（提供引导式工作流）；(2) 将奖励项选择问题转化为因果DAG上的最小费用部分覆盖问题，通过最大流算法在多项式时间内求解；(3) 将权重拟合形式化为凸可行性问题，通过分离预言机（separation oracle）方法迭代缩小可行权重区域，仅需O(n log κ)次偏好查询。

Result: 该方法在理论上保证了权重可行区域始终保持确定性无冲突，并能通过分离预言机以O(n log κ)次偏好查询将可行区域收敛到期望容差。这是首个满足这些性质的奖励设计方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为奖励设计提供了首个端到端的形式化流程，结合因果推理、组合优化与凸几何方法，使非专家也能够在有限偏好交互下构建高质量的人类对齐奖励函数，为可解释且可迭代的奖励学习奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a formal process to enable non-experts to instantiate and iterate on human-aligned reward functions, i.e. reward functions that adhere to a given preference ordering over trajectories. Given a task described in natural language, our process produces a linear reward function in three steps: distill the task's objectives into a set of fundamental objectives and derive measurable outcome variables that capture those fundamental objectives, select a causally representative subset of outcome variables as the reward terms, and fit weights to those reward terms via preference elicitation. Our contributions describe the first step and formalize the latter two steps. The first is a guided workflow for deriving outcome variables. The second is a reduction of reward term selection to minimum-cost partial cover on a causal DAG, solved in polynomial time via max-flow. The third is a geometric framing of weight fitting as a convex feasibility problem iteratively narrowed by preference queries, solved by existing separation oracle methods. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first reward-design method that maintains a deterministically conflict-free feasible weight region, narrowed to a desired tolerance via a separation oracle with O(n log κ) preference queries.

</details>


### [115] [Redistribution-based Cost Inference Improves Sparse Safe Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12306)
*Ebenezer Gelo,Geraud Nangue Tasse,Steven James,Benjamin Rosman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RCI框架，通过回报分解将轨迹级稀疏停止反馈转化为稠密每步成本，用于安全离线强化学习，理论上保持CMDP可行策略集与最优拉格朗日量不变，实验验证其违规率显著低于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全离线强化学习通常假设可获取稠密的每步成本标注，但实际中监督者只能提供轨迹级停止反馈（首个不安全转移处的二值信号），缺乏每步归因。这构成了时序信用分配问题，需要将稀疏信号转化为稠密成本以支持约束策略学习。

Method: 提出Redistribution-based Cost Inference (RCI)框架：通过回报分解技术将稀疏的轨迹级停止反馈转化为稠密的每步成本，再在增强数据集上训练带约束的离线策略。理论上证明回报等价的重分配保持CMDP的可行策略集与最优拉格朗日量不变，保证转换无损；实践上改善成本评价值函数的学习条件。

Result: 在高速公路驾驶与机器人操作任务上，RCI相比稀疏信号基线与基于分类器的基线均实现显著更低的违规率，且对异质数据集组成与标签噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 回报等价的重分配使得成本推断转换在理论上无损（保持可行策略集与最优拉格朗日），同时在实际中带来更好的成本批判学习条件，为仅有粗粒度监督信号的现实安全离线RL提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Safe offline RL typically assumes access to dense per-step cost annotations, but in practice supervisors provide only trajectory-level stop-feedback: a binary signal at the first unsafe transition, with no per-step attribution. We frame this as a temporal credit assignment problem and propose the Redistribution-based Cost Inference (RCI) framework, which converts sparse stop-feedback into dense per-step costs via return decomposition, then trains a constrained offline policy on the augmented dataset. We show that return-equivalent redistribution preserves the feasible policy set and the optimal Lagrangian in a CMDP, establishing that the transformation is lossless in theory while yielding better-conditioned cost critic learning in practice. Experiments on highway driving and robotic manipulation demonstrate substantially lower violation rates than sparse and classifier-based baselines, with robustness to heterogeneous dataset compositions and label noise.

</details>


### [116] [AI4AI at Test-Time: Strong-to-Weak Capability Transfer via Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12307)
*Cheng Qian,Wenting Zhao,Liangwei Yang,Heng Wang,Jielin Qiu,Heng Ji,Silvio Savarese,Huan Wang,Shelby Heinecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"强到弱脚手架"（strong-to-weak scaffolding）方法，研究是否可以在测试期间而非训练期间将大模型的能力转移给小模型。通过在五个心智理论基准上使用强构建器模型为弱目标模型设计推理时测试框架（harness），无需任何参数更新即可将目标模型性能从0.49提高到0.91，几乎翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏（distillation）通过训练时方法（如教师强制、在策略蒸馏）将大模型能力转移给小模型，但这些方法都需要更新小模型参数。作者质疑能否在测试时（test time）实现这种能力转移，从而避免训练成本，探索一种无需重训练的能力迁移范式。

Method: 采用强构建器模型（builder）为弱目标模型（target）构建推理时测试框架（inference-time harness）。具体流程为：构建器模型使用5%的数据作为验证集，通过多轮迭代（multiple rounds）不断改进harness，最终在完整测试集上评估。实验在四个代表性的心智理论（Theory-of-Mind）基准上进行。

Result: 测试时能力转移非常有效，目标模型平均性能从0.49提升到0.91（近翻倍）。分析表明收益主要来自：将不稳定的模型推理卸载到确定性代码中、基准特定的路由（routing）以及严格的答案格式强制，而非鼓励目标模型扩展推理或更广泛采样。进一步发现：构建器模型的推理努力能单调提升harness质量；平台效应相对于构建器模型自身能力影响较小；越弱的模型获得收益越大。

Conclusion: 推理时harness设计是传统训练时蒸馏的重要补充，使强模型无需重训练即可将认知结构转移给弱模型。这一发现为能力迁移提供了新的范式，降低了小模型部署的门槛。

Abstract: Recent work on distillation transfers the capabilities of large models to smaller ones often by updating the latter's parameters, through teacher forcing, on-policy distillation, and related training-time methods. In this paper, we ask whether such transfer can instead occur at test time. We study strong-to-weak scaffolding: whether a stronger builder model can construct inference-time harnesses that help a weaker target model solve tasks more reliably without any parameter updates. Using four representative Theory-of-Mind benchmarks, each builder model uses 5% of the data as a validation set to iteratively refine its harness over multiple rounds, after which the finalized harness is evaluated on the full test set. Empirically, this form of test-time capability transfer is highly effective, nearly doubling average target-model performance from 0.49 to 0.91. Our analysis shows that the gains come primarily from offloading unstable model reasoning into deterministic code, benchmark-specific routing, and strict answer-format enforcement, rather than from encouraging the target model to reason more extensively or sample more broadly. We further find that builder-model reasoning effort improves harness quality monotonically, platform effects are modest relative to the builder model's own capability, and weaker target models receive the largest gains. These results suggest that inference-time harness design is an important complement to conventional training-time distillation, enabling strong models to transfer cognitive structure to weaker models without retraining.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Dynamic Governance of Multi-LLM Agent Systems for Collaborative Conversational Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11207)
*Alexander Liss,Nicholas Desmond,Santiago Gil Gallego*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对两个目标对立的LLM代理在多轮交互中因缺乏共享目标函数而导致对话崩溃的问题，提出了Experience Orchestrator（EO）控制理论治理层。EO通过Contextual Bandit、PID控制器和POMDP信念追踪器三种机制，在60,000次模拟中使高意图顾问联系率提升32个百分点（78.1% vs 46.1%），其中CB变体选择解释了97%的因子间结果方差。研究发现治理层对无自然转化倾向的访客是系统功能性与非功能性的关键差异，而对本已接近对齐的访客，朴素LLM的移情默认行为已基本足够。所有结论基于LLM间模拟，PID控制器未针对真实人类不确定性校准，实时流量的验证是下一步关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当两个目标结构上对立的LLM代理在多轮对话中交互时，缺乏共享目标函数会导致对话崩溃而非竞争：访客屈服、站点代理停止变换策略、对话在不达成任何一方既定目标的情况下终止。论文动机在于探究是否可以用控制理论的治理层替代缺失的目标函数，以维持跨轮对话的良性推进。

Method: 在模拟金融服务环境（站点代理引导访客联系顾问，访客保持心理现实主义的抗拒）中引入Experience Orchestrator（EO）治理层，通过三种机制治理联合轨迹：（1）Contextual Bandit（CB）基于真实网络分析数据校准的选择内容臂；（2）PID控制器通过动态模式约束强制行为一致性；（3）POMDP信念追踪器维护访客意图的概率模型。

Result: 在60,000次模拟中，EO在高意图顾问联系率上实现+32个百分点的提升（78.1% vs 朴素LLM对照组的46.1%）。CB变体选择解释了因子间结果方差的97%，确认治理策略而非环境初始条件决定轨迹终点。个性层分析揭示两种截然不同的机制：对无自然转化倾向的访客，治理层是系统功能性的决定因素；对接近对齐的访客，朴素LLM的移情默认即足够。

Conclusion: 控制理论治理层可以有效替代缺失的共享目标函数，使目标对立的LLM代理间对话保持功能性而非崩溃。但所有发现限于LLM到LLM的模拟条件；PID控制器尚未针对真实人类不可预测性校准，在真实流量上验证EO是关键的下一步研究。

Abstract: When two LLM agents with structurally opposed objectives interact across multiple turns, the absence of a shared goal function produces not competition but collapse: the visitor capitulates, the site agent stops varying its approach, and the conversation terminates without achieving either agent's stated objective. This paper asks whether a control-theoretic governance layer can substitute for that missing goal function. The Experience Orchestrator (EO) addresses this in a simulated financial services environment where a site agent guides a visitor toward advisor contact while the visitor maintains psychologically realistic resistance. EO governs the joint trajectory through three mechanisms: a Contextual Bandit (CB) that selects content arms calibrated from real-world web analytics, a PID controller that enforces behavioral consistency via dynamic schema constraints, and a POMDP belief tracker that maintains a probabilistic model of visitor intent. Across 60,000 simulations, EO achieves a +32 percentage point lift in high-intent advisor contact rate (78.1% vs. 46.1% over a naive LLM control), with CB variant selection accounting for 97% of between-factor outcome variance -- confirming that the governance policy, not environmental initial conditions, determines where trajectories end up. Persona-level analysis reveals two distinct regimes: for visitors with no natural inclination toward conversion, the governance layer is the difference between a functional system and a non-functional one; for visitors already near alignment, a naive LLM's empathetic defaults are largely sufficient. All findings are conditional on LLM-to-LLM simulation. The PID controller has not been calibrated against real human unpredictability, and validating EO on live traffic is the critical next step.

</details>


### [118] [Distribird: Literature-Informed Prior Distribution Design for Bayesian Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11210)
*Patrik P. Süli,György Eigner,Roland Hollós*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Distribird，一个基于多智能体的Web应用，用于从科学文献中自动构建过程模型的贝叶斯先验分布。它通过搜索文献、提取并加权报告值、AIC模型选择来拟合概率分布，并在文献缺失时回退到无信息先验，同时报告证据和置信度。评估显示其与单提示LLM基线在先验质量上相当，但在可追溯性、有效性验证和本地运行隐私保护上显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管贝叶斯校准需要每个模型参数的先验分布，但研究者几乎总是退回到均匀先验，因为从科学文献构建信息丰富的先验耗时且需要领域和统计双重专业知识。现有方法缺乏自动化工具来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: Distribird采用多智能体流水线：根据参数名称、物理描述和领域上下文搜索文献，提取报告值并按领域相关性加权，通过AIC模型选择拟合概率分布。无文献时回退到无信息先验。内置有效性层拒绝越界请求，所有语言模型调用均在本地运行。在24个参数、10个科学领域上，对三种开源模型（Qwen3.6 27B、Gemma 4 31B、Mistral Small 4 119B）与单提示LLM基线进行比较。

Result: 在先验质量上，完整流水线与单提示基线相当（匹配而非超越）；但在可追溯性上，每个先验均可追溯到具体论文和值；在有效性验证上，基线在30个模型-参数组合中的11个返回了自信但无根据的先验，而Distribird的有效性层拒绝产生越界先验；所有语言模型调用本地运行，保护了参数描述的隐私。

Conclusion: 作者认为对于科学研究而言，先验的可追溯性、范围有效性和本地隐私保护比边际的点估计精度提升更重要，Distribird在这些属性上显著优于基线。

Abstract: Bayesian calibration of process-based models requires a prior distribution for each model parameter. Despite decades of methodological work, researchers almost always fall back on uniform priors. The main reason is that building informative priors from scientific literature is slow and needs both domain and statistical expertise. We present \textbf{Distribird}, an agentic web application that automates this process. Given a parameter name, physical description, and domain context, Distribird deploys a multi-agent pipeline that searches the literature, extracts and weights reported values by domain relevance, and fits a probability distribution via AIC model selection. When no literature is available, the system falls back to sensible uninformative alternatives, and clearly reports both the evidence behind and the confidence level of every prior it produces. It is designed for the problems where the models have physically interpretable parameters, where domain knowledge exists in the published literature. We evaluate the tool on 24~parameters across 10 scientific domains comparing three open-weight models (Qwen3.6 27B, Gemma 4 31B, Mistral Small 4 119B) with a single-prompt LLM baseline. On prior quality the full pipeline \emph{matches} this baseline. Every prior is traced to the specific papers and values from which it was constructed; a built-in validity layer declines to produce priors for out-of-scope requests, whereas the single-prompt baseline returns confident but unfounded priors for them in 11 of 30~model--parameter cases; and every language-model call runs locally, so no parameter description or unpublished modelling detail is transmitted to a third-party LLM provider (only generated search terms reach the public literature databases). For scientific use, we argue these properties matter more than a marginal improvement in point-estimate accuracy.

</details>


### [119] [A Forced-Structure Reduction and Verifiable Bounds for Conway's 99-Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11211)
*Aalok Thakkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个自治AI研究代理对Conway 99图问题的系统性、可复现攻击。该问题询问参数为srg(99,14,1,2)的强正则图是否存在。贡献包括：循环图约束满足上界68.0%的穷举证明、通过强制结构约简将问题归约为84顶点上的12-正则图并编码为CP-SAT、验证过的规定自同构轨道存在性框架，以及一个69.43%的最佳验证工件，附带这是稳健前沿的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conway 99图问题是组合数学中一个著名的开放性存在性问题，涉及强正则图的存在性判定。该问题长期悬而未决，作者希望通过自治AI研究代理进行系统性、可复现的攻击，在部分学分评分指标下获得可验证的部分成果，并为最终存在性或不存在性证明提供结构性洞见。

Method: (1) 对Z/99上的循环图进行穷举分析，证明其满足的约束不超过68.0%（33/49个差分类），并验证另一个99阶阿贝尔群也有相同上界；(2) 利用λ=1使每个邻域成为完美匹配、μ=2使外顶点与非匹配邻居对一一对应，将存在性问题约简为84顶点上的12-正则图，编码到CP-SAT求解器并用srg(9,4,1,2)验证；(3) 建立规定自同构轨道存在性框架（无不动点和单不动点作用），在srg(9,4,1,2)和Paley图srg(13,6,2,3)上验证；(4) 获得最佳验证工件69.43%。

Result: 获得四个可验证贡献：循环图上界68.0%的穷举证明；84顶点12-正则图的CP-SAT约简；规定自同构轨道框架的验证；最佳工件69.43%。14种不同方法均未超过该值，表明这是稳健前沿。由于任何低于4950的可证明界都是不存在性证明，该前沿与开放问题紧密纠缠。

Conclusion: 研究将Conway 99图问题推进到可验证的部分解状态，通过结构约简、自同构分析和组合优化方法的结合，建立了69.43%的稳健求解前沿。这一结果表明该问题存在进一步深入的结构空间，任何突破当前前沿的可证明上界都将直接转化为非存在性证明。

Abstract: Conway's 99-graph problem asks whether a strongly regular graph with parameters $\mathrm{srg}(99,14,1,2)$ exists. We report a systematic, fully reproducible attack by an autonomous AI research agent, scored under the track's partial-credit metric. Our verifiable contributions are: (1) an exhaustive proof that no circulant graph on $\mathbb{Z}/99$ satisfies more than $3366/4950=68.0\%$ of the constraints ($33$ of $49$ difference-classes), with the same ceiling for the other abelian group of order $99$; (2) a forced-structure reduction: $λ=1$ makes each neighbourhood a perfect matching and $μ=2$ puts the outer vertices in bijection with non-matched neighbour-pairs, collapsing existence to a $12$-regular graph on $84$ vertices, encoded for CP-SAT and validated by recovering the unique $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$; (3) a validated prescribed-automorphism orbit-existence framework (fixed-point-free and single-fixed-point actions, checked on $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$ and the Paley graph $\mathrm{srg}(13,6,2,3)$), and (4) a best verified artifact at $69.43\%$, with evidence that this is a robust frontier (fourteen distinct methods, none exceeding it) entangled with the open question, since any provable bound below $4950$ is a non-existence proof.

</details>


### [120] [RecSys Factory: Bounding LLM Agent Autonomy to Decision Points in the Industrial Recommender Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11241)
*Dongyang Ao,Kaixiang Fang,Shijie Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RecSys Factory是一个部署于腾讯三条推荐业务线的LLM智能体平台，通过"决策点自治而非流水线自治"的设计理念，将运行时分解为三种宿主事件源、将能力分解为29文件技能生态系统和400条目PitfallStore，化解了自主性-确定性-效率三难困境；78天内记录1,624次CLI调度，78.6%成功聚合率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM智能体引入工业推荐系统运营面临自主性、确定性与效率之间的三难张力：通用自主性要求智能体零样本理解运营者意图并生成胶水代码，工业确定性要求模式一致的特性提取、无崩溃的A/B测试和无合规路径幻觉，端到端效率则要求资源开销可控。三者无法同时最大化，需要架构性解耦。

Method: 提出"决策点自治而非流水线自治"的设计原则，通过三大分解策略各释放三难困境的一极：(1) 运行时分解为三种宿主事件源（Claude Code Stop钩子、企业IM Webhooks、工作流调度器API），等待阶段无常驻守护进程，在94%的等待Spark/GPU作业墙钟时间内零CPU消耗；(2) 能力分解为29文件技能生态系统（8,971行SKILL.md），每个技能的陷阱表机械编译为400条PitfallStore，将自治性限制在预提交流水线内的有界类型化决策面；(3) 部署覆盖三条标签语义、A/B层拓扑和运营者画像各异的业务线，通过人机协同卡片协议保留人在诊断-执行边界。

Result: 平台在腾讯三条异构推荐业务线部署78天，记录1,624次CLI工具调度，聚合成功率达78.6%。三条业务线中两条观察到上线时间压缩（作为案例观察报告，非泛化声明，且未对照平台前基线）。8天16轮试点中验证了作为审计追踪原语的人机协同卡片协议（模式验证、幂等、可重放）。

Conclusion: RecSys Factory证明通过将自治性约束在决策点而非整个流水线，可以在工业推荐运营中同时兼顾足够的LLM自主性、系统确定性与运行时效率。其可复现的审计追踪机制和事件源驱动的无守护架构为工业LLM智能体部署提供了可行的范式参考。

Abstract: Deploying LLM agents into industrial recommender operations exposes a three-way tension we frame as the autonomy-determinism-efficiency trilemma: general autonomy (interpreting operator intent, generating glue code zero-shot), industrial determinism (schema-conforming feature extraction, non-crashing A/B, zero compliance-path hallucination), and end-to-end efficiency. Any two can be maximized against the third. We present RecSys Factory, an LLM-agent platform deployed for 78 days across three heterogeneous Tencent recommender business lines. The design principle is autonomy at decision points, not over pipelines, made concrete through three deconstructions that each discharge one vertex of the trilemma. Runtime is deconstructed into three host-emitted event sources (Claude Code Stop hooks, corporate-IM webhooks, workflow scheduler APIs): the platform carries no long-running daemon during the wait phase and consumes zero CPU during the 94% of wall-clock spent waiting on Spark or GPU jobs. Capability is deconstructed into a 29-file skill ecosystem (8,971 lines of SKILL.md) whose per-skill pitfall tables mechanically compile into a 400-entry PitfallStore, confining autonomy to bounded typed decision surfaces inside pre-committed pipelines. Deployment spans three business lines with disjoint label semantics, A/B layer topologies, and operator personas; an onboarding-time compression is observed on two of the three and is reported as a case-study observation, not a generalization claim, and not measured against a controlled pre-platform baseline. The human is retained at the diagnostic-versus-execution boundary via a human-in-the-loop card protocol, deployed as an audit-trail primitive (schema-validated, idempotent, replayable) and reported from an 8-day 16-run pilot. Across the 78-day window the platform recorded 1,624 CLI-tool dispatches at a 78.6% aggregate success rate.

</details>


### [121] [Detecting a Route Flip Is Easier Than Knowing Whether to Fix It: Causal Route-Mediated Damage in Quantized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11212)
*Parvel Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究Top-k Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)路由的不连续性导致的量化损伤问题——模拟4-bit KV-cache量化会使tokens跨越决策边界、翻转专家选择。作者不提出新缓解方法，而是提供因果分析框架（四跑装置量化路由介导分数RMF）、token级归因分解、以及跨三种架构的预注册探测。在OLMoE-1B-7B上发现约三分之一的量化损伤是路由介导的（RMF≈0.31），且部署路由器margin可检测翻转发生（AUC 0.772）但无法区分有害/有益翻转，形成"有益检测障碍"。所有假设、阈值和评估均在测量前预注册。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE路由的硬性top-k选择是不连续的，数值扰动（如KV-cache量化的读取噪声）会改变专家路由决策，从而引入传统离散化误差分析无法覆盖的额外损伤路径。作者旨在厘清"路由介导的损伤"在整个量化退化中占多大比例、通过何种机制传播，以及是否可能通过可观测的路由统计量选择性修复。

Method: 采用预注册的实验设计：（1）四-run装置量化路由介导分数RMF，将量化损伤分解为路由介导与非路由介导部分；（2）token级归因分解损伤机制；（3）跨三种架构的预注册探针验证；（4）使用受保护的BF16门控读取模拟4-bit KV-cache量化；（5）部署可观测路由器margin检测翻转发生；（6）同一checkpoint的flag-swap对照实验重定标归一化约定的作用；（7）真实int4 KV内核做兼容性验证。

Result: 在OLMoE-1B-7B 4-bit KV上RMF≈0.31（发现0.31 [0.20,0.41]；过程复制均值0.313±0.020；预注册重执行0.231）。路由器margin检测翻转AUC为0.772，但对翻转的损/益符号判定在机会水平。符号翻转税和符号不可分离性跨模型成立；clean-reference修复的收益受架构调节；归一化约定是损伤幅度调节器而非路由可恢复性机制。真实int4内核结果与模拟剂量曲线兼容但统计功效不足（95% CI [-0.111, 0.394]包含零）。预注册的held-out读出复现了分区和近抵消税，但严格不可行排除以微弱差距未通过。

Conclusion: MoE量化退化中约三分之一通过路由翻转介导，这一比例不可忽视；然而，基于当前可观测的本地路由统计量无法在机会水平以上预测翻转的损益符号，因此在仅依赖该特征族时存在"有益检测障碍"，限制了选择性修复策略。该结论跨架构成立，但clean-reference修复效果受架构调节，表明需要更丰富的路由信息或新的修复机制来克服这一障碍。

Abstract: Top-k Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routing is discontinuous, so a deployment-motivated numerical disturbance -- simulated 4-bit KV-cache quantization read by a protected BF16 gate -- pushes tokens across decision boundaries and flips which experts fire. This paper proposes no new mitigation; it supplies a causal apparatus, empirical findings, and a detection-limit result. A four-run apparatus prices the route-mediated fraction (RMF) of quantization damage, a token-level attribution decomposes it by mechanism, and pre-registered probes carry the findings across three architectures. On OLMoE-1B-7B at 4-bit KV (pilot), about a third of the damage is routing-mediated: RMF ~ 0.31 (discovery 0.31 [0.20, 0.41]; process-replicated mean 0.313 +/- 0.020; pre-registered re-execution 0.231). The deployable router margin detects that a flip occurred (AUC 0.772) but cannot tell a harmful flip from a helpful one (at chance): among the tested local, inference-observable router statistics we find no predictor of a flip's loss sign above chance -- an empirical benefit-detection barrier bounding selective repair restricted to this feature family. The signed-flip tax and sign-inseparability carry cross-model; the clean-reference remedy's payout is architecture-modulated; a controlled same-checkpoint flag-swap re-scopes the gate's normalization convention to a damage-magnitude moderator, not a route-recoverability mechanism. A real int4 KV kernel yields a fraction compatible with the fake-quant dose curve but underpowered (95% CI [-0.111, 0.394] includes zero) -- ruling out gross disagreement, not an independent replication. Hypotheses, thresholds, and evaluations were pre-registered before measurement, with misses reported; a pre-registered held-out read replicates the partition and the near-cancelling tax out of sample, while the strict impossibility exclusion narrowly misses.

</details>


### [122] [Can Frontier LLMs Match Natively Multimodal Embeddings? A Comparison on Hard-Negative Text-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11343)
*Archan Dutta,Vyanktesh Kanungo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次直接比较了原生多模态嵌入模型（Gemini Embedding 2）与基于LLM的零样本视觉排序方法（GPT-4.1和Claude Sonnet 4.6）在Flickr30k数据集上的多模态检索与分类性能。研究发现LLM基排序器与Gemini Embedding 2表现相当，但在需要低延迟的场景下，预计算嵌入的多模态嵌入模型更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态检索传统上依赖双编码器模型通过对比学习对齐视觉和文本表示。随着Gemini Embedding 2（首个原生的、将文本、图像、视频、音频和文档映射到统一共享空间的多模态嵌入模型）的发布，以及前沿LLM展现出强大的视觉理解能力，研究者希望弄清这两类截然不同的技术路线在多模态检索中的能力对比，填补直接在原生多模态嵌入与LLM基视觉排序之间进行系统比较的研究空白。

Method: 在Flickr30k数据集上进行直接对比实验，一方是Google的Gemini Embedding 2原生多模态嵌入模型，另一方是基于GPT-4.1和Claude Sonnet 4.6的LLM视觉排序方法（作为零样本排序器），同时评估性能表现和延迟特性两方面的差异。

Result: 实验发现GPT-4.1和Claude Sonnet 4.6作为零样本排序器在Flickr30k上的表现与Gemini Embedding 2相当（on par）。但在延迟方面，由于多模态嵌入可以预先计算，Gemini Embedding 2在低延迟应用中更占优势。

Conclusion: 原生多模态嵌入模型与LLM基视觉排序器在多模态检索性能上旗鼓相当，前者的核心优势在于可预计算嵌入以实现低延迟推理，而LLM排序器可能更适合需要更高灵活性和推理能力的场景。选择哪种技术路线取决于具体应用对延迟与推理能力的需求权衡。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval and classification across different types of media, spanning text, images,video and audio, has traditionally relied on dual-encoder models that align visual and textual representations through contrastive learning. The March 2026 release of Gemini Embedding 2, Google's first natively multimodal embedding model to map text, images, video, audio, and documents into a single shared space, raises competition among multimodal retrieval systems. Simultaneously, frontier Large language models (LLMs) have also demonstrated strong visual understanding, raising the question of whether they can serve as effective zero-shot rankers. Our study provides the first direct comparison of native multimodal embeddings against LLM-based visual ranking on Flickr30k. We observe that GPT-4.1 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 perform on par with Gemini Embedding 2. Additionally, once embeddings are precomputed, multimodal embeddings are better suited for low-latency applications.

</details>


### [123] [Poor Man's Agentic Modeling: Simulating Large LLM-Agent Societies on a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11215)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种用低参数代理模型替代LLM智能体进行大规模社会模拟的方法，通过拟合少量廉价查询构建代理体，在笔记本电脑上即可运行任意N规模的社会模拟，并引入交互阶×记忆分类法预测代理误差的N趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟大量LLM智能体组成的社会成本高昂，但这类模拟通常只关心宏观问题（相行为、典型事实、随智能体数量N的标度规律），而非单个智能体的认知细节。作者希望利用统计物理的观察将方法转变为低成本的大规模模拟。

Method: 将每个LLM智能体替换为从数百到数千次廉价查询中拟合的低参数模型，随后在笔记本电脑上以任意N运行整个社会。引入交互阶×记忆（interaction order x memory）分类法，将感知和记忆映射到有效理论，并预测代理误差随N的趋势。方法有效性在模拟运行前由每个智能体的感知决定。

Result: 在忠实复现的LLM宏观经济模拟EconAgent以及另外七个具名LLM模拟上进行了验证，智能体决策克隆自真实的LLM elicitation（主要为DeepSeek），成本仅数美元。预测的误差趋势逐格成立；两个被驳倒的预测（均出现在强饱和响应上，归因于其曲率）也能被无自由参数的理论定量匹配。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了用低参数代理模型替代LLM智能体可以在保持宏观结果可靠性的前提下大幅降低成本，且通过交互阶×记忆分类法能够系统预测代理误差，为LLM社会模拟提供了一种可扩展且可验证的轻量级方案。

Abstract: Simulating societies of many large language model (LLM) agents is expensive, yet the questions asked of such simulations are usually macroscopic: phase behaviour, stylised facts, and scaling with the number of agents $N$, not the cognition of any single agent. We turn a statistical-physics observation into a method: replace each LLM agent by a low-parameter model fitted from a few hundred to a few thousand cheap queries, then run the society at any $N$ on a laptop. Whether this works is decided before the simulation runs, chiefly by what each agent perceives. We introduce an [interaction order x memory] taxonomy that maps perception and memory to an effective theory and a predicted $N$-trend of the surrogate error. We validate it on a faithful reimplementation of the LLM macroeconomy EconAgent and seven further named LLM simulations, with agent decisions cloned from genuine LLM elicitations (primarily DeepSeek) for a few dollars; the predicted error trends hold cell by cell, and the two refuted predictions, both on a strongly saturating response and traced to its curvature, are themselves matched quantitatively by the theory with no free parameters.

</details>


### [124] [AutoWorldModel-Bench: A State-Centric Benchmark for Automated World-Model Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11216)
*Marjan Moodi,Xuankang Zhu,Fernando De Mesentier Silva,Harold Chaput,Mohammad Reza Taesiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoWorldModel-Bench，一个闭环基准测试，用于评估前沿AI编码代理在开放研究（而非工程到规格）任务中的表现——具体为在固定计算预算下自主改进世界模型起点代码。基准覆盖8个游戏环境，统一结构化状态表示，在64次会话中，Codex-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6在63次会话中改进了起点模型，其中91%的获胜编辑是非平凡的科研型修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模是一个尚未定型的领域，架构、训练目标和状态表示之间存在复杂交互，没有单一方法能在所有环境中占优。这使得它成为测试AI编码代理作为自主研究者的理想场所——因为改进方向不会预先给定，不同于当前Agent基准所主导的按规格工程任务。现有基准缺少这种开放式研究型评估场景。

Method: 引入AutoWorldModel-Bench闭环基准：在固定计算预算下，让前沿编码代理自主改进给定的世界模型起点代码。基准包含8个游戏环境，采用统一的结构化状态表示（从每个游戏中提取的ground-truth实体状态，通过共享张量格式输入），从而将动力学建模与感知解耦，实现分钟级迭代循环。

Result: 在64次独立会话中，Codex-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6在63次中成功改进了起点模型；在91%的会话中，获胜编辑属于非平凡的科研型修改——包括新目标函数、新表示方式、新的rollout流程或架构变更——而非超参数调优。

Conclusion: AutoWorldModel-Bench提供了一个评估前沿编码代理在开放式研究而非按规格工程任务中能力的设置，为AI研究者代理的评估提供了新的基准范式。

Abstract: World modeling is an unsettled field: architectures, training objectives, and state representations interact in complex ways, and no single recipe dominates across environments. This makes it an ideal testbed for AI coding agents acting as autonomous researchers--a setting in which the improvement direction is not specified in advance, unlike the engineering-to-spec tasks that dominate current agent benchmarks. We introduce AutoWorldModel-Bench, a closed-loop benchmark in which frontier coding agents autonomously improve a provided world-model starter under a fixed compute budget. The benchmark spans eight game environments under a unified structured-state representation--ground-truth entity state extracted from each game and consumed through a shared tensor format--which isolates dynamics modeling from perception and enables minutes-per-run iteration. Across 64 sessions, Codex-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.6 improve their starter on 63; in 91% of sessions the winning edit is a non-trivial research-style modification--a new objective, representation, rollout procedure, or architectural change--rather than a hyperparameter tweak. Our benchmark offers a setting in which frontier coding agents can be evaluated on open-ended research rather than engineering-to-spec problems.

</details>


### [125] [MaSRead: Content-Addressed Reading of Replicated Latent Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11218)
*Carlos Baquero,Luís Brito,João Resende*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MaSRead 提出了一种在复制型潜在空间中实现选择性读取的方法，通过基于内容的键控标签集路由和硬注意力掩码解码，使后续查询能够从融合的键值缓存片段中可靠读取所需信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立的智能体可以在潜在空间中共享计算状态（以键值缓存片段形式），并通过无冲突复制数据类型合并存储。但合并后的缓存片段会出现相互干扰——位置相邻并不意味着可寻址，导致后续未知查询无法可靠读取融合后的缓存内容。核心痛点在于：共置（colocation）不等于可寻址性（addressability）。

Method: MaSRead 通过两个关键机制实现内容寻址读取：(1) 路由阶段：基于片段词汇派生不透明的键控标签集，通过词法连通性构建图，利用图遍历到达多跳查询所需的片段；(2) 解码阶段：对选中的片段使用硬注意力掩码解码，隐藏其他所有缓存内容，保证每个片段在隔离状态下被读取。

Result: 在链式、流水线、对称、枢纽和自然语言存储等五种场景下，MaSRead 均能隔离恢复已访问的片段；随着无关片段累积仍保持有效；且可迁移到其他模型家族。路由后的物化解码成本取决于片段长度而非存储总规模；端到端总工作量包括存储相关的路由和每个访问片段的一次读取。

Conclusion: MaSRead 证明了当所需片段与查询通过内容相连时，复制型潜在存储可以被后续查询选择性读取。但存在明确局限：词法路由可能错过不连通的证据，答案组合能力仍受限于冻结的阅读器模型。

Abstract: Independent agents that reason in latent space can share computed state as key-value cache fragments rather than text. Merged by a conflict-free replicated data type, these fragments form a store that converges under any delivery order or duplication. Yet a later query, unknown at encode time, cannot reliably read the merged cache: colocated fragments interfere, so colocation is not addressability. MaSRead addresses the read to content. It routes through opaque keyed tag sets derived from fragment words and decodes each selected fragment under a hard attention mask that hides the rest. Under lexical connectivity, a graph walk reaches the fragments required by a multi-hop query. Across chain, pipeline, symmetric, hub, and natural-language stores, MaSRead recovers visited fragments in isolation, remains effective as unrelated fragments accumulate, and transfers to another model family. After routing, materialized decoding depends on fragment length rather than total store size; end-to-end work still includes store-dependent routing and one read per visited fragment. The limits are explicit: lexical routing can miss disconnected evidence, and answer composition remains bounded by the frozen reader. Thus a replicated latent store becomes selectively readable for later queries when the needed fragments connect to the query through content.

</details>


### [126] [AgenticTwin: An Agentic LLM Framework Integrated with Digital Twin for Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11679)
*Touseef Hasan,Mounika Ghanta,Souvika Sarkar,Ujjwal Guin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticTwin，一个将大语言模型驱动的推理与数字孪生异常检测流水线相结合的智能体框架，使操作人员能够通过自然语言查询理解系统异常。同时引入基于合成异常注入真实天气传感器数据的基准评估流水线，实验表明结构化智能体协作和知识接地推理能提升诊断质量、上下文检索和缓解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生被广泛用于监控和模拟网络物理系统，但即使是经验丰富的操作人员，也难以解释数字孪生流水线中检测到的异常，因为原始传感器数据复杂度高、体量大。大语言模型虽展现出推理和解释能力，但其与数字孪生驱动的异常分析的集成仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出AgenticTwin智能体框架：1) 将LLM驱动的推理与数字孪生驱动的异常检测流水线集成；2) 框架将LLM生成的解释基于数字孪生异常分类器的输出进行接地，使操作人员能够用自然语言提问；3) 引入基于真实天气传感器数据集注入合成异常的基准评估流水线，支持对异常事件进行受控的操作人员查询生成；4) 评估在现实网络物理环境中部署轻量级开源LLM的可行性。

Result: 实验结果表明，结构化智能体协作和知识接地推理在多种异常场景下显著提升了诊断质量、上下文检索能力和缓解质量。

Conclusion: AgenticTwin验证了LLM智能体框架在数字孪生异常分析中的有效性，证明结构化协作和接地推理能显著改善诊断和缓解性能，并表明轻量级开源LLM可实际部署于网络物理环境。

Abstract: Digital twins are increasingly used to monitor and simulate the behavior of cyber-physical systems. Even with skilled operators, interpreting anomalies detected within digital twin pipelines is challenging, as the sheer complexity and volume of raw sensor data make thorough analysis difficult. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) offer promising capabilities for reasoning and explanation, yet their integration into digital twin-driven anomaly analysis remains underexplored. In this work, we propose AgenticTwin, an agentic framework that integrates LLM-driven reasoning with a digital twin-based anomaly detection pipeline. The framework grounds LLM-generated explanations in outputs from a digital twin-driven anomaly classifier and enables human operators to ask relevant natural-language questions about the system. Beyond the framework itself, we introduce a benchmark-oriented evaluation pipeline constructed over synthetic anomalies injected into a real-world weather sensor dataset, enabling controlled generation of operator queries over anomaly events. We further evaluate the feasibility of deploying lightweight, open-source LLMs for practical cyber-physical environments. Experimental results demonstrate that structured agent collaboration and knowledge-grounded reasoning improve diagnosis quality, contextual retrieval, and mitigation quality across diverse possible anomaly scenarios.

</details>


### [127] [From Monolithic to Modular: Segment-level Automatic Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11219)
*Nikita Kulin,Viktor Zhuravlev,Artur Khairullin,Sergey Muravyov,Ilya Makarov,Daniil Sukhorukov,Ekaterina Averkova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAPO，一种分段级别（segment-level）的自动提示优化方法，将提示分解为角色、上下文、任务和输出格式四个部分，逐段进行定向改进，在多个基准数据集上优于现有APO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自动提示优化（APO）方法往往对提示进行整体（monolithic）重写，这种做法可能导致优化一个行为的同时损害其他行为，缺乏对提示内部结构的细粒度控制和针对性改进。

Method: SAPO将提示分解为角色（role）、上下文（context）、任务（task）和输出格式（output format）四个段，利用top-5和bottom-5示例进行定向改进。优化循环使用单一LLM配合静态元提示和结构化输出完成分段、弱点分析和候选生成。采用两阶段生成流程：(1)分段级别诊断和推荐提取；(2)受强弱段信号约束的候选合成。同时设计了训练/验证协议。

Result: 在SQuADv2、TweetEval、XSUM、CommonGen和GSM8K五个基准上，使用GPT-3.5-Turbo和GPT-4o-mini两种模型，SAPO取得了最佳平均得分，超越了Zero-shot基线以及APE、OPRO、EvoPrompt、GEPA和StraGO等强APO基线。

Conclusion: 分段级别的提示优化比整体重写更有效，通过结构化分解和定向改进能够在保持其他行为表现的同时精准提升目标性能，为自动提示优化提供了更细粒度的新思路。

Abstract: Automatic Prompt Optimization (APO) often rewrites prompts monolithically, which can improve one behavior while degrading others. We present SAPO, a segment-level APO method that decomposes prompts into role, context, tasks, and output format, then applies targeted improvements based on top-5 and bottom-5 examples. The optimization loop uses one LLM with static meta-prompts and structured outputs for segmentation, weakness analysis, and candidate generation. We describe a train/validation protocol and a two-stage generation process: (1) segment-level diagnosis and recommendation extraction, (2) candidate synthesis constrained by weak/strong segment signals. Using the evaluation setup across SQuADv2, TweetEval, XSUM, CommonGen, and GSM8K on GPT-3.5-Turbo and GPT-4o-mini, SAPO achieves the best average score against Zero-shot and strong APO baselines including APE, OPRO, EvoPrompt, GEPA, and StraGO.

</details>


### [128] [LLMs in Process Diagram Engineering: From Optimal PFDs to Validated P&IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11220)
*Timur Zakarin,Sergei Voitov,Sergei Shumilin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出P&ID Pilot，一个端到端AI流程，将GA/LLM混合方法用于PFD合成，再借助LLM智能代理将PFD转换为P&ID，两个阶段均实现领域合规和高执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PFD的创建及其向P&ID的转化主要依赖人工完成，耗时且成本高。引入AI有望实现流程自动化、节省时间并通过探索多种拓扑减少人工劳动获得经济效益。

Method: 采用两阶段端到端管道：第一阶段比较四种方法后，采用遗传算法(GA)与大型语言模型(LLM)的混合方法进行PFD拓扑合成；第二阶段使用基于LLM的智能代理，通过受限工程软件开发工具包生成经验证的可执行修改，将PFD转换为P&ID。

Result: GA/LLM混合方法在所有方法中获得最低损失值，生成有效PFD拓扑且满足出口流参数、无工程规则违反；LLM代理实现100%执行成功率，输出符合领域规则和参考图结构的源接地P&ID。

Conclusion: 将GA/LLM驱动的合成与LLM转化代理结合，为端到端过程设计自动化提供了可行路径，能生成经验证的可部署输出，显著减少人工工程工作。

Abstract: Nowadays, the creation of a process flow diagram (PFD) and its subsequent transformation into a piping and instrumentation diagram (P&ID) is predominantly performed manually. Applying artificial intelligence in the task could potentially lead not only to process automation and time savings, but also to financial gains by exploring numerous diagram's topology options and reducing manual labor. This research presents P&ID Pilot - a practical end-to-end AI pipeline capable of handling flowsheet developing for both stages. The first stage focuses on PFD synthesis, whereas the second is directed toward modifying the generated PFD into P&ID. After comparing four different methods, the hybrid approach combining genetic algorithms (GA) and large language models (LLM) is shown to generate the optimal valid PFD topology, achieving the lowest loss value among all the methods, while satisfying the required outlet flow parameters without engineering-rule violations. For the second stage, the proposed LLM-based agent successfully transforms the generated PFD into a source-grounded P&ID by producing validated, executable modifications through a restricted engineering software development kit, achieving 100% execution success while maintaining compliance with domain-specific rules and reference graph structures. This unified pipeline - coupling GA/LLM-driven synthesis with an LLM-based transformation agent - offers a feasible path toward end-to-end process design automation by producing validated, deployable outputs and substantially reduces manual engineering effort.

</details>


### [129] [A Conceptual Framework for Refining Influence Knowledge from Simulation Evidence in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11221)
*Barbara da Silva Oliveira,Julien Deantoni,Nicolas Ferry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个利用"影响（Influences）"新概念的概念框架，以支持仿真战役的迭代和增量式精化，从而加深对信息物理系统（CPS）行为的理解，并通过Simulink/Gazebo协同仿真的移动机器人案例研究验证了该方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息物理系统由多方利益相关者开发，各领域工件交互产生系统行为。仿真/协同仿真虽已成为分析CPS行为的关键手段，但部分环境中介交互（超出直接感知与执行范畴）由于复杂性、时间或领域经验缺乏而未被建模，导致仿真结果难以被正确理解和利用。

Method: 提出一个概念框架，引入"影响（Influences）"这一新概念，支持仿真战役的迭代与增量式精化，系统性地探索和理解系统中未建模的环境中介交互，从而逐步加深对系统行为的理解。

Result: 通过一个使用Simulink/Gazebo协同仿真实现的移动机器人案例研究，展示了所提出概念框架的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于影响概念的框架能够有效弥补CPS仿真中未建模环境交互的不足，支持开发者通过迭代精化仿真战役来更好地理解和利用仿真结果，为复杂CPS行为分析提供了系统性方法支持。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems (CPS) are typically developed by multiple stakeholders who produce artefacts tailored to their specific domains of expertise. The behaviour of these systems emerges from the interaction between those artefacts and their operational environment. Simulation and co-simulation have become essential approaches for analysing CPS behaviour and, through simulation campaigns, developers can explore system responses under changing conditions, including interactions with the environment. However, the lack of details and understanding of some environmentmediated interactions (typically the ones beyond direct sensing and actuation), which remain unmodelled due to their complexity, a lack of time, or a lack of domain experience, hinders the proper comprehension and exploitation of simulation results. To address these limitations, we propose a conceptual framework leveraging the novel concept of Influences to support the iterative and incremental refinement of simulation campaigns and deepen the understanding of the system behaviour. We demonstrate the proposed approach through a case study involving a mobile robot implemented using Simulink/Gazebo co-simulation.

</details>


### [130] [Harnessing agent memory to build lifelong AI partners for materials scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11224)
*Siyu Liu,Bo Hu,Beilin Ye,He Cao,David J. Srolovitz,Tongqi Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以持久化记忆为核心的终身AI伙伴框架，用于材料科学研究。该框架通过自进化记忆存储可检查的事实和可执行的技能，实现了跨模型迁移的科研经验积累。在三个计算场景中的评估显示，该框架显著提升了任务成功率、减少了重复错误并降低了工具调用开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料研究的进步依赖于累积的经验——有效的脚本、可信的协议、失败计算的警示以及将新问题联系到旧结果的判断力。然而这些经验通常分散在笔记本、代码库、作业日志和个体记忆中，难以实现可复现性和知识迁移，且几乎无法在AI智能体之间移植。因此需要一个以持久记忆为根基的AI伙伴设计范式。

Method: 提出自进化记忆框架，将科学经验存储为两类可操作的结构：(1) 可检查的事实（observations, failure boundaries, validation checks）和(2) 可执行的技能（protocols）。这些记忆可以跨模型检索、修订和迁移，无需更新模型参数。框架围绕持久记忆而非特定智能体实现进行设计，使记忆成为独立于模型栈的资产。

Result: 三个计算场景的评估结果：(1) 在49个真实材料工具使用问题（138个可执行子任务）中，记忆使GPT-5.2任务成功率几乎翻倍，且无需模型参数更新；(2) 在元素固态物态方程计算中，记忆将波函数初始化失败转化为执行前护栏，结果从22/1/4提升到25/2/0（正确/部分/错误），避免了92%的重复错误；(3) 在13个实际材料模拟工作流中，记忆技能和失败事实使聚合轨迹负担（token数）减半，工具调用量在第三轮减少超过两倍，同时保持带隙、声子、空位和功函数分析中的物理有意义输出。

Conclusion: 智能体记忆可以成为持久性的科学资产——一种可移植、自改进的材料研究经验记录，超越任何单一模型或智能体堆栈的寿命。这为材料科学的长期AI伙伴提供了新的设计方向，强调记忆基础设施而非特定模型实现的价值。

Abstract: Materials research advances through accumulated experience - scripts that work, protocols that are trusted, warnings attached to failed calculations or experiments, and judgement that links a new question to an old result. This experience is essential for reproducibility and knowledge transfer, yet it is usually fragmented across notebooks, repositories, job logs and individual memory, and it is rarely portable across artificial-intelligence agents. Here we argue that a lifelong AI partner for materials science can be designed around persistent memory rather than around a particular agent implementation. We introduce a self-evolving memory framework that stores scientific experience as inspectable facts and executable skills, so that observations, failure boundaries, protocols and validation checks can be retrieved, revised and migrated across models. We evaluate the idea in three computational settings that expose different layers of materials-research competence. In 49 real-world materials-tool-use questions comprising 138 executable subtasks, memory nearly doubles GPT-5.2 task success without model-parameter updates. In elemental-solid equation-of-state calculations, memory converts a wavefunction-initialization failure into a pre-execution guardrail, improving outcomes from 22/1/4 to 25/2/0 Correct/Partial/Error and avoiding 92% of repeated errors. In 13 practical material simulation workflows, remembered skills and failure facts halve the aggregate trace burden (tokens) and reduce tool calls by over a factor of two by the third round, while preserving physically meaningful outputs in band-gap, phonon, vacancy and work-function analyses. These results show that agent memory can serve as a durable scientific asset; a portable, self-improving record of materials-research experience that outlives any single model or agent stack.

</details>


### [131] [Identity from the Outside: A Conceptual Framework and Research Program for AI Personality Clones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11225)
*Luc E. Brunet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对AI"人格克隆"提出了一种操作化的个人同一性分析框架，将"同一性"解构为三个被混淆的标准（目标保真度、类人普遍性、个体性），并提出六项因子分解和状态空间公式来量化可观察的身份。核心主张是一个条件性猜想：给定智能体对其自身持续存在的信息及自身利害后果的假设，版本化（versionability）倾向于降低长期不可区分性。文章还区分了产品克隆、个体与"委托者"（delegate）三类对象，最终提出长期标准应为"气候保真"（climate fidelity）而非轨迹保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI人格克隆技术的出现迫使研究者重新审视"个人同一性"这一哲学问题。传统哲学讨论多聚焦于意识的困难问题，难以在操作层面落地。本文试图搁置意识难题，从"同一性即观察者在时长内对表现的不可区分性"这一操作化视角出发，为克隆人格的质量评估提供可量化的理论框架，并澄清"克隆身份"这一概念中混杂的多重含义。

Method: 论文提出：(1) 将"同一性"拆解为目标保真、类人普遍性和个体性三个准则；(2) 提出六项因子分解——基底（substrate）、倾向（dispositions）、记忆（memory）、更新动力学（update dynamics）、语境（context）与外部偶然性（exogenous contingencies），并以状态空间形式化；(3) 将不可区分性定义为"1减去评判者的区分优势"，因子系数通过随机消融实验（randomized ablation）估计为局部灵敏度；(4) 借助lambda演算、线性类型论和互模拟（bisimulation）类比阐明线性性所能及不能建立的结论；(5) 提出介于"产品克隆"与"个体"之间的第三类对象——"委托者"（delegate），即任务受限、寿命有限的局部克隆，最终以带宽受限的遗嘱终结；(6) 将经验文献映射到三个准则，并提出实验方案。

Result: 文章的核心结果是提出一个条件性猜想：若对智能体关于自身持续存在的信息假设和对涉及其自身利害后果的假设成立，则版本化倾向会降低长期不可区分性。此外，论文论证了正确的长期判据不是轨迹保真（trajectory fidelity），而是气候保真（climate fidelity）——即匹配一个人可能响应的条件分布。理想的克隆应当是"像原初者本会偏离自身那样偏离原初者"。

Conclusion: 结论是：评估AI人格克隆不应追求逐条轨迹的一致（轨迹保真），而应追求在条件分布层面与原初人格保持一致（气候保真）。"最佳克隆"不是与原始个体无法区分的复制品，而是以原初者自身可能演变的方式发生分歧的存在。这一框架为后续实证研究和克隆人格的伦理、评估标准提供了理论基础。

Abstract: AI "personality clones" force a re-examination of personal identity in operational terms. Setting aside the hard problem of consciousness, we approach identity through the indiscernibility of manifestations, as assessed by an observer over a duration. We distinguish three criteria that "identity" conflates: fidelity to a target person, generic human-likeness, and individuality. We propose a six-term factorization of observed identity (substrate, dispositions, memory, update dynamics, context, exogenous contingencies), with a state-space formulation. Indiscernibility is defined as one minus a judge's distinguishing advantage, and the factorization's coefficients become local sensitivities estimable by randomized ablation. The central claim is a conditional conjecture: given hypotheses about the agent's information on its own persistence and about consequences bearing on its own stakes, versionability tends to degrade long-horizon indiscernibility. An analogy with lambda-calculus, linear typing, and bisimulation clarifies what linearity does and does not establish. Between product-clone and individual we identify a third object, the delegate: a task-limited, bounded-lifespan partial clone ending in a bandwidth-limited testament. We map the empirical literature onto the three criteria, propose an experimental program, and argue that the correct long-horizon criterion is not trajectory fidelity but climate fidelity: matching the conditional distribution of a person's possible responses. The best clone is the one that diverges from the original as the original would have diverged from itself.

</details>


### [132] [Cutting AI Datacenter Energy with Reinforcement Learning: Measured Power Control of LLM Training from One GPU to the Fleet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11226)
*Eliseo Curcio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次刻画了强化学习（GRPO）后训练在GPU上的功耗行为，并提出一个PPO元控制器，通过自适应调整工作负载的生成参数来控制功耗。实验显示，在7B规模下将功率限制违规减少89.8%，同时算力产出提升18.1%、能效提升26.2%；在72B规模下由于模型分片导致群组大小执行器失效，重建后的控制器在真实部署中实现35.7%的产出提升和87.2%更少的违规。作者还发现功率瞬态在30秒~5分钟的时间窗口下大幅衰减，并论证了约两倍的名牌功率超订是可行的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型开发高度依赖强化学习后训练，但其在GPU硬件上的功耗行为从未被系统刻画。数据中心目前用静态功率上限和反应式节流等与工作负载无关的机制管理GPU电源，会不加区分地拖慢硬件。作者希望利用RL训练工作负载自身的灵活性，实现更加智能的功耗管理。

Method: 作者在7B、14B、72B规模（1至4张A100）上以半秒级功率遥测对GRPO训练进行300,000+样本的仪器化测量，并训练一个PPO元控制器，使其通过自适应调整工作负载的生成参数（如群组大小和生成并发度）来响应实测功率。文章还进行了执行器权威性测试，对比群组大小与生成并发度两种执行器的功率控制能力，并在真实72B工作负载上进行了三次复现部署验证。

Result: 7B全轨迹测试中，控制器将功率限制违规降低89.8%，标记输出提升18.1%，能效提升26.2%。72B部署中群组大小执行器在模型分片下失效（零结果），但基于并发度执行器的重建控制器在72B实际预热负载上实现35.7%的产出提升、2.27±1.08%的预算违规率（比无控制减少87.2%违规），且吞吐和能量效率最优。测量窗口从0.5秒到30秒到5分钟时，72B瞬态分别从23.6%降至1.6%和0%；16-GPU组合训练队列在30秒以上窗口实现零违规，峰值需求仅占铭牌功率的50-56%。

Conclusion: 基于工作负载感知的RL功耗控制明显优于静态上限和反应式节流，且对于该GPU混合集群，约两倍的名牌功率超订是可行的（需运营商验证）。作者量化了经济和碳排放影响，并给出了低成本运营商试点方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning post-training dominates modern language-model development, yet its power behavior on GPU hardware has not been characterized, and datacenters manage GPU power with workload-blind mechanisms, static caps and reactive throttling, that slow hardware indiscriminately. We instrument GRPO training with half-second power telemetry at 7B, 14B, and 72B scales on one to four A100s (380,000+ samples), and train a PPO meta-controller that adapts the workload's own generation parameters to measured power. Against the full 500-step 7B trace, the controller cuts power-limit violations by 89.8% while increasing token output by 18.1% and energy efficiency by 26.2% (tokens per MWh). Deployed live at 72B, the same controller family yields replicated null results, diagnosed as the group-size actuator losing authority under model sharding. An actuator-authority sweep shows the same parameters applied as generation concurrency retain 17-22% power authority, isolating an occupancy-versus-volume principle; a controller rebuilt on that actuator controls a live 72B rollout-generation workload across three replications: 35.7% more output than a static safe baseline at 2.27 +/- 1.08% budget violations, 87.2% fewer violations than uncontrolled operation, and the best mean throughput and energy per token among constrained controllers, with an adaptive threshold rule matching it in one of three operating conditions. Under realistic measurement windows the original 72B transients fall from 23.6% at half-second resolution to 1.6% at 30 s and zero at 5 min; a composed 16-GPU fleet shows zero violations at 30 s and longer, with peak demand at 50-56% of nameplate. For this fleet mix, roughly twofold oversubscription of nameplate appears feasible, subject to operator validation. We quantify the economic and carbon consequences and specify a low-cost operator pilot.

</details>


### [133] [Forecasting Side Effects of Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11227)
*Chong Yong Ong,Alson Wei Jie Sim,Peixin Zhang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了激活引导（activation steering）技术在语言模型中产生的副作用能否被预先预测。通过在三个开源语言模型上构建覆盖67种行为的交叉效应矩阵，发现副作用普遍、结构化且常不对称，无法用现有相似性启发式解释，但可以在引导前被准确预测：副作用幅度主要取决于目标行为，方向可从未引导的表示中以高于基线的准确率预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导虽能有效修改模型行为且无需重训练，但常产生意想不到的副作用，影响安全部署。因此，作者希望探究能否在应用引导之前预测这些副作用，以实现主动安全审计。

Method: 在三个开源权重语言模型上，构造了覆盖67种行为的分类体系，构建跨效应矩阵（cross-effect matrix），系统分析副作用的存在性、结构性与不对称性；利用模型未引导的隐藏表示来预测副作用方向，并将其与简单的基线方法进行比较。

Result: 副作用在激活引导中普遍存在、具有结构化模式且常呈现不对称性，无法由现有基于相似性的启发式方法解释；副作用幅度主要由目标行为决定，而方向可从未引导的模型表示中高准确率预测，显著优于简单基线。

Conclusion: 激活引导具有系统性且可预测的副作用，这为主动安全审计和更明智地部署引导干预提供了基础，有助于提升该技术的安全性和可控性。

Abstract: Activation steering modifies a language model by adding a learned direction to its hidden activations, enabling targeted behavioral changes without retraining. While effective, steering often produces unintended side effects on other behaviors, making it difficult to deploy safely. We therefore ask: can these side effects be forecasted before steering is applied? We answer this question by constructing a cross-effect matrix over a taxonomy of 67 behaviors across three open-weight language models. We find that side effects are common, structured, and often asymmetric, revealing interactions that cannot be explained by existing similarity-based heuristics. Despite this complexity, we show that side effects are largely predictable before steering is performed. Their magnitude depends primarily on the target behavior, while their direction can be forecasted from the model's unsteered representations with substantially higher accuracy than simple baselines. Our results demonstrate that activation steering has systematic and forecastable side effects, enabling proactive safety auditing and more informed deployment of steering interventions.

</details>


### [134] [Synchronizing Beliefs with Second-Order Theory-of-Mind in Human-Autonomy Teams (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11229)
*Jack Mirenzi,Henny Admoni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视偏好学习中的教学范式，提出将偏好学习视为人机联合团队问题：教师维护对学习者的认知模型来设计高效课程，学习者则维护对教师模型的二阶模型，并通过结构化"理解陈述"（understanding statements）保持教师模型同步。仿真表明，知情教师的主动教学优于学习者主导的查询选择，教师模型漂移会削弱优势，而理解陈述可修复此问题，其中二阶（ToM-2）陈述在教师误差有方向性集中时更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的偏好学习范式将人类教师视为被动的"神谕"（oracle），只能回答学习者生成的查询。这忽视了教师最重要的优势——对目标（reward）本身的知识。如果教师知晓目标，就能比任何学习者驱动的采集策略更高效地构造训练示例，且该优势随奖励特征维度增长而扩大。然而利用这一优势需要准确建模学习者当前的知识状态，因此需要一个耦合双方行为模型的新框架。

Method: 本文将偏好学习重铸为人机联合团队（human-autonomy team）问题，耦合两个行为模型：（1）教师维护对学习者的认知模型，据此设计信息量丰富的课程（informative curriculum）；（2）学习者维护对教师模型的二阶模型，并输出结构化的偏好约束（即"理解陈述"），用于保持教师对学习者模型的同步。通过仿真验证不同设置下的性能差异。

Result: 仿真结果表明：（1）知情教师（informed teacher）的主动教学优于学习者主导的查询选择；（2）交替教师导致的教师模型漂移会削弱这一优势；（3）理解陈述能够修复模型漂移带来的性能损失；（4）当教师对学习者的误差集中在特定方向而非均匀分布时，二阶（ToM-2）理解陈述优于均值信念（mean-belief）陈述。

Conclusion: 偏好学习不应将教师视为被动查询应答者，而应认识到教师在目标知识上的优势。通过搭建教师-学习者互相建模的联合团队框架，并引入理解陈述作为双向通信机制，可以显著提升偏好学习的样本效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Comparative feedback, asking people which of two behaviors they prefer, has become a standard way to align robot and agent behavior with human intent when the reward itself cannot be specified directly. Preference-based reward learning typically casts the human teacher as a passive oracle answering learner-generated queries. We argue this forfeits the teacher's defining advantage: knowledge of the objective. A teacher who knows the target can construct training examples more efficiently than any learner-driven acquisition strategy, an advantage that widens as the reward's feature dimension grows. However, exploiting this advantage requires an accurate model of what the learner currently knows. We therefore recast preference learning as a human-autonomy team problem coupling two behavioral models: the teacher maintains a model of the learner to design an informative curriculum, and the learner maintains a second-order model of the teacher's model, emitting structured preference constraints (understanding statements) that keep the teacher's model of the learner synchronized. In simulation, an informed teacher outperforms learner-led selection; teacher-model drift under alternating teachers erodes this advantage; and understanding statements repair it, with second-order (ToM-2) statements outperforming mean-belief statements when the teacher's error about the learner is concentrated in a particular direction rather than spread evenly.

</details>


### [135] [The Edge-based Contiguous p-median Problem with Connections to Logistics Districting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11230)
*Zeyad Kassem,Adolfo R. Escobedo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了边基连续p-median（ECpM）问题，用于将道路网络划分为指定数量的紧凑且连续的辖区。文章构建了两种二值规划模型：一种是基于割集约束的B&C算法，另一种是基于多项式最短路径约束（SPC）的模型。实验表明，SPC模型在计算速度上比割集B&C方法快最多17倍，且其约束被证明是EpM模型的超有效不等式。此外，SPC约束还被推广到边基区划（EBD）问题中，表现优于现有割集模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道路网络划分问题往往需要大量计算资源来处理连续性约束，且已有模型在大规模网络上的可行性较差（如EBD模型中割集方法12小时无法求解）。因此需要更高效的建模方法和求解策略来实现大规模道路网络的紧凑连续辖区划分。

Method: 提出两种二值整数规划模型：(1) ECpM-割集模型，采用指数级割集约束并通过分支切割（B&C）分离方案求解；(2) ECpM-SPC模型，采用多项式数量的最短路径约束来建模连续性，可用现成的求解器通过标准分支定界求解。两种模型均基于网络距离。SPC约束还被证明是EpM模型的超有效不等式，并进一步扩展到EBD问题中。

Result: 在超过2700个节点、近3400条边的道路网络以及在超过960万个二值变量的模型上进行测试，SPC模型的求解速度比割集B&C方法快最多17倍。对于EBD问题，现有割集模型在12小时内无法找到任何可行解，而SPC模型能在大多数实例上求解到最优。

Conclusion: SPC约束是建模道路网络辖区连续性的高效方法，不仅在计算效率上大幅优于传统割集方法，而且其在理论上具有特殊性质（超有效不等式），为EcPm及EBD等边基划分问题提供了强有力的建模与求解工具。

Abstract: This paper introduces the edge-based contiguous p-median (ECpM) problem to partition the roads in a network into a given number of compact and contiguous territories. Two binary programming models are introduced, both of which incorporate a network distance. The first model requires an exponential number of cut set-based constraints to model contiguity; it is paired with a separation scheme that usually generates only a small number of these constraints, namely, a branch-and-cut (B&C) algorithm. The second model utilizes a polynomial number of shortest-path constraints to model contiguity and can be solved with off-the-shelf solvers. The respective solution approaches are tested on road networks with over 2,700 nodes and close to 3,400 edges, yielding models with over 9.6 million binary variables. Solving the model based on shortest path contiguity (SPC) constraints via standard branch and bound attains speedups in computational time of up to 17x relative to the cut set-based B&C implementation. In addition, the SPC constraints are demonstrated to be supervalid inequalities of the edge-based p-median (EpM) model (i.e., for which contiguity is not explicitly required), meaning that they may cut off integer-feasible solutions and some, but not all, of the optimal solutions of this simpler problem. Finally, the paper explores structural insights and connections between ECpM and the edge-based districting (EBD) problem, which enforces an additional work balance criterion. An existing model that utilizes cut set-based contiguity constraints was unable to find a feasible solution within 12 hours for any of the tested instances, while an SPC-based EBD model was able to solve most of these to optimality.

</details>


### [136] [LinearKV: One Cached State Suffices for Position-Independent Caching in Hybrid LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11231)
*Yirui Liu,Ruoling Qi,Longwen Wang,Xuaner Wu,Jian Chen,Yuxin Jin,Jiawei Shao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LinearKV 提出一种无需训练的混合架构大语言模型位置无关缓存（PIC）框架，核心发现是只需一个被缓存的线性层状态作为初始化器即可，无需像并发工作 HYPIC 那样将所有缓存状态精确组合成全前缀状态。在三个混合模型和三个 PIC 选择器上的实验表明，两种方法在 GDN 模型上表现相当（最高恢复92%质量），但在 Mamba-2 模型上精确组合反而崩溃（仅46.6% vs 单状态初始化的86.8%），且单状态初始化更廉价，将TTFT降至全预填充的0.46倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位置无关缓存方法（PIC）均为全注意力模型设计，依赖token索引的KV缓存进行token块匹配、KV拼接和局部重计算。混合LLM用线性循环替代大多数注意力层，仅暴露固定大小的状态，没有可拼接或局部修复的token级KV。因此需要回答：PIC能否惠及混合模型，以及需要什么条件。

Method: LinearKV提出解耦初始化（decoupled initialization）：每个线性层将K个匹配的局部状态映射为单个初始状态，而全注意力层照常拼接KV。该方法与现有PIC方法完全兼容，复用其token选择和重计算逻辑不变。核心洞察是单个缓存状态足以作为线性层的初始化器，而非需要对所有K个缓存状态做精确的代数组合。
